Healthy

1 Ingredient Low Carb Alfredo Pasta

Serves: 4 servings

This is the ultimate low carb pasta hack! You save half the calories and if you want to add more protein to it, you can add some grilled chicken and you’re set!

Ingredients

  • Noodles:
  • ½ head of Napa cabbage, sliced into strips
  • 1 Homemade Alfredo Sauce
  • ½ teaspoon garlic powder
  • ½ cup heavy cream
  • 1 tbsp of butter
  • ¼ cup Parmesan cheese
  • Salt and pepper
  • ½ cup almond milk

Directions

  1. In a small pot, make your homemade alfredo sauce. Add in your heavy cream, butter, seasonings and parmesan cheese. Whisk until combined and until thickened.
  2. Slice your cabbage and saute slightly (about 3-4 mins).
  3. Assemble your dish by adding your homemade alfredo sauce and topping with additional parm.

Nutrition (per serving)

Calories: 175 · Fat: 16 · Carbs: 5 · Protein: 4

Recipe from: lowcarblove.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

Dessert

1234 Cakes

Serves: 1 servings

The proportion of 1 cup butter, 2 cups sugar, 3 cups flour, and 4 eggs (all these are main ingredients) lead to the name of 1234 Cakes!

Ingredients

  • 1 Cup of unsalted butter softened
  • 2 cups of caster sugar
  • 3 cups of self-raising flour
  • 4 medium eggs
  • 1 pinch of salt
  • 4 tablespoons milk
  • Ice-cream scoop, optional
  • 1 2*12 hole muffin tins, lined with paper cases

Directions

  1. Set the oven to 190C.
  2. Shift the butter into a bowl and whisk it until softened. Add the sugar, flour, salt, eggs and milk and flutter until the mixture is smooth.
  3. Use a traditional-style ice-cream scoop, or spoon, to separate the mixture between all the paper covers.
  4. Place the muffin tins in the oven and bake for 15 minutes, then swap over the position of the tins over and bake for another 3-7 minutes. This time till both trays of cupcakes is a light golden colour.
  5. Remove the tins from the oven. Leave the cupcakes to cool in the tins for a few minutes, then transfer them to a wire rack to cool down.

Notes

@little_mrs_w Thank you , finally a straight answer instead of "flour Algebra" I know very little about baking, reading through these comments trying to understand. Thank you for your comment.

Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

Asian Soup Healthy

5-Min Egg Drop Soup Recipe: Better Than Takeout

Serves: 4 servings · Cook: 5 min

Egg Drop Soup is one of the easiest and most comforting soups you can make at home. I’ve been making it since I was a kid, and to this day, everyone finishes every last drop! It’s savory, cozy, and ready in just 5 minutes—faster and better than takeout.

Ingredients

  • 6 cups chicken stock
  • ½ teaspoon sea salt
  • 3 tablespoons cornstarch
  • 9 tablespoons water
  • 5 large eggs
  • Pinch of white pepper
  • Dash of sesame oil
  • 2 tablespoons scallions, chopped

Directions

  1. Prepare the eggs:
  2. Crack the eggs into a bowl and whisk until well combined.
  3. Make the cornstarch slurry:
  4. In a separate bowl, mix the cornstarch with water. Whisk until smooth.
  5. Cook the soup:
  6. In a pot over high heat, pour in the chicken stock and add the salt. Stir and bring to a simmer.
  7. Stir in the cornstarch slurry and continue stirring until the soup thickens and reaches a gentle boil.
  8. In a slow, circular motion, pour the beaten eggs into the soup. Let them set briefly, then gently stir.
  9. Add white pepper, sesame oil, and scallions. Serve hot!

Notes

Stock matters: A flavorful chicken stock makes a huge difference. Use homemade or a good quality store-bought version. Cornstarch: It helps thicken the soup and gives it a silky texture. It can be replaced with sweet potato starch or arrowroot starch. Egg ribbons: For thick egg ribbons, wait a moment before stirring. For thin ribbons, stir as you pour. Adjust seasoning: Taste before serving and adjust salt or pepper to your liking. Add-ins: Feel free to add corn, cabbage, carrots, or tofu to make it your own version!

Recipe from: cicili.tv, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

Meat Middle Eastern

@SHREDHAPPENS MIDDLE EASTERN CHUCK ROAST

Serves: 1 servings · Prep: 5 min

This takes just 5 minutes of prep and seriously one of the best ways to have protein on hand for the entire week. It uses just 1 oven-safe dish and it goes in the oven low and slow.

Ingredients

  • 2 ½ lb to 3 lb chuck roast
  • 1 salt & pepper
  • 1 tbsp sumac
  • 2 tsp Aleppo pepper
  • 1 ¼ tsp basil
  • ½ tsp marjoram
  • 1 tsp oregano
  • ½ tsp turmeric
  • 2 tsp onion powder
  • 7 garlic cloves, 6-7
  • 24 oz bone broth
  • 1 cinnamon stick
  • 1 thick greek yogurt, or labneh!
  • 1 red onion, thinly sliced
  • 4 tbsp finely chopped fresh parsley
  • 2 tbsp sumac, 1-2
  • small pinch of salt
  • 1 large lemon, juice
  • fresh herbs, for garnish

Directions

  1. Preheat your oven to 350F.
  2. Grab an oven-safe dish with a lid, or you can use foil to cover.
  3. Pat a 2.5lb to 3lb chuck roast dry with a paper towel to remove the excess moisture. Season both sides with a few generous pinches of salt & pepper. Then rub in your spices. I used 1 tbsp sumac, 2 tsp Aleppo pepper, 1 1/4 tsp basil, 1/2 tsp marjoram, 1 tsp oregano, 1/2 tsp turmeric, 2 tsp onion powder. Rub this equally on both sides.
  4. Add 6-7 garlic cloves to the pot, pour in 24oz bone broth, and a cinnamon stick. The cinnamon is going to make things smell and taste SO good.
  5. Pop it in the oven with the lid on. You’ll want to put it in the oven for 1 hour for each pound, plus an extra hour. So for a 2.5lb chuck roast, I would have it in the oven for 3.5 hours, for a 3lb roast, 4 hours, etc.
  6. After the 3.5 hours, remove, shred the beef using two forks, and put back in the oven uncovered for 15-30 minutes at 400F.
  7. I love serving this with thick greek yogurt (or labneh!) and express pickled red onions. To make red onions, thinly slice 1 red onion and add it to a bowl with 4 tbsp finely chopped fresh parsley, 1 to 2 tbsp sumac, a small pinch of salt, and the juice of 1 large lemon. Give it a few mixes over 15-20 minutes.
  8. To serve, top with dollops of greek yogurt and pickled onions on top. Garnish with fresh herbs, and enjoy as is, with your favorite rice (white, brown, quinoa, or if you’re lowcarb, my Kaizen lowcarb rice!)

Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

Air Fried Pepperoni Pizza Balls😮 💨

Serves: 1 servings · Cook: 8 min

Ingredients

  • 1 Biscuits
  • 1 Pizza Sauce
  • 1 Pepperoni
  • 1 Mozzarella Cheese
  • 1 Butter
  • 1 Minced Garlic
  • Garlic & Herb Seasoning
  • 1 Crushed Red peppers
  • 1 Grated Parmesan Cheese

Directions

  1. Roll out your biscuits.
  2. Add your sauce, pepperoni and mozzarella cheese, then form it into a ball.
  3. Melt some butter and add your seasonings, then brush your pizza balls with the butter and bake in air fryer on 350 for 8 minutes.
  4. Brush with more butter and top it with the parmesan cheese.

Asian

Air Fryer Korean Corn Dogs

Serves: 1 servings

No dough, no deep frying… and still the crispiest Korean corn dogs I’ve made 🙉🙈 Instead of kneading dough, waiting hours for it to rise, and deep-frying (I especially hate the lingering smell), I just used flattened sandwich bread and air-fried them — honestly shocked at how close they taste to the real deal 😭🔥 For the cheese ones, I used two sheets of bread to fully wrap the cheese (it was a thick block and I didn’t want it exploding — glad I did lol). Super crispy on the outside, perfectly melty inside 😍😋 And of course... Korean corn dogs need that little sugar sprinkle at the end ✨ Ketchup + mustard on top = you just can’t go wrong — so good and nostalgic 💛 If you’ve been craving Korean corn dogs but dread the dough or oil situation — please try this. Like genuinely. You’ll thank me 😍🤭🫶🫶🫶

Ingredients

  • 1 Hot dogs or sausages
  • 1 Cheese sticks, mozzarella or similar
  • 1 Sandwich bread slices
  • 2 eggs, beaten
  • 1 Panko breadcrumbs
  • 1 Sugar, for sprinkling
  • 1 Ketchup & mustard, for topping
  • 1 Oil spray, for air fryer

Directions

  1. Flatten sandwich bread with a rolling pin or glass until thin.
  2. Wrap sausages with one slice of flattened bread. For cheese dogs, fully wrap thick cheese sticks with two sheets of flattened bread to keep them sealed.
  3. Dip each in beaten eggs, then coat with panko breadcrumbs.
  4. Lightly spray the air fryer basket and tops of the corn dogs with oil.
  5. Air fry at 370°F (190°C) for 13 minutes, or until golden and crispy.
  6. Sprinkle with sugar while warm, and top with ketchup + mustard.

Notes

Just like you said I was sensitive to eggs 😂 what’s going on is once it’s in my system it makes me sensitive to cer things

Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

Carnivorous Air Fryer

Air fryer pork chicharron/cracklings

Serves: 1 servings · Prep: 10 min · Cook: 60 min

Dust off that air fryer because it’s time to get crispy.

Ingredients

  • 1 piece skin on pork belly, about 2-3lb
  • 1 kosher salt
  • Hardcore Carnivore chili lime seasoning

Directions

  1. Cut the pork into pieces small enough to fit in your air fryer basket
  2. Make a foil boat/pouch for each one, protecting the meat but leaving the skin exposed.
  3. Place a thick coating of salt on the skin, covering it completely.
  4. Air fry for 30 min at 375f.
  5. Remove the pork from the air fryer. Discard salt crust, brushing away any excess and get rid of the foil. Let it rest for 10 mins to get rid of some steam and slice into hedgehogs. DO NOT let the skin sit in any moisture.
  6. Return the strips to the air fryer, skin side up. Cook for 20 minutes at 390f.
  7. Give them one final blast at 400f for 5-10 minutes.
  8. Season generously with the chili lime and enjoy.

Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

Dessert Cookies Mama Goose’s Recipes

Andes Peppermint Crunch Chunkies

Serves: 60 small cookies

I got this recipe from a bag of Andes new peppermint crunch baking chips. The flavor is fabulous, but for some reason, when I baked them, they wouldn't bake through and I had to scrape them off the cookie sheets. They ended up being tasty, slightly chewy blobs... Perhaps someone will be brave enough to try this or to help me figure out how to fix the recipe?

Ingredients

  • 1 cup unsalted butter, softened
  • 1 cup dark brown sugar, packed
  • 1 1⁄3 cup granulated sugar
  • 1 large egg
  • 2 teaspoons vanilla extract
  • 1 1⁄2 teaspoon baking soda
  • 1 1⁄2 teaspoon salt
  • 1 3⁄4 cups flour
  • 1 cup old fashioned oats
  • 1 cup sweetened flaked coconut
  • 1 1⁄2 cups Andes mint baking chips
  • 1 1⁄4 cups coarsely chopped pecans, optional

Directions

  1. Preheat oven to 300 degrees F. Using an electric mixer, cream the butter, brown sugar, and granulated sugar until fluffy (about 3 minutes). Beat in egg and vanilla extract.
  2. On low speed, add baking soda and salt, and then flour. Mix completely. Stir in oats, coconut, pecans, and Andes baking chips.
  3. Measure out 2 tablespoons for large or 1 tablespoons for small cookies. Place on lightly greased cookie sheet two inches apart. Press lightly with a fork so they are about 1/4 thick (like peanut butter cookies). Sprinkle some of the remaining chips (from the 10 oz. bag) on top of each cookie.
  4. Bake for 20 minutes for large cookies or 12-15 minutes for small. Do not overbake.

Nutrition (per serving)

Calories: 73.1 · Fat: 3.8 · Saturated fat: 2.5 · Carbs: 9.2 · Sugar: 5.4 · Fiber: 0.3 · Protein: 0.7 · Sodium: 36.7 · Cholesterol: 11.2

Recipe from: food.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

Dessert Mama Goose’s Recipes

Apple Crisp

Serves: 12 large pieces · Prep: 20 min · Cook: 45 min

Once you try this recipe you will never want another! Perfection in every bite!

Ingredients

  • Apple mixture:
  • 10 medium Granny Smith apples, peeled, cored, and sliced, about 10 cups
  • 1 cup light brown sugar, packed, 200 g
  • 1 tablespoon all-purpose flour
  • 1 teaspoon ground cinnamon
  • Crisp mixture:
  • 2 cups quick oats, 180 g
  • 2 cups all-purpose flour, 250 g
  • 2 cups light brown sugar, packed, 400 g
  • 2 teaspoons ground cinnamon
  • 1 cup Challenge® butter, cold, cubed, 2 sticks / 227 g

Directions

  1. Preheat oven. prepare baking dish.:
  2. Preheat oven to 375°F. Prepare a 9x13-inch baking dish with nonstick cooking spray or coating with butter.
  3. Cover apples with brown sugar, flour, and cinnamon:
  4. Place sliced apples in a bowl. Add brown sugar, flour, and cinnamon. Stir gently and make sure every piece of apple is covered in the sugar mixture. Set aside.
  5. Make crisp. combine all ingredients except butter.:
  6. In a medium bowl, combine the oats, flour, brown sugar, and cinnamon.
  7. Add cubed butter.:
  8. Add the cubed butter. Using your hands or a fork, mix the butter into the flour mixture until the butter pieces are pea-sized or smaller.
  9. Layer crisp, apples, and more crisp on top. bake.:
  10. Spread 3 cups of the crisp over the bottom of your prepared pan and press flat. Pour the apple mixture over top and press down with a rubber spatula. Pour the remaining crisp mixture over the tops of the apples.
  11. Bake, uncovered for 35-45 minutes. The apple crisp is done when the fruit is bubbling and the top is golden brown.
  12. Serve warm.

Notes

Baking the Oat Mixture

If you bake at 375°F for 35 minutes, the bottom oat mixture will be slightly caramelized and gooey while the top will be perfect crispy deliciousness.

If you bake at 375°F for 45 minutes, the bottom oat mixture will be crispy throughout. The top will be darker, but if you prefer a less dark crispy top you can add a foil tent to the crisp after 35 minutes.

Nutrition (per serving)

Serving Size: 12 · Calories: 422

Recipe from: iambaker.net, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

Chicken

Arsenio’s Hot Honey Cornflake Chicken

Serves: 1 servings

Crispy. Sweet. Spicy. Everything you want in a bite.

Ingredients

  • Marinade:
  • ⅓ cup pickle juice
  • ¼ cup milk
  • ½ tsp salt
  • ½ tsp black pepper
  • ½ tsp paprika
  • ½ tsp onion powder
  • ½ tsp garlic powder
  • ¼ tsp chili flakes
  • Dredge:
  • ⅓ cup all-purpose flour
  • 2 eggs
  • 1 Crushed Kellogg’s Corn Flakes
  • Hot honey sauce:
  • ½ cup honey
  • ¼ cup Frank’s RedHot
  • ½ tsp chili flakes

Directions

  1. Combine chicken breast with pickle juice, chili flakes, salt, pepper, garlic powder, onion powder, and paprika for the marinade.
  2. Prepare the dredge using flour, eggs, and crushed cornflakes.
  3. Coat the chicken in the egg mixture and cornflakes.
  4. Prepare the sauce by combining honey, Frank's RedHot, and chili flakes.
  5. Cook the chicken.
  6. Toss the cooked chicken in the hot honey sauce.

Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

Appetizers Mama Goose’s Recipes

Bacon Cheddar Crackers

Serves: 60 crackers · Prep: 15 min · Cook: 16 min

Having a party this season? Christmas, New Year's, blah-de-blah-de-blah? Well, I think we all know that every great party needs a meat and cheese and cracker tray. Why not fancy it up a little this year and make your own crackers? It is super simple and they taste sooooooooooo good. Little bites of crispy, cheesey, bacony, spicy, crackerness. Crackerness? Yes. It's a word now.

Ingredients

  • 2 cups flour
  • 1 tsp baking powder
  • ½ tsp Maple Bacon Sea Salt, optional - available from Epicure Selections
  • ½ tsp cayenne pepper
  • 1 cup butter, cold
  • 2 cups shredded, aged cheddar cheese
  • 10 strips bacon, cooked, crumbled
  • ¼ cup milk

Directions

  1. Preheat oven to 375. Line a cookie sheet with parchment paper. Set aside.
  2. In a large bowl, combine flour, baking powder, Maple Bacon sea salt (optional), cayenne pepper.
  3. Using 2 knives or a pastry cutter, cut in butter until mixture resembles coarse crumbs. Stir in cheese and bacon.
  4. Mix in milk to form dough.
  5. Using a small cookie scoop, place balls of dough about an inch apart. Using the bottom of a glass, dipped in flour, OR the tines of a fork - squish the ball so it is flat.
  6. It should be fairly thin but not too thin.
  7. Bake at 375 for about 16 minutes. I baked for 8 minutes, rotated cookie sheet, and then another 8 minutes. The edges and top will be a little bit brown. Remove from oven and let cool on wire rack.

Notes

This recipe made 60 crackers with me using the small cookie scoop (about 1 to 1 1/2 Tbsp of dough). You can half the recipe if you want but, trust me, you will want to make lots of these. They will disappear quickly. You can also add more or less cayenne pepper to suit your tastes. As written above I found them spicy (I'm a wimp) but Sue and the rest of the family thought they were "just right". Now you can go and impress your guests and have them think you slaved over a hot stove all day making them tasty snacks when really, these only take about 30 to 45 minutes to whip up. Oh, yeah, you can save even more time by using this tip - instead of using a cookie scoop you can roll the dough into 2 logs, chill for a bit to firm up, and then simply slice the crackers about 1/4 inch thick.

Recipe from: joandsue.blogspot.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

Baked Goods Albanian

Balkan Feta-Cream Cheese Pie (Burek sa Sirom)

Serves: 6 servings · Prep: 30 min · Cook: 30 min

Burek sa sirom is a Balkan savory pie made with flaky filo (also spelled "phyllo") dough and, in this case, a combination of feta and cream cheeses. It is popular among Bulgarians, Bosnians, Serbians, Croats, Turks and, of course, there are the famous triangle-shaped Greek spanakopitas. There are myriad versions of burek some with cheese filling and some with meat filling. They can be round (as this one is) or coiled into horseshoe shapes or cigar shapes as well as those baked in a 13x9-inch pan. A slice of burek goes great with a glass of cold kefir especially, some say, if you have a hangover.

Ingredients

  • 1 pound feta cheese, crumbled
  • 8 ounces cream cheese, softened
  • 2 large eggs, at room temperature, beaten
  • 4 tablespoons chopped fresh parsley
  • 2 tablespoons chopped fresh dill
  • 1 package filo dough, thawed, 1-pound
  • 4 ounces unsalted butter, melted, 1/2 cup
  • ½ cup olive oil

Directions

  1. Gather the ingredients.
  2. In a large bowl, mix together feta cheese and cream cheese until light and fluffy. Add eggs, parsley, and dill, mixing well. Set aside.
  3. Separate filo dough into two piles of 24 sheets. Cut filo dough sheets in half. You should have two piles of 48 leaves. Cover with parchment paper followed by a damp towel or plastic wrap so they don't dry out.
  4. Heat oven to 375 F. In a small bowl, mix together melted butter and olive oil. Using a pastry brush, butter 6 (8-inch) round shallow pans.
  5. Lay down 2 sheets of filo dough and brush with butter-oil mixture. Repeat with 2 more sheets of filo dough and butter-oil mixture. Spread 1/6 of the cheese mixture evenly on top and to the edges.
  6. Lay down 2 sheets of filo dough on top of the cheese mixture and brush with butter-oil mixture. Repeat with 2 more sheets. Tuck edges of filo down sides of pan to create a rounded edge. Brush generously with butter-oil mixture.
  7. Repeat for 5 remaining pies. Each pie will use 4 sheets on the bottom and 4 sheets on the top for a total of 8 sheets each.
  8. Bake 20 to 30 minutes or until golden brown.
  9. Serve warm or at room temperature.

Notes

Bureks can be assembled and frozen unbaked. When ready to serve, bake directly from the freezer, increasing baking time by at least 15 minutes.

Nutrition (per serving)

Serving Size: 1 · Calories: 877 · Fat: 69 · Saturated fat: 33 · Carbs: 45 · Sugar: 5 · Fiber: 2 · Protein: 21 · Sodium: 1205 · Cholesterol: 208

Recipe from: thespruceeats.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

Baked Goods Dessert

Banana Bread

Serves: 10 1 loaf · Prep: 10 min · Cook: 60 min

Put those ripe bananas to use in this moist, delicious banana bread and you'll learn why it's the most popular recipe on Simply Recipes.

Ingredients

  • 3 medium very ripe bananas, 2-3, peeled, 7" to 7-7/8" long, about 1 1/4 to 1 1/2 cups mashed
  • ⅓ cup butter, unsalted or salted, melted, 76g
  • ½ teaspoon baking soda, not baking powder
  • 1 pinch salt
  • ¾ cup sugar, 150g, 1/2 cup if you would like it less sweet, 1 cup if more sweet
  • 1 large egg, beaten
  • 1 teaspoon vanilla extract
  • 1 ½ cups all-purpose flour, 205g

Directions

  1. Preheat the oven to 350°f (175°c):
  2. Butter an 8x4-inch loaf pan.
  3. Mash the bananas and add the butter:
  4. In a mixing bowl, mash the ripe bananas with a fork until completely smooth. Stir the melted butter into the mashed bananas.
  5. Mix in the remaining ingredients:
  6. Mix in the baking soda and salt. Stir in the sugar, beaten egg, and vanilla extract. Mix in the flour.
  7. Bake the bread:
  8. Pour the batter into your prepared loaf pan.
  9. Bake for 55 to 65 minutes at 350°F (175°C), or until a toothpick or wooden skewer inserted into the center comes out clean. A few dry crumbs are okay; streaks of wet batter are not. If the outside of the loaf is browned but the center is still wet, loosely tent the loaf with foil and continue baking until the loaf is fully baked.
  10. Cool and serve:
  11. Remove from oven and let cool in the pan for a few minutes. Then remove the banana bread from the pan and let cool completely before serving. Slice and serve. (A bread knife helps to make slices that aren't crumbly.)
  12. Wrapped well, the banana bread will keep at room temperature for 4 days. For longer storage, refrigerate the loaf up to 5 days, or freeze it.

Notes

No need for a mixer for this recipe! Clean-up is easy, too. If you want, you can mix everything in one bowl. This recipe calls for an 8x4-inch loaf pan. If you are baking in an 8 1/2x4 1/2-inch pan, bake for 47 to 57 minutes. If baking in a 9x5-inch pan, bake for 45 to 55 minutes. Note that the larger pan size will produce a flatter loaf. The best bananas to use for banana bread are those that are overripe. The yellow peels should be at least half browned, and the bananas inside squishy and browning.

Nutrition (per serving)

Serving Size: 1 · Calories: 220 · Fat: 7 · Saturated fat: 4 · Carbs: 37 · Sugar: 19 · Fiber: 1 · Protein: 3 · Sodium: 85 · Cholesterol: 35

Recipe from: simplyrecipes.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

Dessert

Banana Bread Pudding Cake

Serves: 1 servings · Cook: 45 min

You guys are incredible!!! I’m still blown away by hitting 500k🍾🎉🥳🤯 Celebrating with one of my fave cakes!

Ingredients

  • 2 cups 1.1 gluten free flour or all purpose flour
  • 2 tsp baking powder
  • ¼ tsp baking soda
  • ½ tsp kosher salt
  • 1 tsp ground cinnamon
  • ⅓ cup granulated sugar
  • ⅓ cup brown sugar
  • 1 cup of mashed bananas, approx 2
  • ⅓ cup maple syrup
  • 2 tsp vanilla extract
  • 3 eggs
  • ¾ cup buttermilk
  • 1 Toffee Sauce
  • 8 tbsp butter
  • ¾ cup brown sugar, packed
  • ¾ cup heavy cream
  • 1 tsp vanilla extraction

Directions

  1. Toffee sauce:
  2. Make the toffee sauce while the cake is baking. Add the butter and sugar to a medium stock pot over medium/low heat. Mix well and once the sugar and butter and mixed together well, about 10 minutes.
  3. Mix in the heavy cream and vanilla. Cook for an additional 5 minutes. Should note boil, if it starts to boil reduce heat. Should be silky smooth. Remove, pour into a glass container to cool while the cake bakes.
  4. Preheat the oven to 350 degrees. This cake will fit a 9x9x3” deep baking dish or a 9x11x3 in deep baking dish. (NOTE: you can use a larger baking dish, this will affect the bake time, always check cake doneness with toothpick) Spray the dish with nonstick baking spray.
  5. Mash the bananas. Add both sugars and the butter to a mixing bowl and mix for 3 minutes. Should be light and a little fluffy.
  6. Mix in the eggs, vanilla, buttermilk, maple syrup and bananas.
  7. In a separate bowl add all of the dry ingredients and mix well. Add the dry to the wet ingredients and mix until well incorporated.
  8. Pour into the baking dish and bake for 45-55 minutes. When you insert a toothpick it should come out clean. The cake will not jiggle. This is a super moist, more of a dense cake. Remove and allow to cool.
  9. Poke holes using a toothpick all over the top of the cake. Pour the toffee sauce on top. Let it soak in for at least 1 hr. Enjoy!

Notes

you can use a

Dessert

banana cake

Serves: 1 servings

Ingredients

  • 330 g cake flour, sifted, 2 ¾ cups
  • 8 g cornstarch, 1 tbsp
  • 10 g baking powder, 2 tsp
  • 6 g baking soda, 1 tsp
  • ¾ tsp fine sea salt
  • 1 tsp ground cinnamon
  • ¼ tsp freshly grated nutmeg
  • ⅛ tsp ground cardamom
  • 360 g ripe banana purée, 1 ½ cups, 3 large bananas, blended smooth
  • 240 g non-dairy milk, room temp, 1 cup
  • 15 g apple cider vinegar, 1 tbsp
  • 160 g plain non-dairy yogurt, room temp, ⅔ cup
  • 200 g organic cane sugar, 1 cup
  • 100 g light brown sugar, ½ cup
  • 120 g neutral oil, ½ cup
  • 2 tsp vanilla bean powder
  • Cinnamon roll filling:
  • 113 g non-dairy butter, ½ cup
  • 200 g brown sugar, 1 cup
  • 1 tsp ground cinnamon
  • ½ tsp fine sea salt
  • 60 g non-dairy heavy cream, ¼ cup
  • 1 tsp vanilla bean paste
  • 1 1–3 tbsp non-dairy milk
  • Cream cheese frosting:
  • 450 g non-dairy cream cheese, cold, 2 cups
  • 340 g non-dairy butter, cool but pliable, 1 ½ cups
  • 720 g powdered sugar, sifted, 6 cups
  • 1 tsp vanilla bean powder
  • 1 1.5–3/4 tsp fine sea salt
  • 1 2–3 tbsp non-dairy milk

Directions

  1. Prep:
  2. Heat oven to 335°F. Grease and line three 8-inch pans.
  3. Curdle:
  4. Stir milk + vinegar; rest 5 minutes.
  5. Wet:
  6. Whisk banana, yogurt, oil, sugars, vanilla. Stir in milk.
  7. Dry:
  8. Whisk remaining dry ingredients.
  9. Combine:
  10. Fold dry into wet just until smooth.
  11. Bake:
  12. Divide evenly. Bake 28–32 minutes until set.
  13. Cool:
  14. Rest 15 minutes, then wrap warm and chill.
  15. Instructions:
  16. Melt butter.
  17. Stir in sugar + cinnamon; cook until glossy.
  18. Whisk in cream; simmer 1–2 minutes.
  19. Remove from heat; add salt + vanilla. Cool.
  20. Once cold, whisk in milk as needed until spreadable.
  21. Method:
  22. Beat butter + cream cheese smooth. Add sugar gradually. Mix in vanilla + salt. Add milk as needed. Whip until fluffy and pipeable.
  23. Assembly:
  24. Pipe thin dam on each layer.
  25. Spread thin frosting inside dam.
  26. Spoon ⅓ goo over each layer.
  27. Stack and chill briefly.
  28. Frost exterior.
  29. Optional:
  30. Drizzle or swirl goo on top.

Notes

Dessert

Banana Cake with Salted Caramel Cream Cheese Frosting

Serves: 1 servings · Cook: 35 min

This cake is a 10/10 and would be perfect for Easter brunch (or any important event coming up). It’s easy to make since there’s no layering involved. You just need a 9×13 pan and a mixer.

Ingredients

  • For the cake:
  • 1 cup salted butter, browned, 2 sticks, or melted
  • 1 cup granulated sugar
  • 1 cup packed light brown sugar
  • 4 large very ripe bananas, mashed, about 1.5 - 2 cups
  • 3 large eggs, room temperature
  • 1 tbsp vanilla bean paste or extract
  • 1 cup buttermilk, room temperature
  • 2 ½ cups all-purpose flour
  • 1 tsp baking soda
  • ¼ tsp salt
  • 1 tsp ground cinnamon, optional
  • For the frosting:
  • 1 Make the Salted Caramel Sauce
  • 1 cup granulated sugar
  • 6 tbsp unsalted butter, room temperature
  • ½ cup heavy cream, room temperature
  • 1 tsp sea salt, plus more to taste
  • 2 Whip the Frosting
  • 8 oz full-fat cream cheese, softened, 1 block
  • ½ cup unsalted butter, softened, 1 stick
  • 1 3–4 cups powdered sugar, sifted
  • ½ cup of your cooled Salted Caramel Sauce
  • 1 tsp vanilla bean paste or extract

Directions

  1. For the cake:
  2. Preheat oven to 350°F (175°C). Grease a 9x13 inch pan.
  3. In a large bowl, mix melted/browned butter with sugars. Whisk in eggs one at a time, then add mashed bananas and vanilla.
  4. In a separate bowl, sift together flour, baking soda, salt, and cinnamon.
  5. Add the dry ingredients and buttermilk to the wet mixture, starting and ending with dry. Mix until just combined; do not over-mix.
  6. Pour into the pan. Bake for 35-40 minutes, or until a toothpick comes out clean. Let it cool completely before frosting.
  7. For the frosting:
  8. Heat sugar in a medium saucepan over medium heat, stirring constantly with a wooden spoon until it melts into a thick, amber-colored liquid.
  9. Carefully whisk in the butter until melted. Be cautious as the mixture will bubble up rapidly.
  10. Slowly pour in the heavy cream while whisking. Let it softly boil for 39 seconds, then remove from heat.
  11. Stir in the salt. Pour into a glass jar and let it cool completely at room temperature (or in the fridge) until thickened.
  12. In a large bowl, beat the softened butter and cream cheese together until very smooth and fluffy (about 2–3 minutes).
  13. Mix in ½ cup of your cooled caramel sauce and the vanilla until fully incorporated.
  14. Gradually add powdered sugar one cup at a time on low speed until you reach your desired spreading consistency.
  15. Increase the speed to medium-high and beat for 2 minutes until light and airy.
  16. After spreading the frosting over your cooled banana cake, use a spoon to drizzle any remaining caramel sauce over the top and swirl it in with a knife for a marbled look.

Recipe from: christinabassrossetti.substack.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

BBQ Cheeseburger Sliders

Serves: 1 serves · Prep: 15 min · Cook: 15 min

#sponsored BBQ Cheeseburger Sliders made with @kingshawaiian Original Sweet Rolls are an easy weeknight dinner the whole family will love!

Ingredients

  • 12 count King’s Hawaiian Original Sweet Rolls
  • 1 lb ground beef
  • ½ tsp each of salt and pepper
  • 1 tsp each of garlic powder, onion powder, and smoked paprika
  • 1 tbsp minced garlic
  • 2 tbsp BBQ sauce
  • 1 Pepper jack cheese slices
  • 1 Shredded colby jack cheese
  • 1 Crispy fried onions
  • 6 pieces of bacon, cooked and crumbled
  • 2 tbsp melted butter
  • 1 Sesame seeds

Directions

  1. Preheat your oven to 350 degrees F.
  2. Cook 1 lb of ground beef in a skillet over medium-high heat. Season with salt, pepper, garlic powder, onion powder, and smoked paprika. Once the ground beef is browned, drain any extra grease from the skillet. Add the minced garlic and BBQ sauce. Stir well.
  3. Slice King’s Hawaiian Original Sweet Rolls in half and put the bottom of the rolls in a greased baking dish or on a sheet pan.
  4. Add a layer of pepper jack cheese. Add the ground beef on top of the cheese. Top the ground beef with shredded colby jack cheese, some crumbled bacon, and crispy fried onions. Top with a little bit more BBQ sauce if desired!
  5. Put the tops of the slider rolls on and brush them with melted butter. Sprinkle sesame seeds on top. Bake at 350 degrees F for about 10-15 minutes. Enjoy!

Breakfast Dessert

Berries & Cream Cottage Cheese Pancakes 🥞🍓

Serves: 1 servings

Our Berries & Cream Protein Smoothie is finally BACK IN STOCK! Head to www.ShopFlavCity.com and grab a bag so you can try this amazing protein-rich recipe!

Ingredients

  • 2 cups cottage cheese
  • 1 egg
  • 1 scoop @shopflavcity Berries & Cream Protein Smoothie, 1/2 cup
  • ¼ cup paleo flour + 2 tbsp for rolling out the pancakes
  • 1 tsp vanilla bean ghee
  • Powdered maple sugar, for dusting
  • 1 Maple syrup
  • Whipped cream quenelle:
  • ¼ cup heavy cream
  • ½ scoop Berries & Cream protein, 1/4 cup

Directions

  1. Add the cottage cheese to a cheese cloth, then strain out as much liquid as you can over your sink or a bowl. This is key in making sure the pancakes maintain their form and cook evenly.
  2. After straining out the liquid, empty the curds in a separate bowl, then mash the curds smaller with a fork. Add the egg, stir it in, then add the flour and @shopflavcity Protein Smoothie, and stir again to combine. If needed, use a potato masher to really incorporate it.
  3. Separate the dough into 4 equal portions and roll each out into a ball, then flattening it on the top and on the sides to create a hockey puck.
  4. Heat our @shopflavcity Ceramic Pan on medium/low heat and add some ghee. Place the pancakes on the pan and cook for a few minutes on each side until they are golden brown. Remember, low and slow is the key!
  5. Remove from the pan and set aside to cool for a few minutes.
  6. While the pancakes cool, make the whipped cream quenelle by beating the heavy cream and the @shopflavcity Berries & Cream protein til it’s nice and thick. Take two spoons and gently roll a spoonful of whipped cream back and forth from spoon to spoon until you get a nice oval shaped quenelle.
  7. Top the pancakes with a dusting of powdered maple sugar, the quenelle, and a dollop of maple syrup.
  8. Enjoy!

Notes

😍😍😍😍😍

Berry Acai Bowl

Serves: 1 Bowl · Prep: 5 min · Cook: 5 min

Perfect healthy breakfast for easy mornings. Acai bowls are essentially thick smoothie bowls loaded with toppings - yum! Thanks to their high antioxidant content, acai berries have many potential health benefits. They're loaded with powerful plant compounds that act as antioxidants and could have benefits for your brain, heart and overall health.

Ingredients

  • Smoothie:
  • 2 tsp acai powder
  • 1 handful blueberries
  • 1 medium banana
  • 0.75 cup almond milk, or milk of your choice - adjust recipe as required
  • Topping:
  • 4 strawberries, sliced
  • 1 sprinkle coconut flakes
  • sprinkle chia seeds
  • 1 medium banana, sliced
  • 1 small handful raspberries
  • 1 handful blueberries
  • 0.5 kiwi, sliced
  • 1 handful granola

Directions

  1. Put all the smoothie ingredients into a blender
  2. If the mixture is having trouble blending, add more almond milk or water
  3. Blend for 2-3 minutes or until the smoothie has no lumps. Pour the smoothie mixture into a bowl
  4. Top with desired ingredients
  5. Serve and enjoy!

Dessert Mama Goose’s Recipes

Best Blueberry Dump Cake

Serves: 12 -ish servings · Prep: 5 min · Cook: 45 min

This delicious Blueberry Dump Cake Recipe is made with only 5 ingredients and is the most amazing buttery, decadent blueberry dessert ever! Sweet Blueberry pie filling topped with a crunchy buttery cake topping, severd with a scoop of vanilla ice cream. What’s not to love?

Ingredients

  • 1 15.25 oz box of yellow cake mix
  • 1 21 oz can of blueberry pie filling
  • 1 large can of crushed pineapple, drained, 20 oz
  • 1 cup fresh blueberries
  • ½ cup shredded sweetened coconut, optional
  • 1 ½ sticks of butter, use as much needed to cover the cake

Directions

  1. Preheat oven to 375.
  2. Spread the can of blueberry pie filling at the bottom of a 9.5 x 13 cake pan/pyrex dish (or similar size-but no bigger.)
  3. Spread crushed pineapple over pie filling and top with shredded coconut and 3/4 of the fresh blueberries.
  4. Evenly sprinkle the dry cake mix over the top of the fruit.
  5. Thinly slice the butter sticks and place the sliced butter pieces over the dry cake mix. (you can melt the butter and drizzle it too, but I have found the results are best by slicing the butter.)
  6. Sprinkle the rest of the fresh blueberries on top of the cake mix.
  7. Bake for 40 to 45 minutes or until the cake topping is golden brown.

Notes

Nutrition calculated on 12 servings, no ice cream.

Nutrition (per serving)

Mama Goose’s Recipes Chicken

Best Chicken Piccata With Lemon Sauce

Serves: 1 servings

Chicken piccata with lemon sauce is exquisite and easy to prepare. Seasoned with parmesan and parsley, the chicken cooks up golden brown, then is drizzled with a light lemon sauce. The light and luscious lemon sauce really pops without being too acidic, it is simply divine.

Ingredients

  • 8 boneless skinless chicken breast halves, 4 ounces each
  • 3 teaspoons olive oil, divided
  • 2 tablespoons butter
  • ½ cup all-purpose flour
  • ½ cup grated Parmesan cheese
  • ½ cup egg substitute
  • ½ teaspoon salt
  • 2 tablespoons plus 1/4 cup dry white wine or chicken broth, divided
  • ¼ cup minced fresh parsley
  • ⅛ teaspoon hot pepper sauce
  • 5 tablespoons lemon juice, divided
  • 3 garlic cloves, minced

Directions

  1. Flatten chicken to 1/4-in. thickness. In a shallow dish, combine the egg substitute, 2 tablespoons wine, 2 tablespoons lemon juice, garlic, and hot pepper sauce. In an another shallow dish, combine the flour, Parmesan cheese, parsley, and salt. Coat chicken with flour mixture, dip in egg substitute mixture, then coat again with flour mixture.
  2. In a large nonstick skillet, brown four chicken breast halves in 1-1/2 teaspoons oil for 3-5 minutes on each side or until juices run clear. Remove and keep warm. Drain drippings. Repeat with remaining chicken and oil. Remove and keep warm.
  3. In the same pan, melt butter. Add the remaining wine and lemon juice. Bring to a boil. Boil, uncovered, until sauce is reduced by a fourth. Drizzle over chicken.

Recipe from: food.menuphd.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

Soup Mama Goose’s Recipes

Best Ever Beef Stew

Serves: 8 servings

This Beef Stew is the #1 most popular recipe on my blog & my readers agree - it's the BEST EVER!

Ingredients

  • 2 pounds beef stew meat
  • ½ cup all purpose flour
  • 1 tablespoon seasoning salt
  • 2 tablespoons olive oil
  • ½ teaspoon black pepper
  • 1 large onion diced
  • 2 bay leaves
  • ¼ cup Worcestershire sauce
  • 2 cups water
  • 2 heaping teaspoons beef demi-glace or beef Swanson Flavor Boost or Better than Bouillon
  • 4 medium to large red skinned potatoes washed & diced
  • 3 large carrots peeled & sliced
  • 1 stalk celery diced

Directions

  1. Shake the beef stew meat in a resealable plastic bag with the flour & seasoning salt until evenly coated. Heat the olive oil in a skillet; add the floured stew meat to the pan & brown on all sides. Remove using a slotted spoon or tongs & place into the bottom of a slow cooker. Season with pepper.
  2. Saute the diced onion in the same hot skillet for 2 minutes. Don't worry about cleaning it out in between - that's added flavor! Transfer the onions into the slow cooker as well. Pour the Worcestershire sauce, water, & beef demi glace (or Swanson Flavor Boost or Better than Bouillon) into the skillet and whisk until mixed, making sure you scrape up any browned bits in the bottom of the pan. Turn off the heat & set aside.
  3. Add the diced potatoes, carrots, celery, & bay leaves to the slow cooker. Now, pour the broth/Worcestershire mixture in the crockpot as well. Cover; cook on low for 8 hours. Remove the bay leaves before serving.

Recipe from: mrshappyhomemaker.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

Dessert Asian

Biko Recipe (Easy Filipino Sticky Rice Cake)

Serves: 20 squares · Prep: 20 min · Cook: 60 min

This easy Biko recipe makes a sweet and chewy Filipino rice cake with coconut milk and brown sugar. It’s rich, sticky, and topped with a caramelized glaze!

Ingredients

  • 4 cups glutinous rice
  • 5 cups full-fat coconut milk, divided
  • 1 ½ cups water
  • 2 ½ cups dark brown sugar, divided
  • ½ tsp salt

Directions

  1. Prepping the rice:
  2. Start by soaking the glutinous rice in water overnight. This ensures a perfectly sticky texture for your Biko. After your rice has finished soaking, drain and rinse.
  3. Cooking the rice:
  4. Set aside 3/4 cup of the coconut milk. Add the remaining coconut milk and water to a large pot. Bring the liquid to a gentle boil, then add soaked rice. Reduce to medium heat, and let it simmer until the rice is cooked and has absorbed most of the liquid. This should take 6-8 minutes.
  5. Adding sweetness:
  6. Once the rice is cooked, it's time to add the sweetness! Reduce heat to medium-low, then stir in 1 1/2 cups of your brown sugar and salt until fully dissolved, infusing the rice with that irresistible caramel flavor. Cook for another 4-7 minutes, stirring constantly. Taste test to ensure the rice is cooked through. The rice mixture will be very thick and sticky.
  7. Letting it set:
  8. Preheat oven to 350 degrees F and transfer the sweetened cooked rice mixture to a greased 9x13" baking dish or pan, spreading it out evenly.
  9. Caramel topping:
  10. To a small sauce pot, combine the 3/4 cup coconut milk that was set aside with the remaining 1 cup brown sugar. Bring to a boil until mixture thickens slightly and sugar is fully dissolved. Remove from heat and allow to cool for 5 minutes. Pour over the rice mixture, then pop it in the oven and let it bake until the top is golden brown and slightly crispy. This will take about 60-65 minutes.
  11. Serve and enjoy:
  12. Once cooled, cut your Biko into squares or diamonds and serve it up to eager family and friends. Trust me, they won't be able to resist!

Nutrition (per serving)

Serving Size: 1 · Calories: 232 · Fat: 12 · Saturated fat: 11 · Trans fat: 0 · Unsaturated fat: 1 · Carbs: 31 · Sugar: 22 · Fiber: 0 · Protein: 2 · Sodium: 74 · Cholesterol: 0

Mexican Sandwich

Birria grilled cheese

Serves: 1 serve

Birria grilled cheese by @brandongouveia is a fusion delight combining the rich savory flavors of Mexican birria with the comforting, gooey classic grilled cheese sandwich. Find the recipe below and linked in bio

Ingredients

  • 3 pounds chuck roast
  • 2 tablespoons olive oil
  • 6 cloves garlic minced
  • 4 Roma tomatoes, chopped
  • 2 tablespoons canned chipotle peppers
  • ½ large yellow onion, thinly sliced
  • 10 dried guajillo peppers, seeds and stems removed
  • 5 arbol chiles peppers
  • 6 cups beef broth, plus more for braising if needed
  • 5 bay leaves
  • 2 tablespoons beef bouillon
  • 3 tablespoons chili powder
  • ½ tablespoon smoked paprika
  • 2 teaspoons dried thyme
  • 1 ½ tablespoons ground cumin
  • 1 teaspoon peppercorns
  • 1 tablespoon Mexican oregano
  • 1 cinnamon stick
  • 1 Sourdough bread loaf, sliced
  • 1 Cheddar cheese, slices
  • 1 Oaxaca cheese, shredded
  • 3 tablespoons unsalted butter, for grilled cheese
  • ½ a yellow onion, diced, for sandwich
  • 1 Cilantro, roughly chopped

Directions

  1. Season the chuck roast generously with salt and pepper. Heat olive oil in a large Dutch oven over medium-high heat. Add the meat sear on all sides, until browned and a crust has formed. Remove beef and set aside.
  2. In the same pot, add garlic, tomatoes, chipotle peppers, and onions and cook until softened and fragrant, about 3-4 minutes.
  3. Add the seared beef back in, along with the guajillo and arbol peppers, beef broth, and al
  4. Next, add in your seared beef, chili peppers, beef broth, bay leaves, chili powder, smoked paprika, thyme, cumin, peppercorns, oregano and cinnamon stick. Stir.
  5. Cover and simmer on low heat for 2 hours, until meat is falling apart. Remove the meat and vegetables, leaving the broth in the pot. Blend vegetables, then stir back into the broth. Shred beef and add back into the broth.
  6. Melt butter on a griddle or skillet over medium heat. Add two slices of bread down. Place two slices of cheddar cheese and two slices of Oaxaca cheese on each slice of bread.
  7. Add shredded beef on the bread, followed by some of the sauce sauce, onions, and cilantro. Allow cheese to melt, and bread to get toasty, then fold up the sandwiches.
  8. Serve with more broth for dipping.
  9. Enjoy!

Notes

Baby Sick

Bone Marrow Immune Lollipops

Serves: 1 servings

Ingredients

  • 8 beef marrow bones or about 1 to 1.25 cups marrow, 6-8
  • ½ to 3/4 cup wild blueberries fresh or frozen
  • 2 teaspoons Manuka honey just enough to lightly sweeten, 1-2, skip this if your baby is not yet 1 year old
  • Optional for toddlers only 1/8 teaspoon ginger or turmeric for the batch

Directions

  1. Roast marrow bones at 400°F for 15 to 20 minutes until soft and bubbling
  2. Scoop marrow into a saucepan and gently warm until melted
  3. Strain if desired for a smooth texture
  4. Optional step whip the marrow until light and creamy then gently warm again
  5. In a separate pan heat blueberries over low to medium heat for 5 to 8 minutes until they break down into a thick syrup
  6. Stir blueberry syrup into the warm marrow
  7. Add 1 to 2 teaspoons Manuka honey to lightly sweeten
  8. For toddlers only add a small pinch of ginger or turmeric
  9. Pour into silicone lollipop or popsicle molds
  10. Freeze until solid and store in the freezer

Notes

IMPORTANT- honey is only allowed after baby is 1 years old Honey is only for babies over 1 year old Ginger and turmeric are best for toddlers not infants Always supervise while eating

Dessert

BROWN BUTTER CREAM CHEESE SNICKERDOODLE COBBLER

Serves: 1 servings · Prep: 15 min · Cook: 35 min

Snickerdoodle + brown butter + cream cheese??? I can’t think of a better combo ✨ Couldn’t be easier and just pure heaven in every bite!

Ingredients

  • ⅓ cup salted butter
  • ¾ cup milk of choice
  • 2 tsp vanilla extract
  • 1 ½ cups granulated sugar
  • 1 ½ cups all-purpose flour
  • 2 ¼ tsp baking powder
  • ¾ tsp salt
  • 2 tsp cinnamon
  • 1 ½ cups brown sugar, light or dark
  • 1 ½ cups hot water
  • 6 oz cream cheese, cut into chunks
  • Topping:
  • ¼ cup granulated sugar
  • ½ tsp cinnamon
  • Vanilla ice cream, for serving

Directions

  1. Preheat the oven to 350F.
  2. Start by browning the butter. Add the butter to a saucepan over medium-high heat. Allow to melt and slightly brown, while stirring continuously (~5-8 minutes). Remove from heat and pour the brown butter into a 9x13” baking pan. Set aside.
  3. Make the cobbler. In a medium bowl, whisk together the flour, baking powder, salt, and cinnamon. Set aside.
  4. In a separate large bowl, whisk together the milk, vanilla, and granulated sugar.
  5. Add the dry ingredients to the wet, and use a wooden spoon or spatula to ix until just combined.
  6. Spoon or scoop the batter over the brown butter in the baking pan, being careful not to mix.
  7. Place the cubes of cream cheese all over the surface of the batter.
  8. Sprinkle the brown sugar all over the batter and cream cheese.
  9. Then, gently pour hot water over the batter and brown sugar.
  10. Carefully place the baking pan into the oven, and bake at 350F for 35-45 minutes, until golden brown and mostly set (the bottom will still be gooey).
  11. While baking, mix the sugar and cinnamon together for the topping.
  12. Remove the cobbler from the oven, and immediately sprinkle the cinnamon sugar mixture over the top.
  13. Let cool slightly before serving.
  14. Serve warm with a scoop of vanilla ice cream.
  15. Enjoy!

Notes

Appetizers

Buffalo Chicken Dip Crispy Rice Cups

Serves: 18 cups

Your favorite dip...but make it handheld.

Ingredients

  • 12 oz shredded chicken
  • 4 oz buffalo sauce
  • 8 oz cream cheese, softened
  • ¼ c sour cream
  • 6 oz shredded cheddar + more for topping
  • 16 oz cooked sushi rice
  • 3 tbsp avocado oil + more for coating muffin tin, 2-3
  • 1 Chives
  • 1 Ranch dressing

Directions

  1. Preheat oven to 400 F.
  2. Add avocado oil to rice and combine.
  3. Coat a muffin tin with avocado oil. (A nonstick muffin tin is highly recommended for this recipe!)
  4. Scoop equal amounts of rice into each compartment, filling about ¾ full. Use a ¼ cup measuring cup to press and smooth the tops. Center the measuring cup on each portion and press down to create a “cup” shape, rice should just reach the top. Bake at 400°F for 20 minutes.
  5. While the rice is baking, combine the chicken, cream cheese, sour cream, cheese and buffalo sauce.
  6. Remove the muffin tin from oven and divide the chicken evenly into each rice cup - just enough that it fills the top. Smooth out the top and add cheese to each one. Return to the oven and bake for another 10 minutes, until cheese is bubbling.
  7. Let the cups cool for 5-10 min. Use an offset spatula to gently release any edges. If using a nonstick tin, they should come out easily! Carefully slide the spatula under each cup and lift out.
  8. Top each cup with ranch, more buffalo sauce, and chives. Enjoy!

Recipe from: substack.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

Buffalo Chicken Sticks

Serves: 1 servings

Anyways… here is how I made them:

Ingredients

  • 2 cups shredded chicken, I used rotisserie
  • 6 oz cream cheese, softened
  • 8 oz mozzarella, grated
  • ½ cup buffalo sauce
  • 1 Pepper, garlic powder, onion powder
  • 4 eggs, whisked
  • 3 cups seasoned breadcrumbs
  • 1 Vegetable oil for frying

Directions

  1. Mix chicken, cream cheese, mozzarella, buffalo sauce, and seasonings.
  2. Press mixture into a parchment paper lined loaf pan and freeze 1–2 hours.
  3. Remove and cut into 8 sticks.
  4. Heat oil.
  5. Dip each stick in eggs, then breadcrumbs, and repeat for a double coat.
  6. Fry in two batches, flipping occasionally, until golden brown.
  7. Serve warm.

Pasta 1 Pot

Buffalo One-Pan Chicken Alfredo

Serves: 1 servings

Ingredients

  • 2 lbs chicken breast, flattened to 1/4 inch thick
  • 10 oz dry pasta, I use mafaldine
  • 6 cloves garlic, minced
  • 2 tablespoons butter
  • 3 tablespoons olive oil
  • 1 ½ cups chicken broth
  • 2 ½ cups milk, room temperature
  • ½ cup buffalo wing sauce
  • 4 oz cream cheese, cubes, room temp
  • 3 tablespoons Italian seasoning, divided
  • ¾ cup grated parmesan cheese
  • 4 green onions, sliced

Directions

  1. Season chicken with most of the Italian seasoning, reserving 1 teaspoon.
  2. Heat olive oil in a skillet over medium heat. Cook chicken for 3-4 minutes on one side, flip, reduce heat to low, and cook another 4-5 minutes until the chicken reaches 165°F.
  3. Remove and in the same skillet, melt butter and cook garlic for 1 minute. Add chicken broth, milk, buffalo sauce, and remaining seasoning. Bring to a low boil.
  4. Add pasta, submerge in the liquid, and simmer for 8-10 minutes until al dente.
  5. Stir in cream cheese until melted, then add parmesan and green onions.
  6. Top with sliced chicken and drizzle with more buffalo sauce if desired.

Notes

Drop the recipe😢

Mexican Mama Goose’s Recipes

Burrito Style Beef Enchiladas

Serves: 10 servings

An easy Burrito Style Beef Enchilada recipe that will have everyone you make them for asking for the recipe!

Ingredients

  • 1 pound ground beef browned and drained
  • 1 1 oz package taco seasoning
  • ¾ cup water
  • 1 16 oz can refried beans
  • 10 pack flour tortillas medium size, 1-10
  • 2 cans cream of chicken soup
  • 1 15.5 ounce jar of salsa
  • 2 cups milk
  • 2 cups taco shredded cheese

Directions

  1. Brown the ground beef; drain.
  2. Add taco seasoning and water.
  3. Simmer for a couple minutes.
  4. Add refried beans and mix together and take off heat.
  5. In a medium sauce pan add the sauce ingredients; cream of chicken, milk and salsa and cook until warm.
  6. Pour half of the sauce in the bottom of a greased 9 x 13 in pan.
  7. Put equal amounts of filling on each shell, about 3 tablespoons.
  8. Roll up the enchiladas and put in the dish.
  9. Cover with cheese and remaining sauce.
  10. Bake at 35o degrees for 30 minutes.

Recipe from: farmwifecooks.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

Appetizers Asian

Candied Sweet Potatoes | Goguma Mattang (고구마 맛탕)

Serves: 1 servings

How Koreans ear sweet potatoes #gogumamattang #candiedsweetpotatoes #koreanfood #easyrecipe

Ingredients

  • 4 Korean sweet potatoes
  • 4 tablespoons cooking oil, for syrup
  • 8 tablespoons white sugar, for syrup

Directions

  1. Peel the sweet potatoes and cut them into bite-sized pieces, rotating as you go.
  2. Soak the sweet potato pieces in water for about 10 minutes to remove excess starch — this helps them get crispier.
  3. Pat the sweet potatoes completely dry with a towel.
  4. Heat enough oil in a pan and fry the sweet potatoes over medium heat until fully cooked through.
  5. Drain the cooked sweet potatoes in a wire rack and let them rest for about 10 minutes.
  6. In a separate pan, spread the oil and sugar evenly and melt over low heat. Do not stir, and let it turn golden brown.
  7. While the sugar melts, fry the sweet potatoes a second time until all sides are nicely golden brown.
  8. Quickly add the fried sweet potatoes to the melted sugar and toss evenly to coat.
  9. Spread the candied sweet potatoes on parchment paper so they don’t stick together and let them cool for about 15 minutes.

Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

Pasta 1 Pot

Caramelized Onion Pasta with Chili Oil

Serves: 2 servings · Prep: 10 min · Cook: 25 min

This caramelized onion pasta is made with sweet jammy onions, garlic, chili crisp, parmesan, and a light cream sauce. A bold and comforting 30-minute dinner.

Ingredients

  • 8 oz dry thin spaghetti
  • 1 large white onion, thinly sliced
  • 1 tablespoon olive oil
  • 4 tablespoons butter, divided
  • 4 cloves garlic, pressed or minced
  • 2 tablespoon chili crisp, 1-2, plus more for serving (depending on your heat preference
  • 2 tablespoons soy sauce
  • 2 tablespoons honey
  • ⅔ cup half and half, coconut milk can be used for dairy free
  • 1 teaspoon paprika
  • ½ teaspoon garlic powder
  • ½ teaspoon Italian seasoning
  • ½ teaspoon black pepper
  • ½ cup freshly grated parmesan
  • fresh parsley, for garnish

Directions

  1. In a large skillet over medium-low heat, add olive oil and 2 tablespoons butter. Add sliced onion and cook, stirring frequently, for about 20 minutes until soft and lightly caramelized.
  2. Meanwhile, bring a large pot of salted water to a boil. Cook spaghetti until al dente. Reserve 1/2 cup pasta water, then drain.
  3. To the onions, add remaining butter and minced garlic. Cook for 1 minute until fragrant.
  4. Stir in chili crisp, soy sauce, and honey until combined.
  5. Reduce heat to low. Pour in half and half. Add paprika, garlic powder, Italian seasoning, and black pepper. Simmer 5 minutes until slightly thickened.
  6. Stir in parmesan until melted and smooth.
  7. Add cooked pasta to the skillet and toss until fully coated. Add reserved pasta water only if needed to loosen the sauce.
  8. Garnish with chili crisp oil and parsley before serving.

Notes

Cook onions slowly to avoid burning. Use freshly grated parmesan for best texture. Add pasta water gradually to adjust sauce consistency.

Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

Dinner Side Dishes

Carbonara fried rice✨

Serves: 6 serves · Prep: 5 min

My debut cookbook Maxi’s Kitchen is available for pre-order now. Link in bio 🤍.

Ingredients

  • 3 Eggs
  • 2 Egg Yolks
  • ¼ Cup Grated Pecorino Romano, plus more for serving
  • 8 oz Pancetta, Guanciale, or Bacon, diced small
  • 4 cups Cooked Jasmine Rice
  • 1 tsp Freshly Ground Black Pepper
  • Kosher Salt, to taste

Directions

  1. In a medium bowl, beat the eggs and egg yolks with the pecorino romano and a sprinkle of fresh ground pepper. Set aside.
  2. In a large wok or pan over medium high heat, add the pancetta and cook, stirring every couple of minutes, until golden brown and crispy (7-9 mins). Turn off the heat, remove the pancetta from the wok with a slotted spoon, and place it on a paper towel lined plate to drain.
  3. Whisk 1 Tbsp of the hot pancetta fat into the eggs (leaving the rest in the wok) which will temper them. Make sure to keep the eggs moving constantly while you pour in the fat.
  4. Turn the heat on the wok back to medium high and add the rice. Spread it into one layer and let it cook undisturbed for 3-4 mins until the bottom of the rice gets golden brown and crisped. Stir the rice and add the pepper. Cook while stirring for 2-3 more minutes until the rice is fully heated through.
  5. Remove the pan from the heat and immediately pour the egg mixture onto the rice while mixing rapidly. Once the eggs are evenly coating the rice, mix in the crispy pancetta.
  6. Taste and season with a little salt if desired (you may not need it though!). Serve immediately with an extra sprinkle of pecorino romano on top and enjoy!

Notes

@ciaraconlon8033

Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

Baked Goods

Cheese Stuffed Garlic Naan

Serves: 4 pieces · Prep: 90 min · Cook: 10 min

Garlic naan is already a God tier food, so I thought to myself, how do you make this even better…CHEESE! This cheese stuffed garlic naan is amazing by itself but I love eating it as an accompany to curry. It is surprisingly easy to make and doesn’t involve many ingredients at all.

Ingredients

  • Dough:
  • 2 cups AP flour
  • ½ tbsp instant yeast
  • ½ tbsp sugar
  • 1 tsp salt
  • ½ cup water
  • ½ cup full fat Greek yoghurt
  • 2 tbsp melted butter
  • Cheese:
  • 1 cup shredded mozarella
  • Garlic butter:
  • 1 stick melted butter
  • 5 cloves minced garlic
  • 1 tsp salt
  • ¼ cup chopped cilantro

Directions

  1. Mix all dough ingredients together in a bowl making sure to mix the dry ingredients first before adding the wet.
  2. Start by mixing with a butter knife then once a ball starts to form, knead on the table until the doughball becomes smooth. (about 5-10 minutes) Alternatively, you can use a stand mixer which would take half the amount of time.
  3. Allow the dough to proof in the bowl with a tsp of olive oil and covered with plastic wrap for 1 hour until double in size.
  4. After 1 hour, the dough should be doubled in size. Flatten each piece of the dough with your palm or a rolling pin so that there is enough surface area to layer on some cheese.
  5. Place a handful of cheese into the flattened piece of dough, making sure not to overcrowd it. 1 solid layer should be enough.
  6. Fold the edges of the dough together, and pinch to seal the dough together. You want to bring all the edges together towards the middle.
  7. Then roll the dough over so that the seam side is facing the table. Shape it into a tight ball, forming surface tension then flatten slightly with your hand. Let this rest to proof again for 30-45 minutes.
  8. While you wait, make the garlic butter by combining all the ingredients.
  9. Heat up a cast iron pan up to med-high heat. (non stick works as well)
  10. Using a rolling pin, roll out the dough as thin as possible. If the dough is too sticky, sprinkle some flour on the rolling pin and work surface. As you can see here, the outline of the shredded cheese is shown and what prevents the dough from breaking is the gluten formation of the dough. That is why it is important to form surface tension on the dough
  11. Place dough in preheated high heat skillet to cook for 1-2 minutes each side. If you don't start to see bubbles and color does not start to change, increase the heat.
  12. Once cooked on both sides, remove and immediately brush with the garlic butter.

Notes

If the dough is too sticky when rolling out, dust with more flour. Be patient with the dough if it feels too wet. Continue to knead and it will get easier. Play around with the temperature of the pan, I find higher heats are better because I like the charred flavour it brings.

Recipe from: kwokspots.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

      Meat 1 Pot

      Cheesy Beef & Rice

      Serves: 1 servings

      Cheesy Beef & Rice is an easy one pan dinner!

      Ingredients

      • 1 lb ground beef
      • ½ tsp each of salt and pepper
      • ¼ tsp cayenne
      • 1 tsp each of cumin, dried cilantro, and chili powder
      • 1 TBSP of minced garlic
      • 8 oz can of tomato sauce
      • 2 cups of beef broth
      • 1 cup of long grain white rice, uncooked & rinsed
      • 1 tbsp olive oil
      • 1 White Queso Cheese Dip
      • 1 Flour tortillas
      • 1 Pico de gallo
      • 1 Fresh cilantro

      Directions

      1. Brown 1 lb of ground beef in a skillet over medium-high heat. Season with salt, pepper, cayenne, cumin, dried cilantro, black pepper, and chili powder.
      2. Let that cook until the beef is cooked through and drain any extra grease. Move the ground beef to the edges of the skillet and pour about 1 tbsp of olive oil into the skillet. Add uncooked, rinsed rice and stir around for a couple of minutes.
      3. Add tomato sauce, minced garlic, and beef broth. Stir well, cover with a lid, and cook on medium-low heat for about 20 minutes or until the rice is cooked. Once the rice is cooked use a fork to fluff it up, turn off the heat, and let sit covered for about 5 minutes.
      4. Microwave queso and pour some over the beef and rice. Top with fresh cilantro. Serve as is or in tortillas with fresh pico de gallo. Enjoy!

      Notes

      @cooking_with_fire___ appreciate it!

      Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

      Healthy Mama Goose’s Recipes Casserole

      Cheesy Beef & Zucchini Casserole

      Serves: 8 servings · Prep: 10 min · Cook: 40 min

      I have no idea why I made a casserole when it was 100°F in Los Angeles yesterday! Anyway, it was really good and easy to make.

      Ingredients

      • 4 cups shredded unpeeled zucchini
      • ½ tsp salt
      • 2 eggs
      • ½ cup grated Parmesan cheese
      • 2 cups shredded part-skim mozzarella cheese, divided, 8 oz
      • 1 cup shredded cheddar cheese, divided, 4 oz
      • 1 pound ground beef
      • ½ cup onion, chopped
      • 1 medium green pepper, chopped
      • 1 can Italian tomato sauce, 15 oz

      Directions

      1. Place zucchini in strainer; sprinkle with salt. Let stand for 10 minutes. Squeeze out moisture.
      2. Combine zucchini with the eggs, Parmesan and half of the mozzarella and cheddar cheeses. Press into greased 13-in. x 9-in. baking dish.
      3. Bake, uncovered, at 400° for 20 minutes.
      4. Meanwhile, cook beef, onion and green pepper over medium heat until meat is no longer pink; drain. Add tomato sauce; spoon over zucchini mixture.
      5. Sprinkle with remaining cheeses. Bake 20 minutes longer or until heated through.

      Recipe from: leslieblythemiller.wordpress.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

      Pasta 1 Pot

      Cheesy Chicken Taco Mac

      Serves: 1 servings

      Cheesy Chicken Taco Mac is an easy one pan dinner!

      Ingredients

      • 1 lb of chicken breast, cut into cubes
      • 1 tbsp oil
      • 1 onion, chopped
      • ½ tsp salt and pepper
      • 2 tsp chili powder
      • 1 tsp each of cumin and dried cilantro
      • 1 ½ cups of chicken broth
      • 2 ½ cups of elbow macaroni
      • 10 oz can of Rotel, not drained
      • 8 oz can tomato sauce
      • 1 tbsp minced garlic
      • 2 cups of shredded Colby Jack cheese
      • ½ cup of milk
      • 1 Fresh cilantro

      Directions

      1. Heat a large skillet over medium-heat. Add 1 tbsp of oil, the chicken, and a chopped onion.
      2. Season with salt, pepper, chili powder, cumin, and dried cilantro.
      3. Once the chicken is pretty much cooked through, add the minced garlic, elbow macaroni, chicken broth, Rotel, and a can of tomato sauce.
      4. Stir everything together well and bring to a quick boil.
      5. Once you see bubbles, turn the heat down to medium low. Cover with a lid and cook for about 12 minutes or until the pasta is cooked. Stir at least once about half way through to keep the pasta from sticking to the skillet.
      6. Finally, add the shredded cheese and milk, stir, and let sit until the cheese is melted. Serve and top with fresh cilantro. Enjoy!

      Notes

      So good🥹

      Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

      Mexican 1 Pot

      Cheesy Chicken Taco Rice

      Serves: 1 servings · Cook: 15 min

      Cheesy Chicken Taco Rice is an easy one pan meal my family loves!

      Ingredients

      • 1 lb of chicken breast, cut into cubes
      • 1 tbsp olive oil
      • ½ tsp each salt and pepper
      • 2 tsp chili powder
      • 1 tsp each of cumin and dried cilantro
      • 1 tbsp minced garlic
      • 10 oz can of Rotel, not drained
      • 8 oz can of tomato sauce
      • 1 ½ cups of chicken broth
      • 1 cup of long grain white rice, uncooked & rinsed
      • 2 cups of shredded Colby Jack cheese
      • 1 Sour cream
      • 1 Cilantro
      • 1 Buffalo Wild Wing’s Southwest Ranch

      Directions

      1. Add the chicken with 1 tbsp of oil to a large skillet over medium-high heat, add all seasonings, and cook until the chicken is cooked through.
      2. Add the untrained Rotel, tomato sauce, minced garlic, chicken broth, and uncooked rice to the skillet. Stir well, make sure to bring the whole skillet to a boil, cover with a lid, and cook on medium-low heat for about 15-20 minutes or until rice is cooked (I stirred it once about half way through).
      3. Top with shredded Colby Jack cheese and let it melt or pop in the oven on broil. Serve and top with sour cream, cilantro, and Southwest Ranch sauce. Serve with tortilla chips. Enjoy!

      Mexican Mama Goose’s Recipes Casserole

      Cheesy Enchilada Rice Skillet

      Serves: 4 servings · Prep: 10 min · Cook: 20 min

      The easiest enchilada you will ever make. No rolling, no folding. Just throw everything into a skillet and you’re set!

      Ingredients

      • 1 cup uncooked rice
      • 1 tablespoon olive oil
      • 2 cloves garlic, minced
      • 1 small onion, diced
      • 1 bell pepper, diced
      • 1 cup canned corn kernels, drained
      • 1 cup canned black beans, drained and rinsed
      • ¾ cup Old El Paso™ mild enchilada sauce
      • ½ cup Old El Paso™ mild green enchilada sauce
      • ½ teaspoon chili powder
      • ¼ teaspoon cumin
      • ¼ teaspoon oregano
      • Kosher salt and freshly ground black pepper, to taste
      • 1 cup shredded Mexican blend cheese
      • 2 tablespoons chopped fresh cilantro leaves

      Directions

      1. In a large saucepan of 1 1 /2 cups water, cook rice according to package instructions; set aside.
      2. Heat olive oil in a large skillet over medium high heat. Add garlic, onion and bell pepper, and cook, stirring frequently, until onions have become translucent, about 2-3 minutes.
      3. Stir in rice, corn, black beans, enchilada sauces, chili powder, cumin and oregano until well combined and heated through, about 2-3 minutes; season with salt and pepper, to taste.
      4. Remove from heat and top with cheese. Cover until cheese has melted, about 2 minutes.
      5. Serve immediately, garnished with cilantro.

      Recipe from: damndelicious.net, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

      Mama Goose’s Recipes Casserole

      Cheesy Ground Beef and Rice Casserole

      Serves: 8 servings · Prep: 5 min · Cook: 90 min

      Picky eaters are going to love this Cheesy Ground Beef and Rice Casserole. Mom called it busy day casserole because it’s a quick and easy dinner.

      Ingredients

      • Quick recipe:
      • 1 Can Cream of Mushroom Soup
      • 1 Cup Long Grain White Rice, uncooked
      • 2 cups Water
      • 1 Cup Carrots, sliced
      • ½ 1 Pound Ground Beef, cooked
      • 1 Cheddar or Colby Jack Cheese, sliced about 9 pieces
      • Semi homemade:
      • 1 Cup Long Grain White Rice, uncooked
      • 2 cups Water
      • ½ to 1 Pound Ground Beef, cooked, depending on preference
      • 1 Cup Carrots, sliced
      • 1 Pacific Foods Mushroom Gravy, 13.9 ounces
      • ½ Cup Milk
      • 1 Colby Jack or Medium Cheddar Cheese, for topping
      • From scratch:
      • 3 tablespoons Unsalted Butter
      • ¼ Cup Onion, minced
      • 1 Clove Garlic, minced
      • ½ Cup White Button Mushroom, minced
      • 3 tablespoons Flour
      • ½ Cup Cream
      • ½ Cup Chicken Broth
      • ¼ teaspoon Freshly Ground Black Pepper
      • Salt, to taste
      • 2 cups Water
      • ½ 1 Pound Ground Beef, cooked
      • 1 Cup Carrots, sliced
      • 1 Cheddar or Colby Jack Cheese, sliced, about 9 slices
      • 1 Cup Long Grain White Rice, uncooked

      Directions

      1. Quick recipe:
      2. Heat the oven to 350 degrees and spray an 8×9" baking dish with nonstick spray.
      3. Stir together the soup, rice, water, carrots, and beef.
      4. Place in the baking dish and bake for 90 minutes.
      5. Top with cheese slices and bake a few more minutes or until melted.
      6. Semi homemade:
      7. Heat the oven to 350 degrees and grease an 8×9″ baking dish.
      8. Whisk together the water, mushroom gravy, and milk.
      9. Add the mushroom mix, rice, cooked beef and carrots to a bowl and stir to combine.
      10. Dump into an 8×9″ baking dish and bake, uncovered for 90 minutes.
      11. Add slices of cheese and return to the oven for another couple of minutes or until the cheese is melted.
      12. From scratch:
      13. In a medium pan over medium heat, add the butter and melt.
      14. Whisk in the onion, garlic and mushrooms.
      15. Add the flour, whisking continually and cook for 30-60 seconds.
      16. Add the cream and broth and cook until thick.
      17. Season and remove from heat.
      18. Heat the oven to 350 and spray an 8×9″ baking dish with nonstick spray.
      19. Place the cream mixture, rice, water, beef and carrots into the dish and stir to combine.
      20. Bake for 90 minutes, add the cheese and bake for a few more minutes or until melted.

      Notes

      May be reheated in the oven or microwave. *The nutritional information is based on the from scratch version of the recipe.

      Nutrition (per serving)

      Serving Size: 1 · Calories: 208 · Fat: 15 · Saturated fat: 8 · Trans fat: 1 · Carbs: 11 · Sugar: 2 · Fiber: 1 · Protein: 7 · Sodium: 93 · Cholesterol: 49

      Recipe from: ohsweetbasil.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

      Pasta

      Cheesy Mexican Honey Beef Pasta

      Serves: 4 servings

      Ingredients

      • 300 g pasta, uncooked weight
      • 500 g beef mince
      • 1 onion, finely diced
      • 1 tbs garlic powder
      • 1 tbs smoked paprika
      • 2 tbs Mexican seasoning - I used fajita, 1-2
      • 3 tsp garlic purée - or 3 cloves
      • 2 tbs tomato puree
      • 1 tbs Honey
      • 300 ml chicken or beef stock
      • 120 ml single cream - I used elmlea light
      • 60 g mozzarella
      • Salt and pepper to taste
      • Parsley and chilli flakes to garnish, optional

      Directions

      1. Fry off the beef mince until it’s starting to brown and then add in the seasoning.
      2. Meanwhile pop your pasta on to boil in salted water.
      3. Once the beef has browned add in the onions and cook until soft.
      4. Then add in the garlic, tomato puree and honey and cook for a few minutes mixing well.
      5. Add in the stock and cream and cook until the sauce has thickened.
      6. Add in the pasta and the mozzarella and season to taste.
      7. Garnish.
      8. Serve and enjoy.

      Cheesy Sausage Wonton Cups

      Serves: 1 servings

      Cheesy Sausage Wonton Cups😮 💨

      Ingredients

      • 1 lb of original sausage
      • 12 oz wonton wraps
      • 8 oz Mexican blend shredded cheese
      • ¾ cup chopped red and green bell peppers
      • ½ cup ranch dressing

      Directions

      1. Heat a skillet over medium-high heat. Brown 1 lb of sausage and drain any extra grease.

      Dinner

      Cheesy Taco Potato Skillet

      Serves: 1 servings

      Cheesy Taco Potato Skillet😮 💨

      Ingredients

      • 5 small/medium potatoes
      • 1 olive oil + salt, pepper, garlic powder, and paprika, for potatoes
      • 1 lb ground beef
      • ½ tsp salt and pepper
      • ¼ tsp cayenne
      • 2 tsp chili powder
      • 1 tsp each of cumin, paprika, and dried cilantro
      • 1 tbsp minced garlic
      • 1 Shredded cheddar cheese
      • 1 ½ cups white Queso
      • 1 Sour cream
      • 1 Cilantro

      Directions

      1. Peel, wash potatoes, and cut up into cubes.
      2. Toss with olive oil, salt, pepper, garlic powder, and paprika (I don’t use exact measurements for this part).
      3. Cook potatoes in the air fryer at 400 degrees for about 20-25 minutes shaking them around at least a couple times while they cook.
      4. Heat a skillet over medium high heat.
      5. Add the ground beef to the skillet and cook until browned. Drain any extra grease. Season with salt, cayenne, chili powder, cumin, paprika, dried cilantro, and black pepper. Add minced garlic and stir well.
      6. Once the potatoes and ground beef are cooked combine them together in the skillet and top with shredded cheddar cheese. Put the skillet in the oven at 400 degrees F to melt the cheese.
      7. Pour warmed up Queso over them. Top with sour cream and cilantro. This is also good wrapped up in tortillas as burritos!

      Notes

      😍😍😍
