      Mexican

      Cheesy Taco Sticks

      Serves: 8 sticks · Prep: 10 min · Cook: 20 min

      Cheesy Taco Sticks combine the bold flavors of tacos with the irresistible pull of melted cheese, all wrapped in a crispy, golden breadstick. This fun twist on tacos makes for a perfect snack, appetizer, or even a light meal. With seasoned beef, two kinds of gooey cheese, and a crisp pizza dough crust, these taco sticks are sure to be a hit at your next gathering. Serve them alongside your favorite dips, like salsa and sour cream, for an extra layer of flavor. Whether you’re hosting a game night or just want a cheesy, savory treat, these Cheesy Taco Sticks will satisfy any craving. They’re quick, easy, and bound to impress!

      Ingredients

      • 1 can refrigerated pizza dough
      • ½ pound ground beef
      • 1 packet taco seasoning
      • 1 cup shredded cheddar cheese
      • 1 cup shredded mozzarella cheese
      • 1 tablespoon butter, melted
      • ¼ teaspoon garlic powder
      • 1 Salsa and sour cream for dipping

      Directions

      1. Preheat oven to 375°F (190°C).
      2. In a skillet, cook ground beef over medium heat until browned. Drain any excess fat, then stir in taco seasoning according to the packet instructions. Set aside.
      3. Roll out the pizza dough on a floured surface into a large rectangle.
      4. Spread the cooked taco meat evenly over one half of the dough. Sprinkle the shredded cheddar and mozzarella cheese on top of the meat.
      5. Fold the other half of the dough over the filling and press the edges to seal.
      6. Cut the stuffed dough into strips about 1 inch wide. Twist each strip and place on a parchment-lined baking sheet.
      7. Brush the tops of the twisted sticks with melted butter and sprinkle with garlic powder.
      8. Bake for 15–20 minutes, or until golden brown and crispy.
      9. Serve with salsa and sour cream for dipping.

      Nutrition (per serving)

      Calories: 320

      Recipe from: medium.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

      Baked Goods Cookies

      Chewy Oatmeal Lace Cookies

      Serves: 60 servings · Prep: 10 min · Cook: 6 min

      These chewy oatmeal lace cookies are super easy to make and are one of my favorite cookie recipes! They're perfectly caramelized so they have crispy golden brown edges and a chewy center.

      Ingredients

      • Chewy oatmeal lace cookies:
      • ½ cup unsalted butter, room temperature 113g, 1 stick
      • 1 cup packed light brown sugar 200 g
      • 1 cup old fashioned oats 96 g
      • 1 Tbsp + 1 tsp all-purpose flour 12 g
      • ½ tsp fine salt 3 g
      • 1 large egg, room temperature 56g
      • 1 tsp vanilla extract or vanilla bean paste 4 g

      Directions

      1. Preheat the oven to 375ºF/ 190ºC and line 4 large baking sheets with silicone baking mats or parchment paper. Set aside. If you don't have that many baking sheets, line however many you have.
      2. Add 1/2 cup of butter and 1 cup of packed brown sugar in a large frying pan and heat over medium heat. Stir frequently until the butter has fully melted and the mixture is smooth and no clumps of brown sugar remain. It's ok if the butter and brown sugar aren't fully combined. The mixture will come together once we mix in the remaining ingredients. Remove from heat.
      3. Stir in 1 cup of oats, 1 Tbsp + 1 tsp flour, and 1/2 tsp salt. Mix until the oats are fully covered with the brown sugar/butter mixture.
      4. Then mix in 1 large egg and 1 tsp of vanilla extract. Stir until fully incorporated. The batter should be a little soupy/thin!
      5. Drop 1 tsp of cookie batter onto the prepared baking sheets to make each cookie. Leave at least 2 inches between each cookie to allow them to spread.
      6. Bake two trays at a time for 5-7 minutes (the sweet spot in my oven is 6 1/2 minutes, but it will vary from oven to oven), until the edges are just starting to brown. If you like a chewier cookie, you can pull them out before the edges have browned much. If you like crispier edges, make sure the edges are nice and golden brown before pulling them out of the oven.
      7. As soon as the two trays are done, pop in the next round. Keep a close eye on these, as the bake time can also vary based on how much batter you use to make each cookie and how warm the batter is when the cookies are baked.
      8. If the cookies come out of the oven and aren't as circular as you'd like, use a large circle cutter or a large glass to scoot the sides of the cookies and reshape them into a circle while they're still warm.
      9. Let the cookies cool on the sheets for 15 minutes, then move to wire racks to cool completely. Drop more cookie batter onto the cleared cookie sheets and repeat steps 5 & 6 until you've used all your batter.
      10. If you don't plan to eat these cookies immediately, keep them out at room temperature in an airtight container for up to 5 days. Baked cookies can also be frozen in an airtight container or freezer ziplock bag for up to a month.

      Notes

      How Many Cookies Does This Recipe Make? This recipe makes about 60 small cookies, but the yield will vary based on how much batter you use per cookie. If you want to make larger cookies, I recommend using 2 tsp of batter per cookie and making sure the cookies are at least 3 inches apart on the baking sheet before being baked. Bake time will be a couple of minutes longer. How to Make the Best Chewy Oatmeal Lace Cookies Leave about 2 inches between each cookie as you spoon out the batter. They spread out quite a bit while they bake. Use a rounded teaspoon of batter to make each cookie. This will help you make chewy cookies with golden brown, caramelized edges. Make sure the cookie mixture isn’t too hot when you add in the egg. You don’t want the heat from the dough to scramble the egg! If the cookies come out of the oven and aren’t as circular as you’d like, use a large circle cutter or a large glass to scoot the sides of the cookies and reshape them into a circle. Let the cookies cool for at least 15 minutes on the pan before you try to move them to a wire rack. Making These Oatmeal Lace Cookies in Advance These cookies can be made in advance! Store them at room temperature in an airtight container for up to 5 days. Baked cookies can also be frozen in an airtight container or freezer ziplock bag for up to a month.

      Nutrition (per serving)

      Serving Size: 1 · Calories: 32 · Fat: 2 · Saturated fat: 1 · Carbs: 4 · Sugar: 3 · Sodium: 22 · Cholesterol: 7

      Recipe from: chelsweets.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

      Appetizers Chicken

      Chick-fil-A Nuggets Dupe

      Serves: 1 servings

      I worked at Chick fil A for way too long, and I probably made an unreal amount of nuggets while I was there, so let me show you how to make them at home. It is easier than you think, way cheaper, and honestly hits that same classic flavor when you do it right. The biggest key is the pickle juice marinade. Cut your chicken into bite sized pieces, let it sit in pickle juice for at least thirty minutes, and you are already halfway to that signature Chick fil A taste. From there it is a simple two step breading. A milk and egg wash, then a seasoned flour mixture with salt, paprika, black pepper, and the real secret, a little sugar. That tiny bit of sweetness is what makes the coating taste like the real thing. Fry the nuggets in peanut oil at 350 until golden and cooked through, then let them drain for a minute before serving. No pressure fryer required, just good technique and the right flavor combo. Now you can make Chick fil A nuggets at home any day of the week, even on Sunday, enjoyyy! ❤️

      Ingredients

      • 4 chicken breasts
      • 1 cup dill pickle juice
      • 2 large eggs
      • 1 cup whole milk
      • 1 ½ cups all-purpose flour
      • 2 teaspoons white granulated sugar
      • 2 teaspoons paprika
      • 1 teaspoon ground black pepper
      • 2 teaspoons kosher salt
      • 8 cups peanut oil
      • 1 Salt, for topping

      Directions

      1. Cut the chicken breasts into 1-inch chunks. Place in a medium bowl and pour the dill pickle juice over the chicken. Cover and refrigerate for at least 30 minutes and up to 2 hours.
      2. In a mixing bowl, whisk together the eggs, whole milk, and 1 tablespoon of the dill pickle marinade. This is your egg wash.
      3. In a separate bowl, whisk together the all-purpose flour, paprika, black pepper, kosher salt, and sugar.
      4. Pour the peanut oil into a large heavy-bottom pot and heat over medium to medium-high until the oil reaches 350°F. A digital thermometer is highly recommended.
      5. When the chicken has finished marinating, drain it and transfer it to the egg wash. Toss to fully coat.
      6. Remove the chicken from the egg wash, allowing excess liquid to drip off, then transfer to the flour mixture. Toss with your fingers until evenly coated.
      7. Shake off excess flour from each piece.
      8. Carefully lower the chicken into the 350°F oil in batches. Fry for 5–7 minutes, or until the internal temperature reaches 160°F.
      9. Remove the cooked nuggets and place on a wire rack or paper towels. Lightly sprinkle with kosher salt while hot.
      10. Serve hot with your favorite dipping sauce.

      Notes

      Do not coat the chicken in the flour mixture (next step) unless your oil is fully preheated. Letting the coated chicken sit too long before frying can cause the breading to clump or fall off. Chicken is safe at 165°F. Removing at 160°F allows carryover cooking to bring it to temperature while keeping the nuggets juicy. These nuggets reheat really well in the air fryer or the oven; I often make large batches ahead of time and reheat them as needed. The nuggets will stay fresh for 3 days in an airtight container or Ziploc bag.

      Recipe from: dishwithdrew.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

      Soup

      Chicken & Dumplings

      Serves: 1 servings

      Ingredients

      • 1 chopped onion
      • 3 chopped carrots
      • 4 chopped celery stalks
      • 1 Tbsp minced fresh rosemary
      • 3 Tbsp olive oil
      • 8 cloves minced garlic
      • ¼ tsp each salt & pepper
      • 4 Tbsp unsalted butter
      • 4 Tbsp all-purpose flour
      • 12 oz evaporated milk
      • 6 cups chicken broth
      • 3 stalks fresh rosemary
      • 2 cooked, shredded chicken breasts
      • 1 tsp each bouillon, pepper, and paprika
      • 8 Pillsbury butter biscuits, quartered

      Directions

      1. Heat the olive oil in a large saucepan over medium heat. Add the minced rosemary and stir for 30 seconds before adding the onions, carrots, and celery.
      2. Once the onions are translucent, add the garlic, salt, and pepper. Cook and stir until garlic is fragrant.
      3. Add in the butter. Once melted, stir in the flour. Keep stirring until the flour turns golden brown and the mixture has thickened.
      4. Slowly pour in the evaporated milk and stir to combine. Once thickened, pour in the chicken broth. Add the chicken, seasonings, and rosemary stalks. Cover and simmer for 10 minutes.
      5. Remove the rosemary stalks and add in the biscuits. Cover and simmer for 10 minutes or until biscuits are fully cooked.

        Pasta Mama Goose’s Recipes

        Chicken Alfredo Bake

        Serves: 8 people · Prep: 35 min · Cook: 30 min

        Cheesy Chicken Alfredo Bake is a simple twist on a family favorite! It’s an easy dinner, perfect any night of the week.

        Ingredients

        • 12 oz pkg penne pasta
        • 3 boneless skinless chicken breasts marinated in Italian dressing if desired
        • 1 vegetable oil
        • ¼ tsp thyme
        • ¼ tsp poultry seasoning
        • salt and pepper to taste
        • 2 TB olive oil
        • ½ c butter
        • 2 tsp minced garlic
        • 4 TB all-purpose flour
        • 3 c half and half
        • 3 c milk
        • 3 c Kraft Italian Five Cheese Blend divided
        • Italian Seasoning, garnish

        Directions

        1. If time allows, always try to marinate your chicken. Throw them in a bag of Italian dressing and let sit overnight or even a few hours will help your chicken to be tender and full of flavor.
        2. Cook pasta as directed. Drain and set aside.
        3. While pasta is cooking, heat 1 – 2 TB of oil in a skillet on medium heat.
        4. Slice your marinated chicken in half so they are thinner pieces. This will help them cook through evenly.
        5. In a bowl mix salt, pepper, thyme and poultry seasoning and rub it on all sides of your chicken breasts. Add chicken to hot oil and cook on both sides until done (about 15-20 minutes). Remove to a plate to let cool.
        6. While the chicken is cooling, preheat your oven to 350°F and start on your sauce. In a separate large skillet melt the butter; add garlic and cook for 1 – 2 minutes.
        7. Using a wire whisk, mix in flour and stir until bubbly. Mix in half & half and milk, stir to combine. Bring to a boil and then reduce to low heat and simmer for 5 minutes.
        8. With your whisk, mix in 1½ cup of cheese. Cook an additional 5 minutes until sauce starts to thicken. Remove sauce from heat.
        9. Cut your cooked chicken into bite size pieces and add to a large bowl. Add the pasta to your bowl and then the sauce. Mix everything to combine.
        10. Spray a 9×13 baking pan with non stick spray. Add the pasta mixture to the pan and sprinkle with an additional 1½ cup of cheese.
        11. Sprinkle with pepper and bake for 30 minutes or until bubbly and golden brown.

        Nutrition (per serving)

        Calories: 588 · Fat: 44 · Saturated fat: 25 · Carbs: 11 · Sugar: 5 · Fiber: 0.9 · Protein: 34 · Sodium: 539 · Cholesterol: 171

        Recipe from: lilluna.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

        Chicken Alfredo Lasagna Rolls

        Serves: 1 servings

        Indulge in these heavenly Chicken Alfredo Lasagna Rolls! Perfectly creamy, cheesy, and packed with flavor, they’re sure to be a hit at your dinner table. Give them a try and let the compliments roll in! 😋🍽️

        Ingredients

        • For the alfredo sauce:
        • ¼ cup butter, cut into large cubes
        • 1 ½ cups heavy cream
        • Pinch of nutmeg
        • Pinch of crushed red pepper flakes
        • 1 ½ cups freshly grated Parmigiano-Reggiano cheese
        • For the lasagna rolls:
        • 9 uncooked/dry lasagna noodles
        • 1 cup ricotta cheese
        • 1 large egg
        • 1 ½ cups shredded mozzarella, divided
        • 2 cups cooked, shredded rotisserie chicken
        • Fresh chopped parsley, for garnish

        Directions

        1. Spread 1/9th of the mixture (about 1/3 cup each) onto the length of each lasagna noodle and roll up in a jelly-roll fashion.
        2. Arrange rolls, seam side down, in the baking dish. Top with remaining 1 cup of Alfredo sauce, and remaining 1/2 cup mozzarella cheese.
        3. Cover loosely with foil and bake for about 25 minutes or until cheese has melted and the inside is warmed through.
        4. Sprinkle with some fresh chopped parsley, serve and enjoy!

        Notes

          Soup Mama Goose’s Recipes

          Chicken Pot Pie Soup

          Serves: 10 servings · Prep: 20 min · Cook: 15 min

          This Chicken Pot Pie Soup recipe is simple to make, lightened up with a few easy tweaks, and deliciously rich and creamy.

          Ingredients

          • 4 tablespoons butter or olive oil
          • 1 medium onion, peeled and diced
          • 2 medium carrots, peeled and diced
          • 2 celery stalks, diced
          • 4 cloves garlic, peeled and minced
          • 4 ounces button or baby bella mushrooms, diced
          • ⅓ cup all-purpose or white whole wheat flour
          • 3 cups chicken stock
          • 2 cups milk
          • 3 cups shredded cooked chicken
          • 1 pound Yukon gold potatoes, diced
          • 1 cup frozen corn
          • 1 cup frozen peas
          • 1 teaspoon Italian seasoning, homemade or store-bought
          • ½ teaspoon salt
          • ½ teaspoon freshly-cracked black pepper

          Directions

          1. Heat butter in a large stockpot over medium-high heat until melted (or heat the olive oil until it is shimmering). Add onion, carrots, and celery. Saute for 6-7 minutes, stirring frequently, until the onion is soft and translucent. Add in the garlic and mushrooms, and saute for an additional 2-3 minutes, stirring frequently, until the garlic is fragrant.
          2. Stir in the flour until it is completely combined, and saute for an additional 1 minute, stirring occasionally. Gradually add in the chicken stock and milk, stirring frequently. Stir in the chicken, potatoes, corn, peas, Italian seasoning, salt and pepper until combined.
          3. Bring the mixture to a simmer (be careful not to bring it to a boil!), stirring occasionally so that the bottom of the soup does not stick to the pan. Then reduce heat to medium-low, and continue simmering the soup for 10-15 minutes until the potatoes are soft and cooked through.
          4. Taste the soup and season with additional salt and pepper, if needed.
          5. Serve immediately, with crackers on top or on the side, if desired.

          Recipe from: gimmesomeoven.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

          CHICKEN TERIYAKI CRISPY RICE

          Serves: 1 servings

          CHICKEN TERIYAKI CRISPY RICE ✨my OG viral recipe✨ If you don’t like or eat seafood, this ones for you! This was my take on *spicy tuna crispy rice* that you can order from sushi restaurants. enjoy y’all ! 😉

          Ingredients

          • Chicken teriyaki topping:
          • 2 cups Chicken, chopped/cooked
          • 8 Teriyaki sauce, 6-8
          • 4 tbsp Whipped Cream cheese, 3-4
          • 2 Scallions, chopped
          • 1 tbsp Spicy Mayo
          • Crispy rice:
          • 1 ¼ cup Sushi Rice, dry
          • 1 ½ tbsp Rice vinegar
          • 1 ½ tsp Sugar or *monkfruit
          • Pinch of salt
          • 1 High heat cooking oil

          Directions

          1. Chicken teriyaki topping:
          2. Add everything to a mixing bowl (starting out with less teriyaki sauce and cream cheese at first, only add in the rest later if needed) and stir until combined. *Note* Adjust teriyaki sauce, based off of the one you are using cause depending on which one/ the consistency differs* I used homemade. Some are thicker than others so please keep that in mind!
          3. Crispy rice:
          4. Cook rice according to your sushi rice package.
          5. Place in a pan *I used a 10 x6 dish, but 8x8 would work great!
          6. Line pan w/ plastic wrap or parchment paper.
          7. Once rice has cooled a bit, evenly place it in the pan. Press the rice down to evenly distribute it around the edges of the pan.
          8. Place in the fridge overnight.
          9. Carefully cut rice into squares, cleaning your knife with a wet paper towel each time.
          10. Fry the rice in oil on medium-high heat for about 3-4 minutes, keeping an eye on it until it’s golden, it can burn quickly so again only until golden. Take out carefully and place on a paper towel.
          11. Top with the mixture. Enjoy!
          12. To the cooked rice:
          13. Add in sugar, rice vinegar, salt and stir.

          Appetizers Asian

          Chicken Teriyaki Crispy Rice Cups

          Serves: 1 servings

          Because why not make everything hand-held

          Ingredients

          • 1 Teriyaki Sauce
          • 1 ½ t Corn Starch
          • 2 T Rice Vinegar
          • 3 T Water
          • 1 Clove Garlic, minced
          • 1 t Fresh Ginger, minced
          • 6 T Soy Sauce
          • 2 T Honey
          • 2 T Brown Sugar
          • 1 t Sesame Oil
          • 1 Other Ingredients
          • 16 oz Sushi Rice, cooked
          • 1 0.25 lbs Chicken Breasts, diced into 0.25-0.5 chunks
          • 1 Spicy Mayo
          • 1 Sesame Seeds
          • 1 Scallions, chopped
          • 1 Avocado Oil / Avocado Oil spray

          Directions

          1. Combine all sauce ingredients, stir well
          2. Add about 2 T avocado oil or spray to sauté pan. Add chicken and cook over medium/high heat for about 10-12 min, until chicken is mostly cooked.
          3. Add sauce, stirring to combine. Cook for about 5 min, until sauce thickens & becomes shiny. Set aside.
          4. Coat muffin tin with avocado oil. (Highly recommend nonstick muffin tin for this recipe)
          5. Scoop equal amounts of rice into each compartment about 3/4 way full. Use a 1/4 measuring cup to smooth and even the top of the rice. Center the measuring cup on top of the rice & press down to create cup. The rice should just reach the top. Freeze for about 45 min. Remove from freezer, spray rice with avocado oil. Bake on 400 for 20 min.
          6. Remove the muffin tray & scoop equal amounts of chicken into each vessel. Bake for another 10 min. Remove from oven & let set for 10/15 min.
          7. Use an offset spatula to release any sides that may be stuck - if you use a nonstick, it will slide out easily. Carefully slide the offset spatula under the cup & lift.
          8. Garnish with spicy mayo, sesame seeds & scallions.

          Recipe from: seriousfoodfetish.substack.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

          Mama Goose’s Recipes Casserole

          Chicken-Pecan Quiche

          Serves: 8 servings

          This recipe was inspired by one I found on MyRecipes.com. The crust is what really makes this quiche unique. It's reminiscent of Southern cheese wafers or cheese straws and provides a nice, crunchy foil to the creamy chicken filling. The filling doesn’t use as many eggs or cheese as a typical quiche does, so it turns out a little looser in texture than a traditional custard–style one. To give your filling more eye-appeal, use orange-colored cheese (instead of white cheddar like I used) and add lots of fresh herbs.

          Ingredients

          • 1 cup all-purpose flour
          • 1 cup sharp cheddar cheese, grated
          • ¾ cup pecans, chopped
          • ½ teaspoon salt
          • ¼ teaspoon paprika
          • ⅓ cup olive oil
          • 1 cup sour cream
          • ½ cup chicken broth
          • ¼ cup mayonaise
          • 3 large eggs, lightly beaten
          • 2 cup cooked chicken, finely chopped
          • ½ cup sharp cheddar cheese, grated
          • ¼ cup white onion, minced
          • ¼ cup scallion, finely chopped
          • 2 tablespoon chives, chopped, or other fresh herbs
          • 4 drop tabasco or other hot sauce, 3-4
          • 2 tablespoon freshly grated parmesan, 1-2
          • ¼ cup pecan halves

          Directions

          1. Combine first 5 ingredients in a medium bowl; stir well. Add oil; stir well. Firmly press mixture in bottom and up sides of a 9-inch deep-dish pie plate. Bake at 350° for 12 minutes. Cool completely.
          2. Combine sour cream and next three ingredients; stir with a wire whisk until smooth.
          3. Stir in chicken and next 5 ingredients.
          4. Pour chicken mixture over prepared crust. Sprinkle a layer of grated parmesan cheese on top of chicken filling.
          5. Arrange the pecan halves in radial lines on top of the parmesan-topped filling.
          6. Bake at 350° F for 55 minutes or until set. Let stand 10 minutes before serving.

          Recipe from: food52.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

          Pasta Mama Goose’s Recipes

          Chili Mac and Cheese Recipe (30 Minute Dinner)

          Serves: 6 servings · Prep: 20 min · Cook: 10 min

          This Chili Mac and Cheese recipe is your new favorite 30 minute dinner! It is so easy to put together and calls for pantry items you always have on hand.

          Ingredients

          • 1 pound ground beef
          • 1 medium onion, chopped
          • 2 teaspoons garlic, minced
          • 2 tablespoons flour
          • 1 teaspoon salt
          • ½ teaspoon paprika
          • ½ teaspoon smoked paprika
          • 1 teaspoon oregano
          • 1 ½ teaspoons cumin
          • 2 ½ teaspoons chili powder
          • 4 cups beef broth *
          • 1 can Rotel **, 10-oz, diced tomatoes and green chilies
          • 1 can tomato sauce, 8-oz
          • 1 can drained beans, any kind, I like white or kidney
          • 2 cups dry elbow macaroni
          • 8 ounces Velveeta, OR 8 ounces freshly shredded sharp cheddar cheese
          • salt and pepper to taste
          • To garnish:
          • 1 tortilla chips
          • 1 sour cream
          • 1 shredded cheese
          • 1 chopped onions
          • 1 avocado

          Directions

          1. Heat a large pot over medium heat and add the ground beef, breaking it up with a wooden spoon. Add the chopped onion and continue cooking for 5-8 minutes, until the ground beef is no longer pink and the onion is translucent.
          2. Add the minced garlic and cook for 1 minute until fragrant.
          3. Drain the meat. (I usually do this by setting a colander over a bowl.) Return the beef to the pot.
          4. Turn the heat on to medium again. Add the flour, salt, paprika, smoked paprika, oregano, cumin, and chili powder. Stir it together and cook for one minute.
          5. Add the beef broth, Rotel, tomato sauce, and drained beans. (Don’t drain the Rotel, add all those juices in.) Turn the heat up to high and bring to a boil.
          6. Add the elbow macaroni. If you are using Velveeta, roughly chop it and add it in along with the noodles. Stir occasionally for about 8 minutes until the noodles are al dente and the Velveeta has melted.
          7. If you are using shredded cheddar cheese, add it after the noodles are al dente. Add the cheese and turn off the heat. Stir until melted.
          8. Chili mac is nothing without the garnishes:
          9. crushed tortilla chips, sour cream, shredded cheddar cheese, chopped white onions, avocado…the sky is the limit here.

          Notes

          *I love to use Better Than Bouillon Beef Base. 4 teaspoons beef base + 4 cups water = 4 cups beef broth. It adds so much flavor to soups. ** Rotel is diced tomatoes with green chilies. I love to use the fire roasted kind, it’s so good! Don’t drain it, open the can and dump the whole thing in.

          Nutrition (per serving)

          Serving Size: 1 · Calories: 593 · Fat: 21 · Saturated fat: 9 · Trans fat: 1 · Carbs: 65 · Sugar: 8 · Fiber: 8 · Protein: 36 · Sodium: 1104 · Cholesterol: 69

          Recipe from: thefoodcharlatan.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

          Dessert

          Chocolate rice and chocolate rice pudding

          Serves: 1 servings

          🍫🍚Chocolate rice and chocolate rice pudding @katlieu

          Ingredients

          • 2 cups uncooked short-grain rice
          • 2 bars of unsweetened or semi-sweet chocolate
          • 1 knob or 1 teaspoon of red or white miso
          • 1 Water, adjust according to your rice cooker’s instructions
          • Optional toppings: dried berries, nuts, honey, maple syrup, sweetened condensed milk, cocoa powder, whipped cream

          Directions

          1. For the chocolate rice:
          2. Rinse the short-grain rice in a colander under cold water until the water runs clear.
          3. Add the washed rice to your rice cooker bowl and fill it with the correct amount of water. For a 5.5-cup Zojirushi rice cooker, use the measuring cup provided and fill the water up to the corresponding water level for 2 cups of rice. Adjust the water level accordingly for your specific rice cooker. For sticky rice, fill the water to the Sticky/Sweet Rice marking in your rice cooker.
          4. Break the chocolate bars into pieces and place them directly on top of the rice, along with a knob of red miso.
          5. Start the cooking cycle on your rice cooker (use a mixed or standard cycle). Once the rice is done, fluff the rice with a rice paddle, mixing the melted chocolate and miso thoroughly into the rice.
          6. Optional toppings:
          7. For extra sweetness, drizzle honey, maple syrup, or sweetened condensed milk over the rice. You can also mix in dried berries and nuts at this point.
          8. For the chocolate rice pudding:
          9. Allow the cooked chocolate rice to cool down.
          10. Take about 1 cup of the cooled chocolate rice and blend it with 1/2 cup of coconut milk or warm water until smooth. Add sweetener (honey, maple syrup, or sugar) to taste, and blend again until there are no remaining rice chunks. If the mixture is too thick, add more liquid to reach your desired consistency.
          11. Pour the blended mixture into ramekins or your chosen serveware. Refrigerate for a few hours, or overnight, to let it set.
          12. Before serving, dust the pudding with cocoa powder and top with your favorite toppings like whipped cream or berries.

          Notes

          Chocapic at the beginning

          Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

          Dessert Cookies Mama Goose’s Recipes

          Chocolate Spritz Cookies

          Serves: 36 s · Prep: 15 min · Cook: 9 min

          These easy Chocolate Spritz Cookies will be your go-to spritz cookie recipe for large family gatherings, office cookie swaps, and sharing with your neighbors this holiday season. This batch makes over 3 dozen cookies and can be scaled up easily!

          Ingredients

          • 4 oz unsalted butter, 1 stick, 113 g, room temperature
          • ⅓ cup granulated sugar, 66 g
          • 1 egg yolk
          • 1 teaspoon pure vanilla extract
          • 1 cup all-purpose flour, 130 g
          • 2 tablespoon cocoa powder, 12 g
          • ¼ teaspoon baking powder
          • 1 large pinch of salt

          Directions

          1. Cream softened butter and sugar in a stand mixer bowl with the paddle attachment until light and fluffy. Add egg yolk and vanilla extract and beat until combined.
          2. Sift together flour, cocoa powder and baking powder in a small mixing bowl. Add the salt and whisk together to distribute evenly. With the mixer on the lowest speed, slowly add the flour mixture to the stand mixer bowl and mix until thoroughly combined. The dough should be soft and easy to handle.
          3. Fill the cookie press with the cookie dough and line three baking sheets with silicone baking mats or parchment paper. Hold the cookie press perpendicular to the prepared baking sheet and press out the cookies, spacing about 1 – 2 inches apart.
          4. Add sprinkles, colored sugars or any decorative touches desired to your cookies. Chill in the fridge or freezer while the oven preheats. Preheat oven to 375°F.
          5. Bake for 8 – 10 minutes. Remove from the oven, let cookies cool on the baking sheet for 5 – 10 minutes before transferring to a wire rack to completely cool.

          Notes

          If you don’t have a stand mixer, a regular mixing bowl and an electric hand mixer will work just as well. Or you can cream the butter by hand with a whisk and just use a spatula to incorporate the dry ingredients. I recommend silicone baking mats for this recipe but parchment paper will also work if you don’t own any silicone baking mat. Just make sure to hold down the paper when lifting up the cookie press when using parchment paper because you may experience the parchment paper lifting up with the dough. You can easily double or triple this recipe to make a lot more when preparing for the holidays.

          Nutrition (per serving)

          Serving Size: 9 · Calories: 45

          Recipe from: wildwildwhisk.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

          Chocolate Strawberry Bites

          Serves: 1 servings

          Healthy Dessert Idea: Chocolate Strawberry Bites🤩 These are such a perfect dessert idea for spring! They are so refreshing and delicious😋

          Ingredients

          • 1 cup diced strawberries, 240 ml
          • ½ cup Greek yogurt, lactose-free, 120 ml
          • 100 g / 3.5 oz. dark chocolate, melted, 80% dark chocolate

          Directions

          1. Mix the strawberries and yogurt together
          2. Spoon dollops onto a plate lined with parchment paper
          3. Freeze for 1 1/2 - 2 hours
          4. Dip in melted dark chocolate (let the chocolate cool down before dipping & make sure that the bites are fully covered in the chocolate)
          5. Store in the fridge.

          Notes

          They last in the fridge for a couple of days. When stored in the fridge they will have a soft consistency. You can also store them in the freezer and let them defrost for a while before serving.

          Cookies Mama Goose’s Recipes

          cinnamon bun cookies

          Serves: 48 cookies · Cook: 12 min

          What's better than a cinnamon bun? Cinnamon bun cookies! Soft, tender, and packed with cinnamon flavour.

          Ingredients

          • 1 cup unsalted butter at room temperature
          • ⅓ cup granulated sugar
          • ¾ cup confectioners' sugar
          • ½ teaspoon salt
          • 1 ½ teaspoons grated orange zest
          • 1 teaspoon pure vanilla extract
          • 1 large egg
          • 2 ¼ cups all-purpose flour
          • Cinnamon smear:
          • 5 tablespoons plus 2 teaspoons unsalted butter, softened
          • ¼ cup packed light brown sugar
          • 1 ½ teaspoons light corn syrup
          • 1 tablespoon ground cinnamon
          • 2 tablespoons all-purpose flour
          • ½ teaspoon salt
          • ½ teaspoon pure vanilla extract
          • Glaze:
          • ½ cup confectioners' sugar
          • 2 tablespoons light corn syrup
          • 1 teaspoon pure vanilla extract
          • 1 teaspoon water

          Directions

          1. In the bowl of a stand mixer fitted with the paddle attachment, beat the butter, sugars, salt, zest, and vanilla together on medium speed until light and fluffy, about 3 or 4 minutes. Scrape down the sides of the bowl and add the egg, beating well to incorporate.
          2. Turn the mixer down to low and add the flour, mixing until just barely combined.
          3. On a sheet of wax or parchment paper (whatever your preference), roll the dough out to approximately 12" square (or more if you want to get more spirals – see my comment above). When it comes time to roll into a log, the paper makes it easier to lift the edge and handle the dough.
          4. Making the roll:
          5. In the bowl of a stand mixer fitted with the paddle attachment, combine all of the cinnamon smear ingredients and beat on medium speed until light and fluffy, about 2 or 3 minutes.
          6. Spread the cinnamon mixture on top of the rolled out cookie dough.
          7. Roll the dough into a log shape. Once you get an edge started rolling by just folding it over slightly, you can simply lift the paper edge and use it to guide the dough to continue rolling. There's a video here that shows what I mean about lifting the paper edge to create the roll. The ends of the log will likely be a bit gappy, so you can just smoosh them in to form a flat edge. The first slice from each end will not have the pretty spiral, but there won't be any wasted dough.
          8. Wrap the log in plastic wrap or the parchment paper you used to roll it out and gently squeeze the log to ensure there are no air pockets inside. You can then roll the log a few times to smooth it back out if necessary.
          9. Transfer the dough to the freezer for at least 15-20 minutes, until firm.
          10. When ready to bake, preheat the oven to 375°F.
          11. Remove the dough log from the freezer and using a sharp knife, cut the dough into 1/4" slices. Give the log a quarter roll after each cut so it doesn't develop a flat edge from constantly pressing on the same side.
          12. Transfer the cut slices to your prepared cookie sheets and bake for about 12-14 minutes, rotating the baking sheet halfway through, until they’re lightly golden around the edges. Allow to cool on a wire rack for a few minutes before removing the cookies from the pan and allowing them to cool completely.
          13. Glazing the cookies:
          14. Combine all the ingredients for the glaze in a small bowl and whisk together. Add additional water if it seems too thick. Using a pastry brush, apply a thin coat to the top of each cookie. Allow to sit at room temperature to dry completely.

          Notes

          I’m almost embarrassed to admit this, but this is the first time I’ve ever made a pinwheel cookie. Honestly, I’m not sure I’ve ever even eaten one before! But every time I see a picture of one online or in one of my cookbooks, I just think “oooh, how pretty!” And even after reading recipes and techniques (and yes, I even watched a couple YouTube videos before attempting), I had this idea that they must be so complicated to make. Nope. If you can roll out dough, you can make a pinwheel cookie. This cookie caught my eye the first time I paged through this cookbook, so I flagged it with a post-it and it’s been in the back of my mind ever since. But if you look at my cookbook shelf, you’ll see a lot of little flags sticking out of the pages. So many cookies, so little time… *sigh* Unfortunately, this is yet another recipe where the picture in the book and the instructions don’t quite jive. According to the recipe, the dough is to be rolled out to approximately 12″ square. Mine was pretty close to that, maybe even a little larger, and you can see I only got a few spirals in the finished cookie. The photo in the book, however, has several spirals, and the dough layer is much thinner than mine. Next time I make these (oh yes, there will be a next time), I’ll roll it thinner – maybe even twice as much. Flavour-wise, there is absolutely nothing wrong with this cookie, but I think it would look much prettier with more spirals. That is, in fact, what drew me to this cookie in the first place. This dough is great to work with. Not sticky at all, but soft and pliable. It rolled out really easily, so getting it thinner should be absolutely no problem. The cinnamon mixture turned out a bit thick and tough to work with, but I’m going to blame that on how cold it was in my kitchen – in a warmer kitchen, the butter would be softer and easier to spread. It probably would have worked out better if I’d softened it a bit in the microwave rather than simply counting on the room temperature to do it. The cookie spreads slightly in the oven, and flattens out a tiny bit, but overall holds its shape well. But the cinnamon layer bubbles up and oozes out of the spiral, making it look even more like a cinnamon bun. So pretty. Using the proportions in the recipe, the glaze was much too thick for me to brush on – I could have maybe spread it on with a knife, but that’s not the effect we’re going for here. I thinned it with a bit more water and it was fine. Although I ran out for the last few and had to whip up a bit more. It seemed like a lot of glaze at first, but when they say to brush on a “thin layer” they really mean “thin”. Oops. Prior to adding the glaze, the cookie has a more subtle flavour. It’s okay, but in my opinion, the glaze adds a touch of extra sweetness that the cookie needs. It doesn’t overpower the cinnamon, but makes the cookie taste more like its namesake. The cookie has a bit of crunch, but is overall soft and tender. One of my coworkers declared this cookie “life-changing”. I think he may have been exaggerating slightly, but he’s adamant that I make more. I tried just one of these immediately after I made them, and yes, it was a good cookie, but I couldn’t figure out why he thought they were so great. And then I had another. OMG, I don’t know if my tastebuds just weren’t picking up on it the first time or these cookies needed a day or two for the flavour to mature, but this is a good cookie. This is only the second recipe I’ve tried from this cookbook, and they’ve both landed among my all-time favourites. I have very high hopes for the rest of this book!

          Recipe from: wannacomewith.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

          Cinnamon Swirl Chocolate Chunk Pumpkin Bread

          Serves: 1 loaf · Prep: 15 min · Cook: 75 min

          It’s gluten free, dairy free, and refined sugar free, and made only with ingredients from @vitacost.

          Ingredients

          • 1 Pumpkin Bread
          • 1 ¼ cups pure pumpkin purée
          • 3 flax eggs, or regular eggs
          • ½ cup olive or avocado oil
          • ¾ cup pure maple syrup
          • 1 tsp pure vanilla extract
          • 3 cups gluten free oat flour
          • 1 ½ tsp baking powder
          • ½ tsp baking soda
          • ¾ tsp salt
          • 2 tbsp pumpkin spice
          • ½ cup chocolate chunks, I use dairy free and refined sugar free
          • 1 Cinnamon Swirl
          • ¼ cup coconut sugar
          • 1 tbsp cinnamon

          Directions

          1. First, make the pumpkin bread batter: whisk together all of the wet ingredients in a large bowl then add in the dry ingredients (except for the chocolate). Once the batter is well mixed, fold in the chocolate chunks.
          2. Next, mix together the coconut sugar and cinnamon in a small bowl then line an 8x5 loaf pan with parchment paper. Add half of the batter to the bottom of the lined pan and top with half of the cinnamon sugar mixture. Top with the remaining batter and cinnamon sugar then gently swirl the top of the loaf.
          3. Preheat the oven to 350°F and let the loaf rest while the oven preheats. Once the oven is preheated, bake the bread for 1 hour and 15 minutes, or until a toothpick inserted in the center comes out clean. Once baked, let the bread cool completely in the pan.
          4. Once the bread has cooled, slice and enjoy! Store leftovers at room temperature for 2-3 days or in the fridge for 4-5 days.

          Notes

          @jonathanreisfeld granulated monk fruit would probably work instead of coconut sugar! And maybe almond flour instead of oat flour but haven’t tried that!

          Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

          Dessert

          Classic Jello Salad

          Serves: 1 12

          This cool, sweet, creamy jello salad is good any time. Tune in to Mad Men, put your apron on, and whip up a retro jello salad!

          Ingredients

          • 1 small package lemon jello
          • 1 small package lime jello
          • 1 cup boiling water
          • 1 can sweetened condensed milk, 14 oz
          • 1 carton small curd cottage cheese, 16 oz
          • 1 cup real mayonnaise
          • 1 can crushed pineapple in juice, 13 to 15 oz

          Directions

          1. Empty jello packages into a large bowl. Pour in boiling water and stir until gelatin is dissolved.
          2. Pour in condensed milk, cottage cheese, mayo, and pineapple, and stir until well-combined.
          3. Pour into a 2 to 3 quart dish, cover, and refrigerate until firm (several hours).

          Notes

          Only a member of this blog may post a comment.

          Dessert

          Coconut Tiramisu (No Eggs or Alcohol)

          Serves: 12 servings · Prep: 15 min

          Coconut Tiramisu is a tropical twist on the classic Italian dessert that’s as easy to make as it is delicious. This no-bake dessert combines creamy mascarpone cheese, whipped coconut cream, and a hint of coconut extract for a flavor that’s smooth and satisfying. No eggs needed! Instead of the usual coffee-soaked ladyfingers, this recipe uses a coconut milk soak, giving each bite a rich, tropical flavor. You can assemble it in minutes, and after a few hours in the fridge, you’re left with a dessert that’s perfect for any occasion, from casual dinners to fancy parties. This simple, yet indulgent treat is made with just a few easy ingredients, like whipped cream, coconut milk, and ladyfingers, making it a breeze to whip up. The final touch of toasted coconut on top adds a bit of crunch and an extra dose of coconut flavor. Whether you’re a fan of classic tiramisu or just looking for a new dessert to try, this one is sure to impress with its tropical flair and creamy texture.

          Ingredients

          • 1 pkg gluten-free lady fingers
          • 24 oz mascarpone cheese room temperature, or use dairy-free cream cheese
          • 1 cup sugar
          • 16 oz heavy cream
          • 2 tsp coconut extract
          • 1 cup coconut milk I used MALK
          • 8 oz whipped topping
          • Topping:
          • 1 cup sweetened coconut flakes toast before using for extra flavor

          Directions

          1. Prepare the cream mixture:
          2. In a mixing bowl, combine the sugar, coconut extract, and cream cheese (or dairy-free cream cheese). Mix on high for 3-5 minutes until fluffy.
          3. Slowly add the heavy cream and mix on high until smooth and whipped.
          4. Prepare the lady fingers:
          5. In a shallow bowl, pour the coconut milk. Dip the lady fingers into the coconut milk one at a time, making sure they are lightly soaked but not soggy.
          6. Line the bottom of your 10×7-inch baking dish with a layer of dipped lady fingers.
          7. Layer the tiramisu:
          8. Pour half of the cream mixture over the lady fingers, spreading it evenly.
          9. Repeat with another layer of dipped lady fingers, then pour the rest of the cream mixture over the top.
          10. Chill:
          11. Place the tiramisu in the fridge to set for at least 6 hours, or overnight for best results.
          12. Add the toppings:
          13. Once chilled, top with the whipped topping and toasted coconut flakes.
          14. Serve:
          15. Slice, serve, and enjoy!

          Notes

          While almond milk is an option, it is less preferred due to its liquid consistency. Consider opting for a milk alternative with a thicker texture.

          Nutrition (per serving)

          Calories: 320 · Fat: 24 · Carbs: 28 · Sugar: 22 · Fiber: 2 · Protein: 4 · Sodium: 50

          Recipe from: thesaltycooker.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

              Appetizers Baked Goods

              Copycat Auntie Anne's Pretzel Bites w/ Cheese Dip & Cream Cheese Frosting

              Serves: 1 servings · Prep: 100 min · Cook: 8 min

              There are very few things in this world that can beat Auntie Anne's Pretzel Bites. Making them at home is surprisingly simple and they really do taste like the real deal! Add some of your favorite homemade sauces for dipping and you've got yourself the most incredible snack that's perfect for an afternoon treat or your next game day watch party!

              Ingredients

              • For the dough:
              • ½ cupswhole milk
              • 1 warm
              • ½ cupswater
              • 1 warm
              • 2 ¼ tspsyeast
              • 1 dry rapid/instant
              • 1 tspgraunulated sugar
              • 3 cupsbread flour
              • ⅓ cupsdark brown sugar
              • 1 lightly packed
              • ½ tspssalt
              • 1 fine
              • ½ tspsbaking powder
              • 2 Tbsbutter
              • 1 unsalted partially melted
              • ½ tspssalt
              • 1 fine
              • For the water bath:
              • 2 cupswater
              • 1 warm
              • 2 Tbsbaking soda
              • For the toppings:
              • 5 Tbsbutter
              • 1 melted unsalted
              • 1 servingcinnamon sugar
              • to taste
              • 1 servingcoarse sea salt or pretzel salt
              • to taste
              • For the cheese dip:
              • 1 ½ Tbsbutter
              • 1 unsalted
              • 2 Tbsall-purpose flour
              • 1 1/16 cupswhole milk
              • 2 cupssharp cheddar cheese
              • 1 freshly grated
              • ¼ tspsgarlic powder
              • ¼ tspssmoked paprika
              • ¼ tspsground black pepper
              • Ingredient, OPTIONAL
              • 1 For the Cream Cheese Frosting Dip
              • 4 ozscream cheese
              • 1 softened
              • 2 cupspowdered sugar
              • 1 sifted
              • ¼ cupsheavy cream

              Directions

              1. For the dough & baking:
              2. In a small microwave-safe bowl, warm the milk and water for about 45 seconds.
              3. Add the granulated sugar and instant yeast to the warm milk/water. Give it a quick mix and set that aside for ~5-8 minutes or until the yeast blooms (you will know that it's ready when it's bubbling and foaming on top!)
              4. In the bowl of your stand mixer (or just a large mixing bowl), combine all of the other dough ingredients using a whisk (bread flour, dark brown sugar, salt, baking powder, and partially melted butter).
              5. Place the dough hook on your stand mixer (or continue mixing by hand). Add the bloomed yeast to the dry ingredients and begin mixing the dough on low speed until a dough ball begins to form.
              6. Sprinkle bread flour on a clean and dry counter and then place the dough ball on top of the counter.
              7. Continue kneading the dough by hand for about 3 minutes until it's just slightly sticky and soft.
              8. Form the dough in a tight ball and place it in a well-oiled bowl.
              9. Cover the bowl with plastic wrap and a warm kitchen towel. Allow the dough to rise for 45 minutes – 1 hour or until it has doubled in size. This rising time will greatly depend on the temperature of the room (a warmer room will allow the dough to rise faster!)
              10. Line a baking sheet with parchment paper and preheat your oven to 400℉.
              11. After the first rise, place the dough back onto a lightly floured counter and gently roll it out using a rolling pin.
              12. Cut the dough into 6 equal strips and then roll each strip out to about 22" long.
              13. Cut 1" pieces from those dough strips.
              14. In a medium mixing bowl, combine the warm water and baking soda for the water bath.
              15. Dunk all of the dough pieces into the water bath for just a few seconds before taking them out, straining the excess water, and placing them on your baking sheet.
              16. Bake at 400℉ on the center rack for 8-9 minutes until the tops are just slightly golden and the bottoms are a deeper brown. As always, the baking time will vary depending on your oven so keep an eye on them as they bake!
              17. Allow the pretzel bites to cool off before tossing in butter, salt, and/or cinnamon sugar.
              18. For the toppings:
              19. In a microwave-safe bowl, melt 5 tbsp of butter (you can use more or less depending on your personal preference)
              20. If you would like to make both the salted pretzels as well as the cinnamon sugar pretzels, divide the pretzel bites in half between two separate mixing bowls.
              21. Add the melted butter to the pretzel bites and then add the coarse salt or cinnamon sugar mixture (this is just granulated sugar mixed with ground cinnamon to taste).
              22. Toss the pretzels to incorporate the melted butter and toppings. This ensures that each pretzel bite is properly coated with all of the yummy goodness!
              23. For the cheese dip:
              24. **If you would like all of your pretzel bites to be salted, I would recommend making 1 batch of the cheese dip as listed in the ingredients. If you are like me and would like to split the pretzel bites between salted and cinnamon sugar, I would recommend only making a 1/2 batch of the cheese dip as it does make a lot!
              25. In a saucepan over medium heat, melt the butter.
              26. Whisk in the all-purpose flour and continue whisking it until it's well incorporated and you don't have any flour lumps (Please do not burn the flour! Keep mixing it the entire time! This should take about 1 minute)
              27. Next, slowly pour the milk into the saucepan as you gently whisk. Do not stop whisking as you don't want that flour to settle to the bottom and burn.
              28. Once the milk mixture thickens, take the saucepan off the heat.
              29. Add in the freshly grated sharp cheddar cheese as well as all of the seasonings.
              30. Begin to gently fold in the cheese and allow the residual heat from the milk mixture to melt the cheese.
              31. Continue mixing until you have a smooth consistency and be sure to serve the cheese sauce while it's warm (you can re-heat it if you need to!)
              32. For the cream cheese frosting dip:
              33. **If you would like all of your pretzel bites to be cinnamon sugar, I would recommend making 1 batch of the frosting dip as listed in the ingredients. If you would like to split the pretzel bites between salted and cinnamon sugar, I would recommend only making a 1/2 batch of the frosting dip as it does make a lot!
              34. In a small bowl, combine all of the cream cheese frosting ingredients and mix very well using a rubber spatula until smooth.

              Notes

              The Ultimate Game Day Snack It’s that time of year where there always seems to be a reason to host (and I’m not mad about it). When it comes to hosting, things tend to be a little stressful but they don’t need to be! Having appetizer options that don’t take all day to prep is a must. This pretzel recipe may seem complicated from the outside, however, since the dough only requires one rise (less than an hour) and the pretzel bites take less than 10 minutes to bake in the oven, you can really have this snack ready in no time. Short on Time? If you’re short on time and you want to have this appetizer ready as quickly as possible, you can always buy pre-made dips at the grocery store as opposed to making them from scratch. Though I always prefer a homemade recipe, it’s never a bad thing to take a few shortcuts when needed! Dough Details Personally, I am a visual learner and sometimes it can be difficult to follow a recipe when the only instructions that are available are written. If you’re like me and you want to see exactly how to prep the dough for these pretzel bites, I have included a step-by-step photo guide below:

              Nutrition (per serving)

              Calories: 5162.6 · Fat: 232.1 · Carbs: 651 · Protein: 129.6

              Recipe from: ostarecipes.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

              Mexican Mama Goose’s Recipes

              Copycat Crunchwrap Supreme

              Serves: 6 wraps · Prep: 25 min · Cook: 3 min

              Make the classic Crunchwrap Supreme at home with this easy to make recipe!

              Ingredients

              • 6 large flour tortillas
              • 6 small corn tortillas or alternatively skip step #2 and use tortilla chips
              • 1 pound ground beef
              • 1 package taco seasoning
              • All to taste:
              • ½ cup sour cream
              • ½ cup nacho cheese sauce from a can or made from scratch, see notes
              • ½ cup shredded lettuce
              • ½ cup diced tomatoes

              Directions

              1. Brown ground beef in a skillet, then add taco seasoning (according to package directions). Set aside.
              2. Bake the corn tortillas in the oven at 400f degrees for a couple of minutes, until they are golden and crunchy.
              3. Microwave the flour tortillas, one at a time (because they cool off quickly), for about 10 seconds to warm them up; this will make wrapping easier. You could also warm them up in the oven; just wrap them in aluminum foil and heat them up for about 10 minutes at 350 degrees.
              4. Lay out the flour tortilla. Spoon some of the seasoned beef into the center of the tortilla. Top with some nacho cheese, or shredded cheese. Place the crunchy corn tortilla on top of the cheese/beef; spread a dollop of sour cream on top of it, then toss on some diced tomatoes and shredded lettuce.
              5. Starting with the bottom of the tortilla, fold the edge up to the center. Continue doing this in a clockwise or counter-clockwise movement until all of the tortilla is folded over, and the filling is entirely enclosed. If your filling isn’t entirely enclosed and you have an open spot, simply cut out a circle from another flour tortilla and place it on the exposed area, then wrap the tortilla again.
              6. Spray a frying pan with cooking spray. Carefully place the Crunchwrap, seam-side down, in the pan. Press with a spatula and cook on medium-low heat, for about 3 minutes, until the bottom is nice and brown. Flip it over and cook until it is golden brown.

              Notes

              Homemade nacho cheese sauce: I cubed up about 1 cup of Velveeta, added to a medium heat saucepan. Add a tablespoon of milk and a few glugs of hot sauce. Stir until melted.

              Nutrition (per serving)

              Serving Size: 1 · Calories: 432 · Fat: 26 · Saturated fat: 10 · Trans fat: 1 · Carbs: 33 · Sugar: 4 · Fiber: 4 · Protein: 18 · Sodium: 924 · Cholesterol: 67

              Recipe from: jamhands.net, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

              Soup Mama Goose’s Recipes

              Corn and Squash Soup

              Serves: 8 servings 1/2 quarts · Prep: 10 min · Cook: 15 min

              This hearty soup pairs squash and cream-style corn for pleasant taste. My family says this is their favorite squash recipe, and friends also comment on its wonderful flavor. —Janice Zook, White River Junction, Vermont

              Ingredients

              • 12 bacon strips, diced
              • 1 medium onion, chopped
              • 1 celery rib, chopped
              • 2 tablespoons all-purpose flour
              • 1 can chicken broth, 14-1/2 ounces
              • 6 cups mashed cooked butternut squash
              • 2 cans cream-style corn, 8-3/4 ounces each
              • 2 cups half-and-half cream
              • 1 tablespoon minced fresh parsley
              • 1 ½ teaspoons salt
              • ½ teaspoon pepper
              • Sour cream, optional

              Directions

              1. In a large saucepan, cook bacon over medium heat until crisp. Remove to paper towels; drain, reserving 2 tablespoons drippings.
              2. In the drippings, saute onion and celery until tender. Stir in flour until blended. Gradually stir in broth. Bring to a boil; cook and stir for 2 minutes or until slightly thickened.
              3. Reduce heat to medium. Stir in the squash, corn, cream, parsley, salt, pepper and bacon. Cook and stir until heated through. Garnish with sour cream if desired.

              Nutrition (per serving)

              Serving Size: 1 · Calories: 381 · Fat: 26 · Saturated fat: 11 · Carbs: 29 · Sugar: 12 · Fiber: 6 · Protein: 9 · Sodium: 1021 · Cholesterol: 53

              Recipe from: tasteofhome.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

              Appetizers Snacks Healthy

              Cottage Cheese Chips

              Serves: 1 servings · Cook: 40 min

              High-protein, low-carb, keto-friendly, and totally customizable—these are going on repeat around here.

              Ingredients

              • 1 cup whole milk small curd cottage cheese, 4% milkfat

              Directions

              1. Preheat oven to 350°F. Line a large baking sheet with parchment paper.
              2. Scoop 1 tablespoon portions of cottage cheese onto the sheet, spacing them about 2 inches apart.
              3. Flatten each scoop using the back of a spoon (to about ¼ inch thick).
              4. Sprinkle with your favorite seasoning (optional but delicious).
              5. Bake for 40–50 minutes, or until the edges are dark golden and the tops are browned and crispy.
              6. Let them cool completely—they’ll crisp up as they cool!

              Notes

              Store in an airtight container and enjoy within a few hours for max crunch. 💡 Flavor ideas: – Everything bagel seasoning + dip in whipped cream cheese – Taco seasoning + dip in guacamole or salsa – Ranch seasoning + dunk in Greek yogurt ranch – Italian herbs + warm marinara

              Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

              Mama Goose’s Recipes Casserole

              Cowboy Casserole

              Serves: 2 servings · Prep: 15 min · Cook: 20 min

              Cowboy casserole—made with ground beef, corn, cheese and Tater Tots—is a big, warm hug after a long day and is especially comforting when the weather turns cold.

              Ingredients

              • ½ pound lean ground beef, 90% lean
              • 1 can whole kernel corn, drained, 8-3/4 ounces
              • ⅔ cup condensed cream of chicken soup, undiluted
              • ½ cup shredded cheddar cheese, divided
              • ⅓ cup 2% milk
              • 2 tablespoons sour cream
              • ¾ teaspoon onion powder
              • ¼ teaspoon pepper
              • 2 cups frozen Tater Tots

              Directions

              1. Preheat oven to 375°. In a large skillet, cook beef over medium heat until no longer pink.
              2. Stir in corn, soup, 1/4 cup cheese, milk, sour cream, onion powder and pepper.
              3. Place 1 cup Tater Tots in a greased 3-cup baking dish. Layer with beef mixture and remaining 1 cup Tater Tots; sprinkle with remaining 1/4 cup cheese. Bake, uncovered, until bubbly, 20-25 minutes.

              Nutrition (per serving)

              Calories: 714 · Fat: 38 · Saturated fat: 15 · Carbs: 56 · Sugar: 9 · Fiber: 6 · Protein: 37 · Sodium: 1675 · Cholesterol: 120

              Recipe from: tasteofhome.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

              Pasta Salads Healthy Mama Goose’s Recipes Holidays

              Cracked Out Pea Salad

              Serves: 6 people · Prep: 15 min

              Cracked Out Pea Salad – Macaroni and green peas tossed in mayonnaise, cheddar, bacon, and ranch. Seriously delicious!!! Great for potlucks or a side dish with sandwiches. Great for all your spring and summer cookouts! Can make ahead and refrigerate until ready to serve. It has become our favorite pasta salad recipe!! #pastasalad #sidedish #bacon #ranch

              Ingredients

              • 1 cup elbow macaroni, uncooked
              • ¼ cup cooked chopped bacon
              • 3 green onions, chopped
              • 2 cups frozen baby peas, thawed
              • ¾ cup mayonnaise
              • ¼ cup bottled ranch dressing
              • ½ cup shredded cheddar cheese

              Directions

              1. Cook macaroni according to package directions. Drain and cool.
              2. In a bowl, combine cooked macaroni and bacon, green onions, peas, and cheese.
              3. Whisk together mayonnaise, Ranch dressing. Pour over macaroni mixture and toss to coat.
              4. Cover and refrigerate 2 hours before serving.

              Notes

              Here is our recipe for Homemade Ranch Dressing: https://www.plainchicken.com/copycat-outback-ranch-dressing/

              Recipe from: plainchicken.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

              Mama Goose’s Recipes Chicken

              Cranberry & Spinach Stuffed Chicken with Brie

              Serves: 4 boneless skinless chicken breasts · Prep: 15 min · Cook: 30 min

              Savor the fusion of savory chicken, creamy Brie, and tart cranberries in this mouthwatering stuffed dish! If you’re looking for a dinner that feels both gourmet and comforting, Cranberry & Spinach Stuffed Chicken with Brie is your new go-to. This recipe combines tender boneless, skinless chicken breasts wrapped around a luscious blend of wilted spinach, sweet dried cranberries, and melty Brie cheese. With just 15 minutes of prep, 30 minutes of cooking, and a brief 5-minute rest, you’ll have a beautiful, restaurant-worthy entrée on the table in under an hour. Clocking in at around 400 calories per serving, it’s hearty enough to satisfy but still fits into a balanced menu. Rated as intermediate difficulty, this dish is perfect for home cooks ready to level up beyond the basics. Whether you’re hosting friends or simply craving something special midweek, this stuffed chicken recipe checks all the boxes for flavor, presentation, and that warm-and-cozy feeling we all love at dinner time.

              Ingredients

              • 4 boneless, skinless chicken breasts
              • Salt and pepper to taste
              • 1 tablespoon olive oil
              • 2 cups fresh spinach leaves
              • 1 1⁄2 cup dried cranberries
              • 4 ounces brie cheese, sliced
              • 1 1⁄2 teaspoon garlic powder
              • 1 1⁄2 teaspoon onion powder
              • 1 teaspoon dried thyme
              • 1 1⁄2 cup chicken broth

              Directions

              1. Preheat your oven to 375°F (190°C).
              2. Begin by preparing the chicken breasts. Place them on a cutting board and cut a pocket into the side of each breast.
              3. Season the inside and outside of the chicken breasts with salt, pepper, garlic powder, onion powder, and dried thyme.
              4. In a pan over medium heat, add the olive oil. Sauté the spinach until wilted, then remove from heat and let cool slightly.
              5. In a small bowl, mix together the sautéed spinach and dried cranberries.
              6. Stuff each chicken breast with equal portions of the spinach and cranberry mixture, followed by slices of brie cheese.
              7. Secure each chicken breast with toothpicks to ensure the filling stays inside during cooking.
              8. Heat the remaining olive oil in an oven-safe skillet over medium heat. Add the stuffed chicken breasts and sear each side until golden brown, about 3 minutes per side.
              9. Pour the chicken broth into the skillet, then transfer the skillet to the preheated oven.
              10. Bake for 20-25 minutes, or until the chicken is cooked through and the internal temperature reaches 165°F (74°C).
              11. Remove from the oven and let the chicken rest for 5 minutes before serving.

              Notes

              This dish pairs well with a simple green salad or roasted potatoes. For a richer flavor, try using a cranberry sauce instead of dried cranberries. Ensure to adjust seasonings according to personal taste preferences. Experiment with adding nuts such as walnuts or pecans for added crunch.

              Nutrition (per serving)

              Calories: 400

              Recipe from: julianrecipes.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

              Healthy Mama Goose’s Recipes Holidays

              Cream Corn Like No Other

              Serves: 8 servings · Prep: 5 min · Cook: 10 min

              Creamed corn made from scratch is so much better than anything you'll find in a can. This quick and easy creamed corn recipe pairs frozen corn kernels with a decadently creamy sauce. My husband is not a fan of corn or creamed dishes, but he thinks this is great! An easy and delicious side for Thanksgiving.

              Ingredients

              • 2 packages frozen corn kernels, thawed, 10-ounce
              • 1 cup heavy cream
              • 1 teaspoon salt, or to taste
              • 2 tablespoons granulated sugar
              • ¼ teaspoon freshly ground black pepper, or to taste
              • 2 tablespoons butter
              • ½ cup whole milk
              • 2 tablespoons all-purpose flour
              • ¼ cup freshly grated Parmesan cheese, or more to taste

              Directions

              1. Gather all ingredients.
              2. Combine corn, heavy cream, sugar, butter, salt, and black pepper in a skillet over medium heat. Cook, stirring occasionally, until butter melts, 3 to 5 minutes.
              3. Meanwhile, whisk milk and flour together in a small bowl until smooth.
              4. Whisk in milk mixture; cook, stirring constantly, until thickened and corn is heated through, about 4 minutes.
              5. Remove from heat and stir in Parmesan cheese until melted. Serve hot.

              Notes

              For extra cheesy flavor, stir in more Parmesan or sprinkle on top before serving. For variation, try adding a pinch of cayenne for heat.

              Nutrition (per serving)

              Calories: 253 · Fat: 17 · Saturated fat: 10 · Carbs: 25 · Sugar: 7 · Fiber: 2 · Protein: 5 · Sodium: 373 · Cholesterol: 54

              Recipe from: allrecipes.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

              Pasta Mama Goose’s Recipes

              Creamy Baked Penne

              Serves: 4 people · Prep: 15 min · Cook: 20 min

              Creamy Baked Penne – quick and easy weeknight meal. Penne pasta, Italian sausage, onion, bell pepper, spaghetti sauce, cream cheese, milk, Italian seasoning, and mozzarella cheese. SO good! Everyone cleaned their plate!!

              Ingredients

              • ½ lb extra-lean ground beef or Italian Sausage
              • ½ cup chopped onions
              • ½ cup green peppers
              • 1 jar spaghetti sauce, 24-oz
              • 1 tub whipped cream cheese, 8-oz
              • 2 Tbsp milk
              • 1 Tbsp Italian seasoning
              • 1 cup shredded mozzarella cheese divided
              • 3 cups cooked penne pasta

              Directions

              1. Heat oven to 350°F.
              2. Stir together whipped cream cheese, milk, and Italian seasoning. Set aside.
              3. Brown meat with vegetables in large nonstick skillet.
              4. Stir in spaghetti sauce, ¾ cup cream cheese mixture and ½ cup mozzarella; cook and stir 2 to 3 min. or until mozzarella is melted.
              5. Add pasta; mix lightly. Spoon into 2-qt. casserole; top with remaining cooking creme and mozzarella.
              6. Bake 20 min. or until heated through.

              Notes

              The original recipe used a 10-oz tub of Philadelphia Italian Cheese and Herb Cooking Creme. The suggested substitute to Philadelphia Italian Cheese and Herb Cooking Creme by Philadelphia Brand is 1 tub of whipped cream cheese, 2 Tbsp milk, and 1 Tbsp Italian seasoning. 3 cups of cooked pasta is approximately 1½ cups uncooked penne pasta. My favorite store-bought spaghetti sauces are Trader Joe’s Roasted Garlic Marinara (green label), Rao’s, and La Famiglia DelGrosso. Can Creamy Baked Penne be made in advance? Yes! You can assemble the casserole ahead of time and refrigerate overnight. You will need to add a few minutes to the cooking time since the dish will be cold. If you are feeding a crowd, feel free to double the recipe.

              Recipe from: plainchicken.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

              Soup

              Creamy Beef & Gnocchi Soup

              Serves: 6 servings

              Ingredients

              • 1 tbsp Olive oil
              • 1 Onion, diced
              • 2 tbsp Minced garlic
              • 1 lb Lean ground beef
              • 1 tbsp Tomato paste
              • 1 tsp Parsley flakes
              • 1 tsp Oregano
              • 1 tsp Italiano seasoning
              • 1 tsp Balck pepper
              • ⅔ tsp Salt
              • 3 cups Chicken stock
              • 400 g Gnocchi
              • 2 cups Heavy whipping cream
              • 2 cups Baby spinach
              • 1 cup Parmesan cheese, grated

              Directions

              1. In a deep pan, heat up your olive oil on med/high heat.
              2. Add in your garlic and onion. Fry until fragrant. 3-4min.
              3. Add in your ground beef and cook until brown.
              4. Drain excess fat (optional).
              5. Add in your tomato paste and mix well.
              6. Add salt, black pepper, oregano, parsley, Italiano seasoning, and chicken stock. Mix and bring to a simmer.
              7. Once simmering, add in your gnocchi and cook for 2-3min.
              8. Turn heat down to medium and add in your heavy cream and baby spinach. Mix and let simmer for 3min or until spinach cooks down.
              9. Add in Parmesan cheese and cook for 1-2min or until cheese is melted.

              Nutrition (per serving)

              Serving Size: 1 · Calories: 646 · Fat: 29 · Carbs: 38 · Protein: 30

              Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

              Pasta

              Creamy Beef and Shells

              Serves: 6 servings · Prep: 10 min · Cook: 30 min

              Creamy Beef and Shells 🔥 This one’s a WINNER. Here’s how to make it:

              Ingredients

              • 1 T olive oil, I used @wearebrightland rosemary
              • 1 yellow onion, diced
              • 8 cloves garlic, 6-8, minced, to taste
              • 1 t red pepper flakes, to taste
              • 1 t Italian seasoning
              • Salt and pepper, to taste
              • 1 t smoked paprika, to taste, can sub regular
              • 1 lb grass-fed ground beef
              • 3 T tomato paste, 2-3
              • 4 cups beef broth, I use @kettleandfire
              • 1 cup cream
              • 1 lb medium shells
              • 4 oz freshly shredded sharp cheddar cheese, I use @tillamook
              • Fresh chives, to garnish

              Directions

              1. In a large saute pan (I use misen), heat your olive oil over medium/low heat. Add in your onion, red pepper, Italian seasoning, and a pinch of salt. Saute for a few minutes. Throw in your garlic and cook for another 30 seconds.
              2. Add in your ground beef and season with salt, pepper, and paprika. Break up with a wooden spoon and cook until no longer pink.
              3. Once your beef is cooked through, make a small center in the middle of your pan and add in your tomato paste. Let it cook off in the pan for a couple minutes before stirring everything together.
              4. Pour in your beef broth and cream, season with a pinch of salt and pepper. Bring to a low boil.
              5. Reduce heat to low. Pour in your uncooked pasta and stir. Simmer, uncovered, for 16-17 minutes, or until your pasta is tender.
              6. Sprinkle in your cheese, stir, and taste for seasonings.
              7. Top with extra cheese and garnish with fresh chives. Enjoy!

              Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

              Mama Goose’s Recipes Chicken

              Creamy Chicken Breast in Spinach Parmesan Sauce

              Serves: 3 servings · Prep: 5 min · Cook: 20 min

              This creamy chicken breast recipe is an easy one-pan chicken dish that will wow the entire family for dinner!

              Ingredients

              • 2 chicken breasts
              • 3 tablespoons butter, divided
              • ½ teaspoon salt and pepper, or to taste
              • 1 teaspoon oregano
              • 1 medium onion, minced
              • 4 cloves garlic, minced
              • 1 small jar sun-dried tomatoes, drained from oil and chopped
              • ½ cup chicken broth
              • ½ cup heavy cream
              • ⅓ cup grated Parmesan cheese
              • 1 cup spinach, or more if you like

              Directions

              1. How to make the creamy chicken breast:
              2. Thoroughly season chicken breasts with salt, pepper, and 1/2 teaspoon oregano on both sides.
              3. In a large skillet, melt 2 tablespoons of butter with a bit of oil from the sun-dried tomatoes over medium-high heat. Add chicken breasts and cook for about 4 minutes on each side until browned. Once done and no longer pink, transfer grilled chicken breasts to a plate and set aside.
              4. To make the creamy spinach parmesan sauce, add the remaining tablespoon of butter to the same pan. Cook onion, garlic, 1/2 teaspoon oregano, and sun-dried tomatoes. Slowly add chicken broth, heavy cream, and Parmesan cheese and mix to combine until smooth while simmering for 2-3 minutes. Adjust seasoning with salt and pepper.
              5. Add spinach to the mixture and continue cooking until wilted.
              6. Add the chicken breasts back to the pan and reheat for 2-3 minutes.
              7. Serve the creamy chicken breasts with spinach and parmesan sauce immediately, garnished with fresh chopped parsley. Enjoy! ❤️

              Recipe from: eatwell101.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

              Creamy Garlic Pasta

              Serves: 1 servings

              There are few things I love as much as cheese. One of these few things, however, is garlic. I’m the kind of person that nearly doubles the amount of garlic a recipe calls for because I feel like I can never get enough of it, and you can bet I always pair garlic bread with my pasta. This time, however, I decided to cut out the middleman and go straight for garlic pasta. It’s rich and creamy, while the fresh parsley gives it a nice bit of color and flavor. But more importantly, it gives me my garlic fix. And when I can combine my two loves, garlic and cheese? Now that’s a beautiful thing.

              Ingredients

              • 2 tsp olive oil
              • 4 cloves garlic, minced
              • 2 tbsp butter
              • ¼ tsp salt
              • ½ tsp pepper
              • 3 cups chicken stock
              • ½ lb spaghetti or angel hair pasta
              • 1 cup grated parmesan cheese
              • ¾ cup heavy cream
              • 2 tbsp chopped fresh parsley

              Directions

              1. In a pot, bring the olive oil to medium-low heat. Add the garlic and stir, allowing it to cook for 1-2 minutes. Mix in the butter until melted. Add the salt, pepper and chicken stock. Raise the heat to high and let it come to a boil.
              2. Once it is at a rolling boil, add the pasta and cook for as long as the box’s directions indicate. Reduce the stove to medium heat and mix in the parmesan until completely melted. Turn off the heat and stir in the cream and parsley. Serve immediately.

              Notes

Place spoonfuls of the potato mixture in the oil and flatten them. Flip them occasionally until golden brown. Once finished, lay out on paper towels to drain the excess oil.
