              Pasta 1 Pot

              Creamy Harissa Chicken Pasta

              Serves: 4 serves · Cook: 15 min

              Welcome back to my new series where we’re keeping dinner quick, easy and all in one pan 🙌 If you’re short on time but still want something packed with flavour, this creamy harissa chicken pasta is the one 💥 It’s spicy, silky and done in 15, so you can spend less time cooking and more time doing literally anything else 😅 One pot, big flavour, zero faff.

              Ingredients

              • 500 g chicken breast, diced
              • 1 tbsp chicken seasoning
              • 2 tsp garlic purée, or 2 cloves
              • 1 tbsp tomato purée
              • 1 tbsp rose harissa paste, add more if you like it spicy
              • 300 ml chicken stock, hot
              • 100 g single cream
              • 500 g fresh pasta
              • 120 g cheese, i used a mix of cheddar, Red Leicester and mozzarella
              • Fresh parsley or basil, to garnish, optional

              Directions

              1. Season the chicken with chicken seasoning.
              2. Add to a large pan over medium-high heat and cook for 4–5 mins until starting to brown.
              3. Stir in the garlic purée, tomato purée and harissa paste. Cook for 30 seconds.
              4. Pour in the hot stock and stir in the cream.
              5. Add in the fresh pasta, stir well, then cover and simmer for 4–5 mins, stirring occasionally, until the pasta is tender and the sauce has thickened.
              6. Sprinkle over the cheese pop the lid back on and let it melt for 1–2 mins.
              7. Garnish with parsley or basil if using, and serve straight away.

              Notes

              😍😍

              Nutrition (per serving)

              Calories: 542 · Protein: 38.5

              Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

              Pasta Healthy Chicken

              Creamy Pesto Chicken & Broccoli Pasta Bake

              Serves: 1 servings

              Creamy Pesto Chicken & Broccoli Pasta Bake 🤤

              Ingredients

              • 1 lb pasta of choice
              • ⅓ cup cun-dried tomatoes + oil
              • 1 lb chicken
              • 1 tbsp butter
              • ½ yellow onion, diced
              • Salt and pepper, to taste
              • Paprika, to taste
              • Oregano, to taste
              • Tony’s seasoning, to taste
              • 1 head of garlic, minced, or to taste
              • 2 cups heavy cream
              • 2 cups cooked broccoli, 1-2, to taste
              • 2 handfuls spinach, to taste
              • 2 cups freshly shredded parmesan cheese, plus more for topping and serving
              • 2 cups shredded mozzarella, I used @tillamook in this recipe
              • ½ cup pesto, to taste, I recommend Costco brand
              • Parsley to garnish

              Directions

              1. Preheat oven to 350F
              2. Boil noodles in salted water until al dente. Drain and set aside.
              3. In a large (oven-safe) saucepan or dutch oven, heat about 1 tbsp of sun-dried tomato oil over medium/high heat. Cook your chicken until no longer pink. Remove from pan and set aside. Let rest for a few minutes and then cut into bit sized pieces.
              4. In the same pan, melt your butter. Add in your seasonings and stir to toast. Add in your onion and sauté for 4 minutes or so. Throw in your garlic and stir for another 30 seconds.
              5. Pour in your heavy cream and stir. Simmer for a couple of minutes.
              6. Add in 1 cup of parmesan and 1 cup of mozzarella cheese. Reduce heat to medium/low, and stir until melted.
              7. Add in your sun-dried tomatoes, cooked broccoli, spinach, and pesto. Stir.
              8. Top with remaining mozzarella and parmesan cheese. Bake at 350F for 15 minutes, then finish under the broiler for another 5 minutes, or until you have a nice and golden crust.
              9. Sprinkle with fresh parsley, a touch more parmesan, and enjoy!

              Notes

              Also the spice is Tony Chacheres Creole Seasoning Although this dish had many steps, it tasted great. I will make it again just not on a week night. I seasoned my chicken before cooking and I used penne pasta.

              Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

              Creamy Pesto Chicken Spaghetti Squash Boats

              Serves: 4 boats · Prep: 15 min · Cook: 60 min

              These creamy, cheesy spaghetti squash boats are filled with chicken, spinach, and a cottage cheese pesto sauce, then baked with melty mozzarella. They’re a cozy, high-protein dinner with plenty of veggies in every bite!

              Ingredients

              • 2 spaghetti squashes, about 2.8 lb each
              • 2 tsp olive oil
              • Salt and black pepper, to taste
              • 1 cup 2% cottage cheese
              • 6 tbsp basil pesto
              • ¼ cup grated Parmesan
              • 2 tsp garlic powder, 1-2
              • Optional:
              • ½ tsp Italian seasoning
              • 16 oz cooked chicken breast, shredded or chopped, about 2 large breasts; use cooked weight for accuracy - can also use rotisserie chicken
              • 2 cups fresh spinach, roughly chopped
              • 1 cup shredded mozzarella, divided

              Directions

              1. Preheat oven to 400°F. Carefully cut each spaghetti squash in half crosswise, across the middle, and scoop out the seeds. Brush the cut sides with olive oil and season with salt and pepper. Place cut-side down on a lined baking sheet and roast for 35–45 minutes, or until fork-tender, adding time as needed.
              2. Blend the cottage cheese, pesto, Parmesan, garlic powder, and optional Italian seasoning until smooth. Taste and add salt and pepper as needed.
              3. Transfer the sauce to a large bowl. Stir in the cooked chicken, spinach, and 1/2 cup mozzarella. Refrigerate while the squash finishes roasting.
              4. Let the squash cool slightly, then gently loosen the strands with a fork, keeping the shells intact. Add the strands to the chicken mixture and toss until evenly coated. If the squash releases excess liquid, drain it before mixing.
              5. Divide the filling among the four squash shells and sprinkle with the remaining 1/2 cup mozzarella. Return to the oven for 15–20 minutes, or until the filling is hot throughout and the cheese is melted.

              Nutrition (per serving)

              Serving Size: 1 · Calories: 525 · Fat: 23 · Carbs: 27 · Sugar: 10 · Fiber: 5 · Protein: 55

              Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                Asian Mama Goose’s Recipes Chicken

                Crispy Bang Bang Chicken: An Incredible Sweet and Spicy Sensation

                Serves: 4 serve · Prep: 15 min · Cook: 20 min

                Crispy Bang Bang Chicken is an incredible sweet and spicy sensation that tantalizes the taste buds with its perfect flavor balance. If you have a penchant for dishes that offer both heat and sweetness, then this delightful recipe is just what you need. With crispy chicken pieces drenched in a creamy, spicy sauce, each bite is an explosion of flavor that is both enjoyable and addictive. Whether you’re serving it for a cozy dinner at home or a lively get-together with friends, Crispy Bang Bang Chicken is sure to impress your guests and leave them wanting more.

                Ingredients

                • 1 pound boneless, skinless chicken breasts, cut into bite-sized pieces
                • 1 cup all-purpose flour
                • 1 teaspoon garlic powder
                • 1 teaspoon onion powder
                • 1 teaspoon paprika
                • ½ teaspoon salt
                • ½ teaspoon black pepper
                • 2 eggs, beaten
                • 1 cup panko breadcrumbs
                • 1 Vegetable oil for frying
                • For the bang bang sauce:
                • ½ cup mayonnaise
                • 2 tablespoons sweet chili sauce
                • 2 tablespoons Sriracha, 1-2, adjust to taste
                • 1 teaspoon honey
                • 1 tablespoon lime juice

                Directions

                1. Prepare the chicken:
                2. Cut the chicken breasts into bite-sized pieces. Pat them dry with paper towels.
                3. Make the seasoned flour:
                4. In a shallow bowl, mix together the flour, garlic powder, onion powder, paprika, salt, and black pepper.
                5. Set up the breading station:
                6. In a separate bowl, beat the eggs. In another bowl, place the panko breadcrumbs.
                7. Coat the chicken:
                8. Dip each chicken piece first into the seasoned flour, shaking off excess. Then, dip into the beaten eggs, and finally coat with panko breadcrumbs, pressing gently to adhere.
                9. Heat oil:
                10. In a large skillet or deep pan, heat vegetable oil over medium-high heat until shimmering.
                11. Fry the chicken:
                12. Fry the chicken pieces in batches, avoiding overcrowding, until golden brown and cooked through, about 5-7 minutes per side. Ensure the internal temperature reaches 165°F.
                13. Drain the chicken:
                14. Remove the chicken from the oil and place it on a paper towel-lined plate to drain.
                15. Prepare the bang bang sauce:
                16. In a small bowl, whisk together the mayonnaise, sweet chili sauce, Sriracha, honey, and lime juice until smooth. Adjust Sriracha to taste for desired spice.
                17. Coat the chicken in sauce:
                18. In a large mixing bowl, add the crispy chicken and pour the Bang Bang sauce over it. Toss gently to coat each piece.
                19. Serve immediately:
                20. Transfer the coated chicken to a serving platter, garnishing with green onions or sesame seeds if desired.

                Notes

                To achieve maximum crispiness, double-dip the chicken. After flouring it, dip it back in the egg and then in the panko breadcrumbs again. Yes, chicken thighs can be used for a juicier result. Just remember to adjust the cooking time slightly if they are thicker. If panko is unavailable, you can use crushed regular breadcrumbs or even crushed cornflakes for a different texture. To increase the heat, add extra Sriracha or crushed red pepper flakes into the Bang Bang sauce. You can also mix in chopped fresh jalapeños when coating the chicken. Yes, you can make this gluten-free by using gluten-free breadcrumbs and substituting all-purpose flour with a gluten-free flour blend.

                Nutrition (per serving)

                Serving Size: 4 · Calories: 550 · Fat: 35 · Protein: 30

                Recipe from: easyplateideas.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                Healthy Sandwich

                Crispy Chicken Caesar Salad Croissants

                Serves: 1 servings

                Crispy chicken caesar salad croissants.

                Ingredients

                • 4 thin sliced chicken breasts
                • 1 cup all-purpose flour, 120g
                • Seasonings (to taste):
                • salt, black pepper, garlic powder, onion powder, smoked paprika, italian seasoning, chili powder, kinder bold chicken seasoning blend
                • For the wet mixture:
                • 2 eggs
                • 1 2–3 tbsp heavy cream, 30–45g
                • 1 1–2 tbsp pickle juice
                • For the coating:
                • 1 ½ cups panko breadcrumbs, 90g
                • 1 2–3 tbsp grated parmesan
                • For the caesar salad:
                • 1 romaine lettuce, chopped
                • 1 croutons
                • 1 extra parmesan
                • Other ingredients:
                • 4 large croissants
                • 1 oil for frying
                • Garlic butter:
                • 2 tbsp melted butter, 28g
                • 1 clove garlic, minced
                • 1 parsley
                • 1 extra parmesan
                • Caesar dressing:
                • 1 cup mayo, 240g
                • ½ cup finely grated parmesan, 50g
                • 1 2–3 cloves garlic, minced
                • 2 tbsp lemon juice, 30g
                • 1 tbsp dijon mustard, 15g
                • 1 tsp worcestershire sauce
                • ½ tsp salt
                • ½ tsp black pepper
                • ¼ cup olive oil, 60g
                • 1 2–4 tbsp water, to thin if needed

                Directions

                1. Season the chicken generously with your spices. In one bowl, mix the flour with any remaining seasoning. In another bowl, whisk together the eggs, heavy cream, and pickle juice. In a third bowl, combine the panko and parmesan.
                2. Coat each piece of chicken in the flour, then the egg mixture, then the panko mixture. Let the breaded chicken rest for 5–10 minutes.
                3. Fry in oil over medium heat until golden brown and cooked through, about 4–5 minutes per side. Transfer to a wire rack.
                4. For the dressing, whisk together the mayo, parmesan, garlic, lemon juice, dijon, worcestershire, salt, and pepper. Slowly whisk in the olive oil, then add water until smooth and pourable.
                5. Toss the chopped romaine with some of the caesar dressing. Add croutons and extra parmesan.
                6. Slice the croissants in half and spread a little caesar dressing on both sides. Add the crispy chicken, top with the caesar salad mixture, and sprinkle with more parmesan.
                7. Mix together the melted butter, garlic, and parsley. Brush the tops of the croissants with the garlic butter and finish with extra parmesan.
                8. Serve immediately and enjoyyyy

                Snacks

                Crispy Corn Cheese Balls

                Serves: 1 servings

                Ingredients

                • 280 g boiled corn
                • 40 g all-purpose flour
                • 35 g cornstarch
                • 1 egg yolk
                • Salt, to taste
                • 0.5 tsp black pepper
                • 1 tsp garlic powder
                • 1 tsp smoked paprika
                • 100 g mozzarella cheese, cut into 10 g pieces
                • Oil, for frying

                Directions

                1. In a large bowl, add boiled corn, flour, cornstarch, egg yolk, salt, black pepper, garlic powder, and smoked paprika. Mix well.
                2. Cover and freeze for 30 minutes.
                3. Take a full spoonful of the mixture and flatten it slightly in your hands.
                4. Place 10 g mozzarella in the center. Shape into a ball, completely sealing the cheese inside.
                5. Freeze the balls for another 10 minutes.
                6. Fry in hot oil at 350°F (175°C) until golden and crispy.
                7. Serve hot with your favorite sauce.

                  Chicken Sandwich 1 Pot

                  Crockpot Marry Me Chicken Sandwiches

                  Serves: 1 slices · Prep: 15 min · Cook: 240 min

                  Crockpot Marry Me Chicken Sandwiches are an easy dinner recipe!

                  Ingredients

                  • 1 lb chicken breast
                  • ½ tsp each salt, pepper, oregano, thyme
                  • 1 tsp each onion powder and garlic powder
                  • ¼ tsp red pepper flakes
                  • 1 tbsp minced garlic
                  • ⅓ cup sun dried tomatoes, chopped
                  • ½ cup of chicken broth
                  • 8 slices of bacon
                  • 1 block of cream cheese
                  • 1 cup of shredded mozzarella cheese
                  • 1 Brioche buns

                  Directions

                  1. Spray a crockpot with cooking spray and add the chicken. Season the chicken with salt, pepper, oregano, thyme, onion powder, garlic powder, and red pepper flakes. Next, add the minced garlic, chopped sun dried tomatoes, and chicken broth.
                  2. Be sure to stir the sun dried tomatoes and garlic around. You want the sun dried tomatoes to be down in the chicken broth, so they don’t burn.
                  3. Cook on low for 4-6 hours. Once the chicken is fully cooked shred the chicken, add a block of cream cheese, and shredded mozzarella cheese. Let this keep cooking on low until the cream cheese is melted and stir everything together well.
                  4. Cook bacon while cream cheese is melting. Once bacon is cooked build sandwiches on toasted buns and top with the crispy bacon. Enjoy!

                  Notes

                  Gonna have to give this one a try! Hopefully without sun dried tomatoes doesn’t change the taste too much! 😂 looks great!

                  Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                  Dessert Asian

                  Croffles (Croissant Waffles)

                  Serves: 4 servings · Prep: 5 min · Cook: 20 min

                  These croffles are the viral Korean dessert trend that took the internet by storm, and for good reason! They're crispy, flaky, buttery, and absolutely addictive. If you've never heard of croffles, they're basically croissants pressed in a waffle maker, which transforms those delicate buttery layers into the most incredible crispy, crunchy texture while keeping all that flaky goodness inside. The genius is in the simplicity: you take frozen croissant dough (the kind you can buy at the grocery store), let it thaw, sprinkle it with a little sugar, then press it in your waffle maker until it's golden brown and crispy.

                  Ingredients

                  • For the croffles:
                  • 1 Butter or cooking spray, for greasing the waffle maker
                  • 4 frozen croissants, thawed completely, from 1 package
                  • 2 tablespoons granulated sugar or brown sugar
                  • Optional toppings (choose your favorites):
                  • 1 Fresh strawberries and whipped cream
                  • 1 Crushed chocolate cookies
                  • 1 Boba pearls and sweetened condensed milk
                  • 1 Chocolate sauce, caramel sauce, or maple syrup
                  • 1 Powdered sugar
                  • 1 Ice cream
                  • 1 Fresh berries
                  • 1 Nutella or peanut butter

                  Directions

                  1. Preheat Your Waffle Maker Plug in your waffle maker and preheat it to medium heat. A standard-size waffle maker with a 7-inch grid works perfectly, but any size will do. Once it's heated up, lightly grease it with butter or cooking spray. Don't skip this step or your croffle might stick!
                  2. Prepare the Croissant Dough Make sure your frozen croissants are completely thawed. If they're still cold or firm, let them sit at room temperature for about 30-45 minutes until they're soft and pliable. Separate the croissants into individual portions. You can gently roll or flatten each one slightly with your hands to help it fit better in the waffle maker and ensure even cooking, but don't roll them too thin or you'll lose those beautiful flaky layers.
                  3. Add the Sugar Sprinkle about ½ tablespoon of sugar over both sides of each croissant. You can use granulated sugar for a classic sweet crunch or brown sugar for a deeper, caramelized flavor. The sugar will caramelize as it cooks, creating that amazing crispy, slightly sweet exterior.
                  4. Cook in the Waffle Maker Place one piece of sugared croissant dough right in the center of your preheated waffle maker. Close the lid gently but firmly. You might need to press down a little to help the croissant flatten and fill the waffle grids. Cook for 2-3 minutes, keeping an eye on it. The croffle is ready when it's golden brown, crispy on the outside, and those layers are all visible and flaky. Cooking time might vary depending on your waffle maker, so check after 2 minutes and add more time if needed.
                  5. Let It Set Carefully remove the croffle from the waffle maker using tongs or a fork (be careful, it's hot!). Transfer it to a wire rack or plate and let it sit for 1-2 minutes. This cooling time helps the croffle set and get even crispier. Resist the urge to stack them or they'll steam and get soggy.
                  6. Repeat with Remaining Dough Repeat the cooking process with the remaining croissant pieces, re-greasing the waffle maker lightly between each one if needed. You should end up with 4 beautiful, crispy croffles.
                  7. Add Your Toppings Now comes the fun part! Top your warm croffles with whatever you're craving. Fresh strawberries and a cloud of whipped cream is classic and delicious. Crushed chocolate cookies with a drizzle of chocolate sauce is decadent. Boba pearls with sweetened condensed milk drizzled over top is trendy and amazing. Or keep it simple with just maple syrup and butter. There's no wrong way to do this!
                  8. Serve Warm Serve your croffles immediately while they're still warm and crispy. They're best eaten fresh, but leftovers can be stored and reheated.

                  Notes

                  Make sure croissants are fully thawed: This is super important! If the croissants are even slightly frozen or too cold, they won't press properly in the waffle maker and could cook unevenly. Let them sit at room temperature until they're completely soft and pliable before pressing them. Don't overfill the waffle maker: Place the croissant dough in the center of the waffle maker, not too close to the edges. As it cooks and presses, it will spread out to fill the grid. If you start with it too big or positioned wrong, it might overflow and make a mess. Adjust your heat setting: Every waffle maker is different. Start with medium heat and adjust from there. If your croffles are browning too quickly on the outside but not cooking through, lower the heat. If they're taking forever and not getting crispy, increase the heat slightly. The sweet spot is golden brown and crispy in about 2-3 minutes. Get creative with toppings: The beauty of croffles is that they work with both sweet and savory toppings. Try them with fried chicken and maple syrup for a sweet and savory combo, cream cheese and smoked salmon for brunch, or Nutella and bananas for dessert. Those waffle pockets are perfect for holding whatever you pile on top! Use the sugar strategically: The sugar not only adds sweetness but also helps create that crispy, caramelized exterior. Brown sugar gives you a deeper caramel flavor, while granulated sugar gives you a lighter, classic sweetness. You can also skip the sugar entirely and season with cinnamon sugar, or go savory with a sprinkle of salt and herbs.

                  Nutrition (per serving)

                  Calories: 280

                  Recipe from: mymanabites.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                  Crunchy Japanese Long Fries with garlic mayo parmesan sauce 🥔

                  Serves: 1 servings

                  Crunchy Japanese Long Fries with garlic mayo parmesan sauce 🥔

                  Ingredients

                  • 1 3–4 big potatoes
                  • 1 80–100 g cornstarch, about 6–7 tbsp
                  • Seasoning:
                  • 1 tsp onion powder
                  • 1 tsp garlic powder
                  • 1 tsp dried parsley
                  • 1 tsp salt
                  • 2 tsp paprika powder
                  • 2 tsp black pepper
                  • Garlic mayo sauce (aioli):
                  • 4 tbsp Mayo
                  • 1 tbsp Garlic powder
                  • 1 tbsp Onion powder
                  • 1 Parsley

                  Healthy German

                  Crunchy Pasta Salat

                  Serves: 1 servings

                  Knusprige Parmesan-Pasta aus dem Airfryer trifft auf frischen Salat und ein cremiges Zitronen-Dressing. Außen ultra crunchy, innen leicht chewy und durch das Knoblauchöl besonders aromatisch.

                  Ingredients

                  • Salat:
                  • 1 Romana Salatherz, grob geschnitten
                  • 1 Rote Zwiebel, fein gewürfelt
                  • 1 Cherry Tomaten, geviertelt
                  • 1 Petersilie, grob gehackt
                  • Crunchy pasta:
                  • 1 Muschelnudeln
                  • 1 Olivenöl
                  • 1 Parmesan, fein gerieben
                  • Knoblauchöl:
                  • 1 Knoblauchzehen, gepresst
                  • 1 Olivenöl
                  • Dressing:
                  • 1 Joghurt
                  • 1 Saure Sahne
                  • 1 Senf
                  • 1 Zitronensaft
                  • 1 Zwiebelpulver
                  • 1 Olivenöl
                  • 1 Salz
                  • 1 Pfeffer

                  Directions

                  1. Muschelnudeln in Salzwasser al dente kochen und gut abtropfen lassen. - Cook shell pasta in salted water until al dente and drain well.
                  2. Die noch warmen Nudeln mit Olivenöl und reichlich Parmesan vermengen. - Toss the still-warm pasta with olive oil and plenty of Parmesan cheese.
                  3. Die Pasta im Airfryer bei 200 °C rösten, bis sie goldbraun und knusprig ist. - Toast the pasta in an air fryer at 200°C (390°F) until golden brown and crispy.
                  4. Für das Knoblauchöl gepressten Knoblauch mit reichlich Olivenöl verrühren. - For the garlic oil, combine pressed garlic with plenty of olive oil.
                  5. Die knusprige Pasta mit dem Knoblauchöl vermengen. - Toss the crispy pasta with the garlic oil.
                  6. Joghurt, saure Sahne, Senf, Zitronensaft, Zwiebelpulver, Olivenöl, Salz und Pfeffer zu einem cremigen Dressing verrühren. - Whisk together yogurt, sour cream, mustard, lemon juice, onion powder, olive oil, salt, and pepper to make a creamy dressing.
                  7. Romana Salat, rote Zwiebel, Cherrytomaten und Petersilie in eine große Schüssel geben. - Place romaine lettuce, red onion, cherry tomatoes, and parsley in a large bowl.
                  8. Das Dressing über den Salat geben und alles leicht vermengen. - Place romaine lettuce, red onion, cherry tomatoes, and parsley in a large bowl.
                  9. Zum Schluss die crunchy Parmesan Pasta oben auf den Salat geben.- Drizzle the dressing over the salad and toss gently. Finally, top the salad with the crispy Parmesan pasta.

                  Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                  Healthy Mama Goose’s Recipes Casserole

                  Crustless Broccoli Quiche with Cheddar and Scallions

                  Serves: 8 slices · Prep: 10 min · Cook: 30 min

                  This crustless broccoli quiche is easy to make, is perfect for meatless weeknight dinners, and also makes a filling brunch or lunch.

                  Ingredients

                  • 1 tablespoon butter - for the pan
                  • 16 ounces frozen chopped broccoli - or fresh broccoli cut into florets
                  • 8 large eggs
                  • ½ cup sour cream - or full-fat Greek yogurt
                  • 1 teaspoon Diamond Crystal kosher salt - or 1/2 teaspoon of any other salt, including Morton kosher salt
                  • ¼ teaspoon black pepper
                  • 1 teaspoon garlic powder
                  • ¼ cup scallions - chopped, white and green parts
                  • 1 cup cheddar cheese - sharp, shredded; 4 ounces

                  Directions

                  1. Preheat the oven to 400°F. Generously butter a 9-inch glass or ceramic pie dish.
                  2. Frozen broccoli:
                  3. Place the chopped broccoli in a large microwave-safe bowl. Add ¼ cup of water. Cover and microwave on high for 6 minutes, stirring after the first 3 minutes. Drain well.
                  4. Fresh broccoli:
                  5. Place the broccoli florets in a large microwave-safe bowl. Add ¼ cup of water. Cover and microwave on high for 3 minutes. Drain well, then chop the florets into small pieces.
                  6. In a large bowl, whisk the eggs, sour cream, kosher salt, black pepper, and garlic powder.
                  7. Stir in the broccoli, scallions, and cheese.
                  8. Transfer the mixture to the prepared pie dish. Bake the quiche until it's golden brown, and a knife inserted in its center comes out clean, about 30 minutes.
                  9. Allow the quiche to cool and set in the pan on a wire rack for about 15 minutes, then slice it into 8 triangles and serve.

                  Notes

                  You can double the recipe, bake it in a 9x13-inch baking dish, and cut the quiche into squares. Butter works best for greasing a pie plate. I prefer it to a cooking spray or brushing the pan with oil. I'm loyal to cheddar, but I tested this recipe with shredded Gruyere, shredded Gouda, and crumbled feta. All of these options were delicious. If you don't need this quiche to be meatless, you can add a cup of cooked meat, such as shredded cooked chicken or diced ham. ¼ cup of crumbled bacon is another great addition. To make sure the leftovers don't become soggy, let the quiche cool and slice it. Let the slices cool for 30 more minutes on a cooling rack. Store the slices on a layer of paper towels in an airtight container in the fridge and replace the paper towels daily. Stored this way, the leftovers keep well for about 4 days. Once completely cool, you can also freeze individual slices in freezer bags. You can reheat them in the microwave directly from their frozen state.

                  Nutrition (per serving)

                  Serving Size: 1 · Calories: 196 · Fat: 14 · Saturated fat: 8 · Carbs: 5 · Sugar: 2 · Fiber: 2 · Protein: 12 · Sodium: 317

                  Recipe from: healthyrecipesblogs.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                  Dessert Pie Mama Goose’s Recipes

                  Easy Buttermilk Pie

                  Serves: 8 slices · Prep: 15 min · Cook: 55 min

                  Buttermilk pie is an easy classic dessert made with simple pantry ingredients! The result is a deliciously comforting custard pie with a slightly caramelized topping.

                  Ingredients

                  • 1 single pie crust unbaked, store-bought or homemade
                  • 3 large eggs room temperature
                  • ½ cup unsalted butter softened
                  • 1 ½ cups granulated sugar
                  • 3 tablespoons all-purpose flour
                  • 1 cup buttermilk room temperature
                  • 2 teaspoons vanilla extract
                  • 1 tablespoon fresh lemon juice
                  • ¼ teaspoon ground nutmeg freshly grated

                  Directions

                  1. Preheat oven to 350°F.
                  2. Line a 9-inch pie plate with unbaked pie dough.
                  3. In a medium bowl, whisk the eggs until foamy. Beat in butter, sugar, and flour until fluffy.
                  4. Add the buttermilk, vanilla extract, lemon juice, and nutmeg, along with a small pinch of salt, and mix until smooth.
                  5. Pour the batter into the prepared crust and bake for 45-55 minutes until a knife inserted in the center comes out clean.
                  6. Cool completely before serving.

                  Notes

                  You can use pre-made crust or prepare a Flaky Homemade Pie Crust. If the edges of the crust begin to brown, you can cover them with aluminum foil or a pie shield.

                  Nutrition (per serving)

                  Calories: 400 · Fat: 19 · Saturated fat: 10 · Carbs: 51 · Sugar: 39 · Protein: 4 · Sodium: 243 · Cholesterol: 95

                  Recipe from: spendwithpennies.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                  Easy Cheeseburger Puffs

                  Serves: 9 puffs · Prep: 10 min · Cook: 21 min

                  These easy Easy Cheeseburger Puffs are the perfect lunch or snack. Top them with your favorite cheeseburger toppings to make them your own!

                  Ingredients

                  • ½ lb lean ground beef
                  • ⅓ cup minced onion
                  • ⅓ cup barbecue sauce
                  • ¾ cup flour
                  • 1 teaspoon baking powder
                  • ¾ cup milk
                  • 1 egg lightly beaten
                  • 1 cup shredded cheddar cheese
                  • ½ teaspoon onion powder
                  • 1 teaspoon dried parsley
                  • 3 cheddar cheese strings
                  • pickles for garnish

                  Directions

                  1. Preheat the oven to 375 degrees. Grease a muffin pan very well.
                  2. Brown onion and ground beef until no pink remains. Drain well.
                  3. Add barbecue sauce and cook on low until sauce thickens. Cool completely.
                  4. Combine flour, baking powder and spices in a bowl. Add in milk and egg and stir until combined.
                  5. Stir in shredded cheese and beef. Allow mixture to stand for 10 minutes.
                  6. Divide among 9 muffin wells. Cut each cheese string into 3 pieces and gently press 1 piece into the center of each muffin. Cover with batter.
                  7. Bake 19-21 minutes until lightly browned and puffed.

                  Nutrition (per serving)

                  Calories: 164 · Fat: 7 · Saturated fat: 4 · Carbs: 14 · Sugar: 5 · Fiber: 1 · Protein: 11 · Sodium: 220 · Cholesterol: 49

                  Recipe from: kitchenfunwithmy3sons.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                  Soup Mama Goose’s Recipes

                  Easy Cheesy Taco Soup

                  Serves: 6 servings · Prep: 10 min · Cook: 20 min

                  Cheesy Taco Soup is so simple to throw together and is loaded with taco inspired ingredients. The cheesiness takes this soup to the next level of delicious and will be loved by the entire family!

                  Ingredients

                  • 1 pound lean ground beef
                  • 2 cans diced tomatoes I like to use fire roasted, 14.5-ounce
                  • 1 drained can whole kernel corn, 15-ounce
                  • 1 drained and rinsed can kidney beans, 15-ounce
                  • 1 drained and rinsed can black beans, 15-ounce
                  • 1 drained can sliced olives, 4-ounce
                  • 1 package McCormick® taco seasoning about 2 heaping tablespoons, 1-ounce
                  • 1 can beef broth, 14.5-ounce
                  • 1 package cubed Velveeta cheese or 2 cups shredded cheddar cheese, 16-ounce

                  Directions

                  1. Heat a large pot over medium-high heat and add 1 pound lean ground beef. Cook and crumble the meat until it is cooked through and no longer pink. Drain off the grease.
                  2. Add 2 (14.5-ounce) cans diced tomatoes, 1 (15-ounce) drained can whole kernel corn, 1 (15-ounce) drained and rinsed can kidney beans, 1 (15-ounce) drained and rinsed can black beans, 1 (4-ounce) drained can sliced olives, 1 (1-ounce) package McCormick® taco seasoning , and 1 (14.5-ounce) can beef broth to the pot and stir to combine.
                  3. Add 1 (16-ounce) package cubed Velveeta cheese. Stir until the Velveeta is fully melted, then bring to a low boil. Reduce the heat to medium-low and simmer for 5-10 minutes to let the flavors blend. Serve warm with your desired toppings.

                  Nutrition (per serving)

                  Calories: 567 · Fat: 17 · Saturated fat: 8 · Trans fat: 0.3 · Carbs: 60 · Sugar: 11 · Fiber: 14 · Protein: 46 · Sodium: 2248 · Cholesterol: 79

                  Recipe from: therecipecritic.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                  Mexican Mama Goose’s Recipes

                  Easy Layered Taco Bake

                  Serves: 6 servings · Prep: 15 min · Cook: 30 min

                  The Easy Layered Taco Bake is a simple and delicious twist on taco night, made with layers of seasoned taco meat, creamy salsa con queso, tortillas, and melted cheese. This dish comes together quickly and bakes to perfection, creating a cheesy, flavorful meal that can be sliced and served in wedges.

                  Ingredients

                  • 1 lb ground beef
                  • 1 ¼ ox pkg taco seasoning
                  • water as called for on back of seasoning packet usually 3/4 cup water
                  • ½ cup salsa con queso
                  • 3 flour tortillas 8-inch soft taco size
                  • 2 cups shredded Mexican cheese blend divided

                  Directions

                  1. Preheat oven to 350F degrees.
                  2. In a skillet, brown ground beef until fully cooked and drain off excess grease.
                  3. Add the taco seasoning and water as called for on the back of the seasoning packet (usually 3/4 cup). Then, follow the directions on the back of the seasoning packet.
                  4. Once the taco meat is ready, turn off the heat and mix in 1/2 cup salsa con queso.
                  5. Spray an 8-inch round baking pan with nonstick cooking spray.
                  6. Layer the bottom of the pan with one of the flour tortillas. Next, layer 1/3 of the ground beef taco filling followed with 1/3 of the shredded Mexican cheese blend.
                  7. Repeat layers 2 more times:
                  8. another tortilla, 1/3 of the taco mixture, 1/3 of the cheese.
                  9. Bake at 350F degrees for about 15-20 minutes until cheese is melted and edges are slightly golden brown.
                  10. Allow to cool for a couple of minutes. Slice and serve. Top with desired toppings. Serves 4-6.

                  Notes

                  Can I use corn tortillas instead of flour tortillas? Yes, you can substitute corn tortillas if you prefer or want a gluten-free option. Keep in mind that corn tortillas are smaller and more delicate, so you may need to use more of them, so adjust the layering accordingly.

                  Can I make this dish ahead of time? Yes, you can assemble the layered taco bake ahead of time and store it in the refrigerator for up to 24 hours before baking. When ready to bake, take it out of the fridge and bake as instructed, adding a few extra minutes to account for the chill.

                  Can I add extra ingredients to the taco bake? Absolutely! You can customize this recipe by adding ingredients like black beans, corn, diced onions, or bell peppers to the taco meat mixture. This will add flavor and texture to the dish.

                  How do I store leftovers? Leftovers can be stored in an airtight container in the refrigerator for up to 3-4 days. To reheat, simply warm individual portions in the microwave or bake in the oven at 350°F until heated through.

                  What toppings go well with this taco bake? You can top the taco bake with various toppings, including sour cream, guacamole, salsa, shredded lettuce, diced tomatoes, green onion, jalapeños, black olives, and chopped cilantro. These toppings add fresh flavors and textures to the dish.

                  Can I use ground turkey or chicken instead of beef? Yes, ground turkey or chicken can be used instead of ground beef for a lighter option. Follow the same instructions for browning the meat and seasoning with taco seasoning.

                  Nutrition (per serving)

                  Serving Size: 1 · Calories: 398 · Fat: 28 · Saturated fat: 13 · Trans fat: 1 · Unsaturated fat: 9 · Carbs: 11 · Sugar: 2 · Fiber: 1 · Protein: 26 · Sodium: 791 · Cholesterol: 92

                  Recipe from: realmomkitchen.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                  Dessert Mama Goose’s Recipes

                  Easy Pineapple Upside-Down Cake

                  Serves: 12 servings · Prep: 15 min

                  Super moist and fluffy, pineapple upside-down cake is a comforting and festive one-layer dessert. The combination of yellow cake mix, pineapple, brown sugar, and cherries keeps the cake wonderfully tender and flavorful. After baking, the cake is flipped, and the caramelized fruit becomes the stunning, glossy topping. The sweet syrup created while baking infuses the cake with extra flavor, making it as impressive as it is easy to make. With just 15 minutes of prep, it's easy to enjoy pineapple upside-down cake.

                  Ingredients

                  • ¼ cup butter
                  • 1 cup packed brown sugar
                  • 1 can pineapple slices in juice, drained, juice reserved, 20 oz
                  • 1 jar maraschino cherries without stems, drained, 6 oz, about 18 cherries
                  • 1 box Betty Crocker™ Super Moist™ Yellow Cake Mix
                  • 1 Vegetable oil and eggs called for on cake mix box

                  Directions

                  1. Heat oven to 350°F (325°F for dark or nonstick pan).
                  2. As the oven is preheating, place the butter in a 13x9-inch pan and set it on the middle oven rack to melt. Set a timer for 5 to 7 minutes to remind yourself to check when the butter is fully melted.
                  3. Sprinkle brown sugar evenly over melted butter. Arrange pineapple slices on brown sugar. Place a cherry in the center of each pineapple slice, and arrange remaining cherries around slices; press gently into brown sugar.
                  4. Add enough water to reserved pineapple juice to measure 1 cup. Make cake batter as directed on box, substituting pineapple juice mixture for the water.
                  5. Pour batter over pineapple and cherries.
                  6. Bake 40 to 46 minutes (44 to 53 minutes for dark or nonstick pan) or until toothpick inserted in center comes out clean. Immediately run knife around side of pan to loosen cake.
                  7. Place heatproof serving plate upside down on top the pan; turn plate and pan over. Leave pan over cake for 5 minutes so brown sugar topping can drizzle over cake; remove pan.
                  8. Cool 30 minutes. Serve warm or cool. Store covered in refrigerator.

                  Nutrition (per serving)

                  Serving Size: 0 · Calories: 360 · Fat: 12 · Saturated fat: 4 · Trans fat: 0 · Carbs: 58 · Sugar: 42 · Fiber: 0 · Protein: 3 · Sodium: 260 · Cholesterol: 55

                  Recipe from: bettycrocker.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                    Soup

                    Egg drop soup

                    Serves: 3 servings

                    Ingredients

                    • 500 g skinless chicken thighs, cut into large pieces
                    • 3 eggs, beaten
                    • 300 g corn, 2-300, preferably on the cob
                    • 1 ½ tbsp soy sauce
                    • 1 knob ginger, sliced or smashed, ≈ 25–30 g
                    • 1 5–7 cloves garlic, minced, ≈ 20 g
                    • 2 green onions, sliced for finishing, ≈ 30 g
                    • 500 ml chicken stock
                    • 1 300–500 ml water, adjust to preferred thickness
                    • 1 3–4 tbsp potato starch
                    • 4 tbsp cold water, for slurry
                    • ½ tbsp oil, olive, avo

                    Directions

                    1. Marinate chicken in a bowl and mix with soy sauce and a touch of pepper
                    2. During marinade, mince garlic, slice and smash the ginger, cut the corn and beat the eggs in a bowl, slice the green onions for finishing.
                    3. Over medium-high heat add the oil and cook the chickenfor 2-3 minutes per side until lightly browned. Add the ginger during searing so it perfumes the oil.
                    4. Remove chicken from the pot. Cut the chicken into bite-size cubes and set aside.
                    5. Lower heat to medium. Add minced garlic into the same pot and cook 30–45 seconds until fragrant, using the remaining chicken fat.
                    6. Pour in chicken stock and 300 ml water. Bring to a gentle simmer.
                    7. Mix potato starch with cold water to form a smooth slurry. Slowly stir the slurry into the simmering broth and cook 1–2 minutes until the soup thickens slightly.
                    8. Return the cubed chicken to the pot and simmer gently for 3–5 minutes until cooked through.
                    9. Reduce heat to low. Slowly pour in beaten eggs in a thin stream while gently stirring in one direction to create egg ribbons.
                    10. Turn off heat, add sliced green onions, taste, and adjust seasoning if needed. Serve hot.

                    Notes

                    EYYYEEE Liyue bg les goooo, and on theme too!

                    Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                    Dessert

                    Fluffy Lime Jello Salad

                    Serves: 16 servings · Prep: 15 min

                    I gave the vintage lime Jello salad a modern makeover. With cream cheese for tang, pineapple and lime for zip, and a splash of 7-Up for that light, fizzy fluff, this retro-inspired treat is now a sweet, citrusy dream I know you guys will love!

                    Ingredients

                    • 1 cup water
                    • 1 cup reserved pineapple juice
                    • 1 box lime gelatin, 6-ounce
                    • 8 ounces softened cream cheese
                    • 1 cup 7-Up Soda or other lemon-lime soda
                    • 1 can drained crushed pineapple juice reserved, 20-ounce
                    • 1 container thawed whipped topping, 8-ounce

                    Directions

                    1. Spray a 9 x 13 x 2-inch baking dish lightly with nonstick cooking spray and set aside.
                    2. Combine 1 cup water with 1 cup reserved pineapple juice and heat it in the microwave for 1-2 minutes, until boiling. Add it to a medium bowl with 1 (6-ounce) box lime gelatin and whisk until the gelatin is completely dissolved. Set it aside to cool for at least 10 minutes,
                    3. Add 8 ounces softened cream cheese to a large bowl and use a hand mixer or stand mixer fitted with the paddle attachment to beat the cream cheese until smooth. Take the time to scrape down the sides and bottom of the bowl and beat it again, ensuring that there are no lumps of cream cheese remaining.
                    4. Add the cooled gelatin to the cream cheese and carefully mix until completely combined.
                    5. Slowly add 1 cup 7-Up Soda to the cream cheese mixture to ensure it's evenly incorporated and there aren't any lumps. Add 1 (20-ounce) can drained crushed pineapple and mix until combined.
                    6. Whisk in 1 (8-ounce) container thawed whipped topping until smooth, then pour the gelatin into the prepared baking dish.
                    7. Cover with plastic wrap and chill for at least 4 hours, or until completely set. Serve with additional whipped topping or whipped cream.

                    Notes

                    Leftover Instructions: Store leftovers tightly covered in the fridge for up to 5 days—skip the freezer, Jello doesn’t thaw well.

                    Nutrition (per serving)

                    Calories: 96 · Fat: 7 · Saturated fat: 4 · Carbs: 8 · Sugar: 7 · Fiber: 0.03 · Protein: 1 · Sodium: 58 · Cholesterol: 15

                    Recipe from: therecipecritic.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                      French Dip Loaded Garlic Bread

                      Serves: 1 servings

                      Ingredients

                      • Garlic bread:
                      • ½ French loaf
                      • 1 lb shaved roast beef
                      • 3 onions, thinly sliced
                      • 1 tbsp butter
                      • 1 tbsp brown sugar
                      • 8 slices provolone
                      • Garlic butter:
                      • ¾ cup butter
                      • 4 garlic cloves, minced
                      • 2 tbsp parsley
                      • ½ tsp salt
                      • Au jus:
                      • 3 cups beef broth
                      • 1 ½ tbsp Worcestershire sauce
                      • 1 ½ tsp soy sauce
                      • ¾ tsp onion powder
                      • ¾ tsp black pepper

                      Directions

                      1. Melt 1 tbsp butter over medium-low heat.
                      2. Add the sliced onions, brown sugar and a pinch of salt. Cook, stirring occasionally, until deeply golden and caramelized, about 45–60+ mins.
                      3. Mix all Garlic Butter ingredients together.
                      4. Add all Au Jus ingredients to a pot and simmer for 15–20 mins.
                      5. Warm the shaved roast beef in the hot Au Jus for 30–60 seconds.
                      6. Slice the loaf lengthwise. Spread with Garlic Butter, then top with beef, caramelized onions and provolone.
                      7. Bake at 400°F for 12 mins, then broil for 2 mins until the cheese is golden.
                      8. Slice into portions and serve with the warm Au Jus for dipping.

                      Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                      Baked Goods Sandwich

                      GARLIC KNOT DOUBLE SMASHBURGER WITH CARAMELIZED ONION & CHIPOTLE SAUCE

                      Serves: 1 slices

                      Juicy double smash patties, melty cheese, sweet caramelized onions, and smoky chipotle sauce, all stacked between buttery toasted garlic knots. This burger is the perfect fusion of comfort food and creativity, soft, garlicky buns meet bold, smoky flavor in every bite.

                      Ingredients

                      • For the burgers:
                      • 4 giant garlic knots – click here for the homemade recipe
                      • 240 g ground beef, 4 patties, 60 g each
                      • 4 slices melting cheese, American or cheddar
                      • 1 Butter for toasting buns
                      • 1 For the Caramelized Onions
                      • 1 large onion, thinly sliced
                      • 10 g butter
                      • 1 tbsp sugar
                      • Salt to taste
                      • 1 For the Chipotle Sauce
                      • 3 tbsp mayonnaise
                      • 1 tsp Dijon mustard
                      • 1 tsp lemon juice
                      • 1 tsp chipotle paste, or chipotle in adobo, finely chopped
                      • 1 tbsp caramelized onions, from above
                      • Salt & pepper to taste

                      Directions

                      1. Caramelize the onions:
                      2. Heat butter in a pan over medium-low heat. • Add sliced onions and 1 tbsp sugar. Cook slowly for 15–20 minutes until deep golden brown. Stir occasionally to prevent burning. Add a pinch of salt at the end and set aside.
                      3. Make the chipotle sauce:
                      4. In a small bowl, mix mayonnaise, mustard, lemon juice, chipotle paste, and 1 tbsp of the caramelized onions. Season with salt and pepper, and stir until creamy.
                      5. Form & smash the patties:
                      6. Roll the beef into 60 g balls (2 per burger). Heat a cast-iron pan or griddle over high heat. Place a beef ball on the hot surface and smash it flat using a burger press or spatula. Season with salt and pepper. Flip once crispy, add a slice of cheese, and cook until melted.
                      7. Toast the garlic knots:
                      8. Slice the garlic knots in half like buns. Toast the inside on a hot pan with a bit of butter until golden.
                      9. Assemble the burger:
                      10. Spread chipotle sauce on the bottom bun. Add one cheesy patty, a spoon of caramelized onions, the second patty, more sauce, and top with the garlic knot bun.

                      Recipe from: delicate-kitchen.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                      Chicken

                      Garlic Parmesan Chicken & Potato Skillet

                      Serves: 1 servings · Cook: 20 min

                      Garlic Parmesan Chicken & Potato Skillet is an easy weeknight dinner recipe and one of my most viral recipes I’ve made!

                      Ingredients

                      • 1 lb chicken breasts, cut into cubes
                      • 5 small/medium sized potatoes, 4-5
                      • 1 olive oil + salt, pepper, garlic powder, paprika, for potatoes
                      • 2 tbsp of butter
                      • ½ tsp each of salt and pepper
                      • 1 tsp each of paprika, onion powder, and Italian seasoning
                      • 2 tbsp minced garlic
                      • 1 Buffalo Wild Wings Parmesan Garlic Sauce
                      • 1 cup of shredded mozzarella cheese
                      • 1 Dried parsley

                      Directions

                      1. Wash the potatoes, peel them, and cut them up into small cubes. Toss the potatoes with olive oil, salt, pepper, garlic powder, and paprika (I don’t use exact measurements for this part).
                      2. Cook the potatoes in the air fryer at 400 degrees F for about 20 minutes shaking them around at least a couple times while they cook.
                      3. Once the potatoes are about half way through cooking, cut chicken into cubes. Cook the chicken in a skillet on medium high heat with a little butter. Season the chicken with 1/2 tsp each of salt and pepper, 1 tsp each of paprika, onion powder, and Italian seasoning.
                      4. Once the chicken is finished cooking add minced garlic and the Buffalo Wild Wings Parmesan Garlic Sauce. I use just enough sauce to coat the chicken. Next, add the cooked potatoes to the skillet. Stir everything together well and top with some shredded mozzarella cheese.
                      5. Broil in the oven for just a few minutes to melt the cheese (you could also cover with a lid and let this sit on the stove until cheese melts). Top with dried parsley and more Parmesan Garlic sauce if needed. Enjoy!

                      Notes

                      Made this today, absolutely fire, my parents loved it

                      Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                      Chicken

                      GARLIC PARMESAN CHICKEN SKEWERS

                      Serves: 4 serves · Cook: 15 min

                      Day 1 | 30 days of 30 minute dinners 😋 Welcome to my new series! I can’t wait to start sharing some easy, quick and DELICIOUS dinners you’ll make time and time again. We’re starting off strong with these heavenly skewers 🤤 Enjoy! Chris x

                      Ingredients

                      • ¡ï¸chicken:
                      • 640 g boneless skinless Chicken Thighs, 1.4lb
                      • 1 tbsp Olive Oil
                      • 1 tsp Paprika
                      • ¾ tsp EACH: Garlic Powder, Salt
                      • ½ tsp Black Pepper
                      • ¼ tsp EACH: Onion Powder, Cayenne Pepper, reduce if you really don’t like spice - it’s very subtle though
                      • ¡ï¸butter:
                      • 75 g Unsalted Butter, softened, 5 tbsp
                      • 20 g / 1/4 cup freshly grated Parmesan
                      • 3 cloves of Garlic, finely grated/minced
                      • 1 tbsp very finely diced Fresh Parsley
                      • ¼ tsp EACH Salt, Black Pepper, or to taste

                      Directions

                      1. Slice the chicken thighs into bite-sized pieces then combine in a bowl with the oil and seasoning. Thread onto 4x 20cm/8” skewers, ensuring you leave a gap at both ends. Make sure the pieces are nice and compact so they stay nice and juicy. Place in the air fryer and cook at 200C/400F for 10 minutes.
                      2. Meanwhile, combine the butter with parmesan, garlic, parsley, salt and pepper. You want the butter to be soft enough to brush, but not completely melted. A little creamy if you will. Pop in the microwave for 5 second blasts until you reach this consistency.
                      3. After 10 minutes, flip the skewers (I find this easiest with a pair of tongs) and brush with 1/3 of the butter. Air fry for 2 minutes and 30 seconds, then flip them and brush with another 1/3 of the butter. Air fry again for another 2 minutes and 30 seconds, or until lightly charred on the outside and piping hot right through the centre (timings may depend on size of chicken pieces - safe internal temp for chicken is 165F/74C).
                      4. Remove the skewers and brush with the remainder of the butter. Sprinkle over more parmesan & parsley whilst they’re still hot if desired.

                      Notes

                      *The chicken isn’t far off being cooked after 10 minutes and the outside is piping hot, so I just use the same brush/butter throughout. If you’re at all wary just save 1/3 of the butter for the end baste and use a different brush (or clean the first one).

                      Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                      Dessert

                      GOLD DROP CAKE (THE ONE THAT CRIES)

                      Serves: 1 cake · Prep: 30 min · Cook: 65 min

                      As it cools overnight, moisture rises through the meringue and pulls the sugar to the surface… creating those golden tears.

                      Ingredients

                      • 1 CRUST
                      • 200 g flour
                      • 75 g sugar
                      • 75 g cold butter
                      • 1 egg
                      • 1 tsp baking powder
                      • pinch of salt
                      • 1 FILLING
                      • 750 g Greek yogurt, 5% fat
                      • 150 g sugar
                      • 3 eggs, separated
                      • 2 packs vanilla pudding powder
                      • 1 pack vanilla sugar
                      • 1 juice of 1/2 lemon
                      • 150 ml avocado oil
                      • 500 ml milk
                      • 1 MERINGUE
                      • 3 egg whites
                      • 100 g sugar

                      Directions

                      1. Start with the crust — mix everything, press it into a 26 cm (10-inch) springform pan lined with parchment paper on the bottom and sides, and let it chill in the fridge.
                      2. In the meantime, prepare the filling until smooth and pour it over the crust. Bake at 180°C (356°F) on the middle rack with top and bottom heat for about 45–50 minutes. It will still be slightly jiggly in the center — that’s exactly how it should be.
                      3. Ten minutes before it finishes baking whip the egg whites, gradually add the sugar, and beat until stiff peaks form.
                      4. Remove from the oven and spread the meringue evenly on top. Return it to the oven and bake for another 15–20 minutes, just until lightly golden.
                      5. Take it out, gently poke holes all over the surface, then place it back into the switched-off oven with the door slightly open and let it rest for about 15 minutes.
                      6. Remove from the oven and let it cool completely at room temperature. Once cooled, cover and refrigerate overnight.

                      From Lucy's own cookbook collection.

                      Snacks

                      Goldfish Rice Krispies

                      Serves: 12 servings · Prep: 10 min · Cook: 5 min

                      A salty, sweet, and savory treat that will blow your mind.

                      Ingredients

                      • ½ cup unsalted butter, 1 stick
                      • 1 bag marshmallows, 10 ounce
                      • 1 teaspoon vanilla extract
                      • 6 cups Rice Krispies cereal
                      • 2 cups Goldfish crackers

                      Directions

                      1. Grease a 9x13 inch pan with butter or cooking spray. Set aside.
                      2. In a large pot, melt the butter over low heat.
                      3. Add the marshmallows and stir until completely melted and smooth.
                      4. Remove from heat and stir in the vanilla extract.
                      5. Add the Rice Krispies cereal and Goldfish crackers. Stir until evenly coated.
                      6. Press the mixture evenly into the prepared pan. You can use a buttered spatula or your hands (lightly buttered) to prevent sticking.
                      7. Let cool completely before cutting into squares and serving.

                      Recipe from: open.substack.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                      Healthy Mama Goose’s Recipes Casserole

                      Grandma's Green Bean Casserole

                      Serves: 1 casserole 2 ½-quart · Prep: 20 min · Cook: 35 min

                      This green bean casserole without mushroom soup is my grandmother's recipe that she made every Thanksgiving. Instead of traditional French-fried onions, this from-scratch casserole is loaded with lots of melty Cheddar and a buttery cracker crumb topping. A homemade sour cream-based sauce adds just the right amount of tang to every bite.

                      Ingredients

                      • Green beans:
                      • 2 tablespoons butter
                      • 2 tablespoons all-purpose flour
                      • 1 cup sour cream
                      • ¼ cup diced onion
                      • 1 teaspoon salt
                      • 1 teaspoon white sugar
                      • 3 cans French-style green beans, drained, 14.5 ounce
                      • Topping:
                      • 2 cups shredded Cheddar cheese
                      • ½ cup crumbled buttery round crackers
                      • 1 tablespoon butter, melted

                      Directions

                      1. Gather all ingredients. Preheat the oven to 350 degrees F (175 degrees C).
                      2. To make the green beans:
                      3. Melt butter in a large skillet over medium heat. Stir in flour until smooth, and cook for 1 minute. Stir in sour cream, onion, salt, and sugar.
                      4. Add green beans, and stir to coat. Transfer green bean mixture to a 2 ½-quart casserole dish.
                      5. Spread Cheddar cheese over green bean mixture. Toss cracker crumbs and melted butter together in a small bowl; sprinkle over bean mixture.
                      6. Bake in the preheated oven until topping is golden and cheese is bubbly, about 30 minutes.
                      7. Serve and enjoy.

                      Nutrition (per serving)

                      Serving Size: 1 · Calories: 234 · Fat: 16 · Saturated fat: 9 · Carbs: 15 · Sugar: 6 · Fiber: 4 · Protein: 9 · Sodium: 414 · Cholesterol: 45

                      Recipe from: allrecipes.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                      Dessert

                      Grandma’s Lime Green Jello Salad Recipe (with Cottage Cheese & Pineapple)

                      Serves: 8 servings · Prep: 15 min · Cook: 240 min

                      Grandma’s Lime Green Jello Salad Recipe (with Cottage Cheese & Pineapple) is a retro lime jello salad that my husband’s grandma made for many holiday meals. To continue with the tradition, I make this every Thanksgiving.

                      Ingredients

                      • 1 cup crushed pineapple, drained with juice reserved
                      • 1 cup water, more or less depending on what’s needed, see instructions below
                      • 2 packages lime jello, 3 oz each
                      • 1 cup mayo
                      • 1 cup evaporated milk, canned
                      • 1 cup small curd cottage cheese
                      • ½ cup walnuts, chopped

                      Directions

                      1. In a large multi-cup measuring cup (I use my 4 cup Pyrex), add all of the reserved pineapple juice and then add enough water to the juice to make a total of 2 cups. Pour into pot and bring just to a boil.
                      2. Add the lime Jell-o mixes to the boiling pineapple juice/water and stir until dissolved. Remove from heat, allow to cool just slightly.
                      3. In a mixing bowl, stir together the mayo and evaporated milk. Combine until very smooth. Slowly stir in the Jell-o mixture until all is incorporated with the mayo/milk mixture. Stir in the crushed pineapple, cottage cheese, and walnuts.
                      4. Pour mixture into an 8x8″ glass casserole pan. Cover and refrigerate for at least 4 hours or overnight (preferred).

                      Notes

                      –To avoid waste, I often add all of the drained crushed pineapple from the can into this recipe, even if it’s more than a cup, and if I don’t need the leftovers for anything else. –For serving, this Lime Jello Salad can be cut into squares or spooned from the dish. –This recipe can be made 1-3 days in advance.

                      Recipe from: homecookingmemories.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                      Pasta Mama Goose’s Recipes

                      Ground Beef Stroganoff (No Cream Soups)

                      Serves: 8 servings · Cook: 35 min

                      This recipe for homemade beef stroganoff recipe is made without any cream soups and made from scratch but still easy! Creamy and delicious!

                      Ingredients

                      • 1 small yellow onion, diced
                      • 6 ounces fresh sliced mushrooms, optional
                      • 2 tablespoons salted butter
                      • 2 pounds lean ground beef
                      • 3 tablespoons all-purpose flour
                      • 2 cups beef broth
                      • 1 cup sour cream
                      • salt and freshly ground pepper, to taste

                      Directions

                      1. In a large nonstick skillet melt butter over medium-high heat.
                      2. Add diced onion (and sliced mushrooms, if using) and cook until mushrooms are soft and onion is translucent.
                      3. Remove from skillet and set aside on a plate.
                      4. Add ground beef to skillet. Cook and crumble until brown. Drain any grease.
                      5. Sprinkle flour over beef, then pour in beef broth.
                      6. Stir and continue to cook until mixture thickens.
                      7. Add the onions and mushrooms back into skillet then gently stir in sour cream.
                      8. Season with salt and pepper to taste. Let simmer over low heat for another 5 minutes until heated through.

                      Notes

                      Feel free to add some red chili flakes or cayenne pepper for a spicy kick. Heavy cream can be substituted for the sour cream.

                      Nutrition (per serving)

                      Calories: 315 · Fat: 15 · Carbs: 15 · Sugar: 2 · Fiber: 1 · Protein: 28 · Sodium: 349

                      Recipe from: thecountrycook.net, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                        Holidays

                        Homemade Creamed Corn

                        Serves: 1 servings

                        Ingredients

                        • 2 tablespoons butter
                        • 2 packages frozen whole kernel sweet corn thawed, 10-ounces each
                        • 1 tablespoon granulated sugar
                        • 1 teaspoon salt or to taste
                        • ¼ teaspoon black pepper
                        • 2 tablespoons all-purpose flour
                        • 2 cups cream, or half-and-half

                        Directions

                        1. Melt butter in a large skillet over medium heat.
                        2. Add corn, sugar, salt, and pepper, and saute for about 2 minutes.
                        3. Stir in flour, and allow to heat through, about 1 minute.
                        4. Slowly pour in cream or half-and-half until fully incorporated.
                        5. Cook stirring constantly over medium heat until the mixture is thickened, and corn is cooked through.
                        6. Remove from heat.
                        7. **Optional** Use a stick blender to lightly blend some of the corn and create more corn-taste in the sauce.
                        8. Season with more salt and pepper to taste. Serve hot.

                        From Lucy's own cookbook collection.

                          Drinks

                          Homemade Lemonade

                          Serves: 4 servings · Prep: 15 min

                          Refreshing and sharp, perfect for summer!

                          Ingredients

                          • 5 lemons, unwaxed
                          • 1 litre water
                          • 1 large handful ice cubes
                          • 180 grams caster sugar, or brown sugar

                          Directions

                          1. Put the lemons in a blender and blitz along with half of the sugar, half the ice cubes and 500ml water.
                          2. Strain the juice into a jug to get rid of any bits.
                          3. Put the lemon pulp (from the straining) back into the food processor. Add the rest of the sugar, ice cubes and 500ml water and blitz again.
                          4. Strain it into the jug with the first lot of juice and discard the pulp.
                          5. Serve with slices of lemon and lots of ice.

                          Notes

                          Keep in mind that using brown sugar will result in a slightly different taste and colour compared to using caster sugar. Brown sugar has a deeper, more caramel-like flavour, while caster sugar has a cleaner, more neutral sweetness.

                          Nutrition (per serving)

                          Servings: 1.00 · Calories: 197.71 kcal · Carbs: 52.57 g · Protein: 0.90 g · Total fat: 0.24 g · Saturated fat: 0.03 g · Unsaturated fat: 0.21 g · Trans fat: 0.00 g · Sugars: 47.03 g · Fiber: 2.27 g · Cholesterol: 0.00 mg · Sodium: 1.26 mg

                          Saved from Lucy's CookBook app, no website source listed for this one.

                          Soup Mama Goose’s Recipes

                          Homemade Panera Broccoli Cheese Soup

                          Serves: 4 servings · Prep: 20 min · Cook: 30 min

                          Comfort in a bowl! Wanna round it out into a full meal? Serve this Homemade Panera Broccoli Cheese Soup in a breadbowl!

                          Ingredients

                          • 2 cups broccoli florets
                          • 2 cups chicken broth
                          • ½ cup of sharp cheddar cheese plus extra for topping [freshly grated]
                          • 1 heaping cup of regular gouda cheese [freshly grated]
                          • 1 cup fresh carrots
                          • 2 small white onion
                          • 2 large cloves of garlic
                          • 1 cup half and half
                          • 3 Tbsp all purpose flour
                          • 3 Tbsp butter
                          • 1 bay leaf
                          • ¼ tsp garlic powder
                          • ⅛ tsp ground nutmeg
                          • ⅛ tsp dried basil
                          • ⅛ tsp cayenne pepper
                          • ⅛ tsp salt
                          • dash of ground black pepper to taste
                          • 1 adapted from peas & crayons

                          Directions

                          1. Dice the onions, mince the garlic and shred the carrot.
                          2. For the broccoli:
                          3. Chop 2/3 of it into tiny florets and then take the remaining 1/3 and give it a rough chop so you have a few large pieces to add to the soup.
                          4. Place chicken broth, broccoli, onion, garlic, carrots, and bay leaf to a pot and cook over medium-high heat 8-10 minutes or until mixture comes to a boil.
                          5. Cook 4-6 minutes more or until broccoli is tender.
                          6. Once the broth is done, start your roux:
                          7. In a large pot, melt 3 tablespoons of butter on medium heat, whisking constantly.
                          8. Once melted, slowly add 3 tablespoons of flour as you continue to whisk.
                          9. Remove from heat and pour in the broth-veggie mixture and slowly stir in 1 cup of room temperature half and half.
                          10. Place on very low heat, uncovered.
                          11. Remove bay leaves from the pot and season with the above herbs and spices.
                          12. Add the cheddar and gouda cheese a handful at a time, stirring to melt the cheese after each addition.
                          13. Serve immediately in bowls or bread bowls.
                          14. Steam reserved broccoli in your microwave.
                          15. Garnish the soup with the reserved broccoli florets and a little extra grated cheddar.
                          16. Bon appétit!

                          Notes

                          To make this soup gluten-free you can use pureed potato to thicken the soup without using a flour-based roux or use sweet rice flour in place of all purpose flour.

                          Nutrition (per serving)

                          Calories: 300 · Fat: 19 · Carbs: 21 · Sugar: 0 · Fiber: 7 · Protein: 12 · Sodium: 1250 · Cholesterol: 65

                          Recipe from: sugarapron.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                          Dessert Cookies Mama Goose’s Recipes

                          Homemade Samoas (a.k.a. Caramel de-Lites)

                          Serves: 3 dozen cookies · Cook: 10 min

                          Samoas, also known as Caramel de-Lites, have always been my favorite girl scout cookie (although I do enjoy Do-Si-Dos, Tagalongs and Thin Mints). I’m not sure how they rank in the overall scheme of GS cookie popularity, but I will go so far as to say that I have never met someone who didn’t care for them at all – even people who say that they usually don’t like coconut. I think that the cookie is just a good and rather unique combination of flavors.

                          Ingredients

                          • 1 Cookies
                          • 1 cup butter, soft
                          • ½ cup sugar
                          • 2 cups all purpose flour
                          • ¼ tsp baking powder
                          • ½ tsp salt
                          • ½ tsp vanilla extract
                          • 1 up to 2 tbsp milk
                          • 1 Topping
                          • 3 cups shredded coconut, sweetened or unsweetened
                          • 12 oz good-quality chewy caramels
                          • ¼ tsp salt
                          • 3 tbsp milk
                          • 8 oz dark or semisweet chocolate, chocolate chips are ok

                          Directions

                          1. Preheat oven to 350F.
                          2. In a large mixing bowl, cream together butter and sugar. Mix in flour, baking powder and salt at a low speed, followed by the vanilla and milk, adding in the milk as needed to make the dough come together without being sticky (it’s possible you might not need to add milk at all). The dough should come together into a soft, not-too-sticky ball. Add in a bit of extra flour if your dough is very sticky.
                          3. Roll the dough (working in two or three batches) out between pieces of wax paper to about 1/4-inch thickness (or slightly less) and use a 1 1/2-inch cookie cutter to make rounds. Place on a parchment lined baking sheet and use a knife, or the end of a wide straw, to cut a smaller center hole. Repeat with remaining dough. Alternatively, use scant tablespoons of dough and press into an even layer in a mini donut pan to form the rounds.
                          4. Bake cookies for 10-12 minutes, until bottoms are lightly browned and cookies are set. If using a mini donut pan, bake for only about 10 minutes, until edges are light gold.
                          5. Cool for a few minutes on the baking sheet then transfer to a wire rack to cool completely.
                          6. Preheat oven to 300. Spread coconut evenly on a parchment-lined baking sheet (preferably one with sides) and toast 20 minutes, stirring every 5 minutes, until coconut is golden. Cool on baking sheet, stirring occasionally. Set aside.
                          7. Unwrap the caramels and place in a large microwave-safe bowl with milk and salt. Cook on high for 3-4 minutes, stopping to stir a few times to help the caramel melt. When smooth, fold in toasted coconut with a spatula.
                          8. Using the spatula or a small offset spatula, spread topping on cooled cookies, using about 2-3 tsp per cookie. Reheat caramel for a few seconds in the microwave if it gets too firm to work with.
                          9. While topping sets up, melt chocolate in a small bowl. Heat on high in the microwave in 445 second intervals, stirring thoroughly to prevent scorching. Dip the base of each cookie into the chocolate and place on a clean piece of parchment paper. Transfer all remaining chocolate (or melt a bit of additional chocolate, if necessary) into a piping bag or a ziplock bag with the corner snipped off and drizzle finished cookies with chocolate.
                          10. Let chocolate set completely before storing in an airtight container.

                          Notes

                          These cookies are fairly time consuming to make, but if you take your time and have fun with them, the results will be worth it. That said, if you want something a little bit quicker, try baking a batch of Samoas Bars instead, which require no rolling and cutting of the dough!

                          Recipe from: bakingbites.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.
