                                            Mexican Mama Goose’s Recipes

                                            Navajo Tacos

                                            Serves: 1 servings

                                            It's fair time and that means time to enjoy your favorite fried foods! One of my all time favorite fair foods is Navajo tacos, also called Indian tacos. I realize many people have probably never heard of Navajo tacos. It's a unique dish to my area and a local favorite. Usually when you order one of these tacos, the fry bread is as big as the plate and its almost impossible to eat the whole thing. It also comes with a healthy serving of red or green chili. The secret to these tacos is the fry bread. It's basically fried dough. And its good. You can eat the fry bread plain or add honey much like a sopapilla or enjoy it with a dusting of powdered sugar, but I like my fry bread served with the taco toppings. Do you have a favorite fair food such as candy apples, cotton candy, or funnel cake? I like them all! If you'd like to try Navajo tacos, just use the recipe below.

                                            Ingredients

                                            • 1 Fry Bread
                                            • 1 ¼ cup flour
                                            • 1 ½ teaspoon baking powder
                                            • ½ teaspoon salt
                                            • ½ cup milk
                                            • 2 cups vegetable oil for frying
                                            • 1 Chili
                                            • 1 pund ground beef
                                            • 1 can kidney beans, rinsed, 15 oz.
                                            • 1 packet low salt taco seasoning mix, 1.5 oz, or make your own with this homemade taco seasoning mix
                                            • ¾ cup water
                                            • 4 tablespoons tomato paste
                                            • Toppings:
                                            • 1 cheddar cheese, shredded
                                            • 1 lettuce, chopped
                                            • 1 onions, diced
                                            • 1 tomatoes, diced

                                            Directions

                                            1. Mix flour, baking powder, and salt in a mixing bowl. Add milk. Mix together to form dough. Kneed on lightly floured surface for five minutes or until smooth ball forms. Let dough rest.
                                            2. Brown beef in skillet. Drain. Add taco seasoning and water to skillet and stir into the beef. Cover and simmer on low heat about 10 minutes. Add drained kidney beans and tomato paste. Stir well. Keep covered on low heat stirring occasionally
                                            3. Cut ball of dough in half. With hands or rolling pin, flatten the dough. It should look like pizza dough and be about 1/2 inch thick.
                                            4. In separate skillet, heat vegetable oil. Gently place flattened dough one at a time into the hot oil. Cook dough for about one minute or until it just begins to turn golden brown. Using tongs, turn the dough over and cook the other side 30 seconds to a minute. Place on paper towels to drain excess oil.
                                            5. While fry bread is hot, top with warm chili, cheese, lettuce, onions, and tomatoes.

                                            Recipe from: thepinjunkie.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                            Freezer

                                            No. 175 Lizzie’s Bean and Cheese Burritos

                                            Serves: 4 burritos · Prep: 15 min · Cook: 20 min

                                            Ingredients

                                            • 1 tablespoon olive oil
                                            • 1 small yellow onion, diced
                                            • 1 jalapeño, seeded and minced
                                            • 2 cloves garlic, minced
                                            • 1 teaspoon ground cumin
                                            • 1 teaspoon chili powder
                                            • 1 can pinto beans, drained and rinsed, 15-ounce
                                            • 1 cup vegetable broth
                                            • 1 teaspoon kosher salt
                                            • ½ teaspoon black pepper
                                            • 4 large flour tortillas
                                            • 1 cup shredded Monterey Jack cheese
                                            • Optional toppings: sour cream, hot sauce, cilantro, avocado

                                            Directions

                                            1. Heat the olive oil in a large skillet or pot over medium heat. Add the onion and jalapeño and cook until softened, about 5-7 minutes.
                                            2. Add the garlic, cumin, and chili powder and cook for 1 minute more, until fragrant.
                                            3. Add the pinto beans and vegetable broth. Bring the mixture to a simmer and cook for 5 minutes, until the liquid has reduced slightly. Use a potato masher or the back of a fork to mash the beans until mostly smooth. Season with salt and pepper.
                                            4. Lay the tortillas on a clean surface. Divide the bean mixture evenly among the tortillas, spreading it in a line down the center of each. Top with the shredded cheese.
                                            5. Fold in the sides of each tortilla, then roll them up tightly from the bottom. If desired, you can pan-fry the burritos for a few minutes per side until golden brown and crispy.
                                            6. Serve immediately with your favorite toppings.

                                            Recipe from: open.substack.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                            Chicken 1 Pot

                                            One pot Honey Mustard Chicken and Rice

                                            Serves: 4 servings

                                            15 days of quick and easy comfort food Episode 1. One pot Honey Mustard Chicken and Rice

                                            Ingredients

                                            • 500 g chicken, diced
                                            • 1 tbs chicken seasoning
                                            • Salt and pepper - to taste
                                            • 1 onion, finely diced
                                            • 2 cloves garlic, finely grated
                                            • 2 tbs mustard - either dijon or English
                                            • 1 tbs Honey
                                            • 300 ml chicken stock
                                            • 120 ml @elmlea_ukie light single cream
                                            • 500 g of cooked rice- I used microwave rice
                                            • 120 g cheese- I use a mix of cheddar, Red Leicester and mozzarella.
                                            • Parsley to garnish

                                            Directions

                                            1. Fry off the chicken and the seasoning and cook for around 5 minutes or until browned.
                                            2. Add your onions into the same pan and cook until they’re soft, then add the garlic.
                                            3. Cook that for around 30 seconds before adding the mustard, honey, stock and cream.
                                            4. Cook for a couple of minutes and then add in the cooked rice.
                                            5. Season to taste and sprinkle on the cheese, pop it under the grill until bubbling and golden.
                                            6. Garnish, Serve and enjoy.

                                            Notes

                                            You could make it less calories/syns by swapping the cream for stock or another alternative but I love the creaminess of it.

                                            Nutrition (per serving)

                                            Calories: 557 · Protein: 51.5

                                            Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                            Mama Goose’s Recipes Casserole 1 Pot

                                            One-Pot Recipe: Hearty Chicken and Biscuit Casserole

                                            Serves: 8 servings

                                            This dish is the epitome of comfort food for us. There’s nothing better than a hearty chicken dish where your biscuits are built in and you get some with every bite! We initially assumed a meal like this would be complicated and messy, but not with this recipe: it’s basically a one-pot wonder, using only your Dutch oven (or casserole dish) and the assistance of your trusty food processor or mixer to create a full dinner with meat, veggies and a side. We’re looking out for you (as always) in trying to find ways to cut down the prep time and effort, that’s why we use rotisserie chicken here that deliver on flavor and don’t take away from the other ingredients. This dish is a crowd-pleaser and you won’t be disappointed; it comes together easily, and then you can kick back and relax while the oven works its magic!

                                            Ingredients

                                            • Chicken:
                                            • 3 cups rotisserie chicken, cubed
                                            • 1 large carrot, chopped
                                            • 1 cup frozen baby peas, thawed and drained
                                            • 2 ½ cups low-sodium chicken broth
                                            • 2 tablespoons unsalted butter
                                            • 2 tablespoons Parmesan cheese, grated
                                            • 1 tablespoon all-purpose flour
                                            • salt and pepper, to taste
                                            • Biscuits:
                                            • 4 tablespoons unsalted butter
                                            • 1 ¼ cups all-purpose flour
                                            • ¼ teaspoon salt
                                            • 1 ½ teaspoon baking powder
                                            • ½ cup whole milk

                                            Directions

                                            1. Preheat oven to 375º F.
                                            2. In a large Dutch oven over medium(-high) heat, melt butter and saute carrots until soft. About 8 minutes.
                                            3. Add 1/2 cup of chicken broth and stir until it’s almost evaporated. Then add flour and the rest of broth. Stir mixture and bring to a boil.
                                            4. Season with salt and pepper, and reduce heat to low, simmering until thickened. 3 minutes. Add chicken, peas and Parmesan, and stir to coat thoroughly with sauce.
                                            5. In a food processor, pulse together flour, butter, salt and baking powder.
                                            6. Slowly pour milk into mixture and pulse until a soft dough forms.
                                            7. Using a spoon, scoop dough balls and evenly cover the top of chicken with biscuit mixture.
                                            8. Bake uncovered for 30-40 minutes, or until biscuits are golden brown.
                                            9. Remove from oven and let cool for 10 minutes before serving.

                                            Recipe from: 12tomatoes.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                            Pasta 1 Pot

                                            One-Pot Skillet Pasta (Lazy Lasagna / Lasagna Soup)

                                            Serves: 1 servings

                                            This is a quick and simple makeshift lasagna without the noodles, perfect for busy parents. It's a one-pot skillet pasta that's toddler approved and can be made gluten-free with brown rice noodles.

                                            Ingredients

                                            • 1 diced onion
                                            • 1 olive oil
                                            • 4 cloves of minced garlic
                                            • 1 pound of ground beef
                                            • Italian seasoning
                                            • 1 salt
                                            • 1 pepper
                                            • 28 oz jar of pasta sauce
                                            • 3 cups of chicken broth, 2-3
                                            • 1 pasta, brown rice noodles for GF
                                            • ½ a tub of ricotta
                                            • 1 shredded mozzarella
                                            • 1 fresh basil

                                            Directions

                                            1. Sauté diced onion in olive oil over medium heat until it turns soft and translucent, 5-7 minutes.
                                            2. Add 4 cloves of minced garlic and cook just until fragrant, about 1 minute.
                                            3. Stir in a pound of ground beef + cook until browned and no longer pink, breaking it apart as it cooks.
                                            4. Season generously with Italian seasoning, salt, and pepper.
                                            5. Pour in your favorite 28oz jar of pasta sauce along with 2-3 cups of chicken broth, then add your favorite pasta straight into the pot.
                                            6. Stir, bring to a boil, then cover and reduce the heat to low. Let it simmer until the pasta is cooked through and the sauce thickens…add more chicken stock if needed.
                                            7. Once everything’s cooked through, stir in half a tub of ricotta.
                                            8. Top with a shredded mozzarella and finish with fresh basil.

                                            Notes

                                            Some of this I eyeball, but you get the gist of it. It’s so simple that you really can’t go wrong 🥰

                                            Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                            Pasta Mama Goose’s Recipes 1 Pot

                                            One-Pot Spinach Tomato Pasta

                                            Serves: 1 servings · Prep: 10 min · Cook: 25 min

                                            If you ask me, pasta dinners don’t get much better than this. This simple spinach tomato pasta is the kind of meal that just makes sense on busy weeknights. Fresh tomatoes and wilted spinach mix with perfectly cooked pasta, creating a dish that’s both comforting and light. It’s tossed with garlic and olive oil, then finished with a sprinkle of parmesan cheese that melts right into the warm noodles. A handful of fresh basil adds that little something extra that makes it feel special. It’s a no-fuss recipe that works just as well for family dinners as it does for casual get-togethers with friends.

                                            Ingredients

                                            • 14 ounces penne pasta
                                            • 4 cups chicken stock
                                            • 1 medium onion, chopped
                                            • 1 cup heavy whipping cream
                                            • 5 ounces fresh spinach, 4-5
                                            • 1 cup sun-dried tomatoes in olive oil, chopped
                                            • ½ cup freshly shredded parmesan cheese
                                            • 1 tablespoon sun-dried tomato oil
                                            • ⅓ cup tomato paste
                                            • 1 teaspoon red chili flakes
                                            • 5 garlic cloves, minced
                                            • ¼ cup fresh basil
                                            • 1 teaspoon kosher salt
                                            • 1 teaspoon black pepper

                                            Directions

                                            1. Sauté the vegetables:
                                            2. Heat a pot over medium-high heat and add 1 tablespoon of sun-dried tomato oil.
                                            3. Once the oil is warm, add the chopped onion and cook until slightly brown.
                                            4. Then, add the minced garlic and sun-dried tomatoes to the pot and cook for 2-3 minutes, stirring frequently to prevent the garlic from burning.
                                            5. Stir in the tomato paste and cook for an additional couple of minutes, continuing to stir.
                                            6. Build the sauce:
                                            7. Slowly add the chicken stock to the pot, mixing it in to deglaze the pan and blend with the tomato paste.
                                            8. Once well combined, pour in the heavy whipping cream and mix well.
                                            9. Add the black pepper, kosher salt, and red chili flakes, stirring to fully incorporate the seasonings into the sauce.
                                            10. Cook the pasta:
                                            11. Add the penne pasta to the pot and stir to incorporate it into the sauce.
                                            12. Reduce the heat to low-medium and cook uncovered for about 10 minutes, stirring frequently.
                                            13. Keep an eye on the pasta to ensure it cooks to about 75% done, checking for the desired texture.
                                            14. Add vegetables and complete cooking:
                                            15. Add the fresh spinach and basil to the pot, allowing the spinach to wilt down.
                                            16. If it seems like the spinach absorbs too much liquid, feel free to add a little more water or chicken stock for consistency.
                                            17. Once the spinach has wilted nicely, stir in the freshly shredded parmesan cheese until it is melted and well distributed throughout the dish.
                                            18. Finalize and serve:
                                            19. Turn off the heat and mix everything together until well combined and creamy.
                                            20. Serve the creamy tomato spinach pasta in bowls.
                                            21. Optionally, you can add grilled chicken on top for a more substantial meal.
                                            22. Enjoy your delicious, creamy pasta dish!

                                            Nutrition (per serving)

                                            Calories: 2100 · Fat: 150 · Carbs: 130 · Protein: 85

                                            Recipe from: mollyshomeguide.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                            Pasta Mama Goose’s Recipes

                                            One-Skillet Spicy Honey BBQ Sausage & Pasta

                                            Serves: 4 servings · Prep: 10 min · Cook: 20 min

                                            Craving something bold, comforting, and super easy to make? This One-Skillet Spicy Honey BBQ Sausage & Pasta has got you covered. It’s a smoky, sweet, and spicy dish that combines caramelized sausage, tender pasta, and a luscious honey BBQ sauce—all cooked in just one skillet. Perfect for a quick dinner, satisfying lunch, or even meal prep. Whether you love a bit of heat or prefer a milder kick, this recipe is customizable and always delicious. Try this irresistible pasta dish that’s sure to become a family favorite!

                                            Ingredients

                                            • 12 oz Rotini Pasta
                                            • 12 oz Spicy Sausage, sliced, Andouille or Italian
                                            • 1 tbsp Olive Oil
                                            • 3 cloves Garlic, minced
                                            • 1 cup BBQ Sauce, smoky or honey-based
                                            • 2 tbsp Honey
                                            • 1 tsp Paprika
                                            • ½ tsp Chili Powder or Cayenne Pepper, adjust to taste
                                            • Salt & Pepper, to taste
                                            • Fresh Parsley, chopped, for garnish

                                            Directions

                                            1. Cook the pasta:
                                            2. Bring a large pot of salted water to a boil. Cook the rotini pasta according to package instructions until al dente. Drain and set aside.
                                            3. Cook the sausage:
                                            4. Heat olive oil in a large skillet over medium-high heat. Add sliced sausage and cook until browned and caramelized, about 5–7 minutes. Remove sausage from skillet and set aside.
                                            5. Sauté Garlic: In the same skillet, add a bit more olive oil if needed. Add minced garlic and sauté for about 1 minute, until fragrant and golden.
                                            6. Make the sauce:
                                            7. Add BBQ sauce, honey, paprika, chili powder, salt, and pepper to the skillet. Stir well and simmer for 2–3 minutes until slightly thickened.
                                            8. Combine and toss:
                                            9. Return the cooked sausage to the skillet along with the drained pasta. Toss everything until the pasta and sausage are evenly coated.
                                            10. Garnish and serve:
                                            11. Sprinkle fresh parsley over the top and serve hot. Enjoy!

                                            Notes

                                            Don’t Overcook the Pasta: Slightly undercook it before tossing it in the skillet. It’ll finish cooking in the sauce, absorbing more flavor. Use Smoky BBQ Sauce: Opt for a smoky or honey-based BBQ sauce for the best flavor. Slice Sausage Evenly: Keeping the sausage pieces uniform ensures they cook at the same rate. Adjust Spice Level to Taste: Add or reduce chili powder or cayenne pepper depending on your heat tolerance. Use Fresh Garlic: It makes a noticeable difference in the depth of flavor compared to garlic powder. Finish with Fresh Herbs: A sprinkle of fresh parsley or even chopped chives brightens up the dish. Cook Over Medium-High Heat: It helps caramelize the sausage and thicken the sauce nicely. Serve Immediately: This dish is best enjoyed hot and fresh.

                                            Nutrition (per serving)

                                            Calories: 520 · Fat: 18 · Saturated fat: 6 · Carbs: 65 · Sugar: 18 · Fiber: 4 · Protein: 22 · Sodium: 1280 · Cholesterol: 55

                                            Recipe from: cookcraze.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                            Pasta 1 Pot

                                            One-Tray Caramelized Onion & Garlic Pasta 🔥

                                            Serves: 1 slice

                                            Minimal effort, max comfort. Sweet, savory, a little spicy... this one has all the flavor.

                                            Ingredients

                                            • 2 large onions, thinly sliced
                                            • 1 whole garlic head
                                            • 2 tbsp chili crisp @momofukugoods
                                            • 1 ½ cups of cream or coconut milk, room temp
                                            • 1 tbsp soy sauce, optional
                                            • ½ cup fresh Parmesan cheese, grated
                                            • ½ lemon, juiced
                                            • 12 oz pasta of choice, 340 g
                                            • 5 tbsp olive oil
                                            • 1 Pasta water, as needed
                                            • Seasonings:
                                            • 1 tbsp paprika
                                            • 2 tsp garlic powder
                                            • 2 tsp salt
                                            • 2 tsp pepper
                                            • 2 tsp italian seasoning

                                            Directions

                                            1. Preheat oven to 400°F (200°C). Add sliced onions to a baking dish. Toss with paprika, garlic powder, salt, pepper, Italian seasoning, and 4 tbsp olive oil until well coated.
                                            2. Slice the top off the garlic head (about ¼ inch), drizzle with 1 tbsp olive oil, sprinkle with salt, and place it cut-side up on top of the onions.
                                            3. Cover with foil and bake for 1 hour, tossing the onions every 20 minutes (twice total). Each time, add a splash of water to prevent burning. You’re looking for deep golden, jammy onions.
                                            4. While that magic is happening, cook pasta according to package instructions. Reserve up to 1 cup of pasta water before draining.
                                            5. Remove garlic from the dish and let cool slightly (freezer hack = elite move). Squeeze the roasted garlic into the onions.
                                            6. Add chili crisp, cream (or coconut milk), soy sauce (if using), lemon juice, Parmesan, and cooked pasta. Toss everything together, adding some pasta water as needed until glossy and saucy. Taste and adjust salt if needed.
                                            7. Finish with extra Parmesan, chili crisp, and fresh parsley if you’re feeling fancy.

                                            Notes

                                            Best served with with chicken, shrimp, or tofu 🫡

                                            Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                            Dessert

                                            Oreo Tres Leches Cake

                                            Serves: 1 servings

                                            Ingredients

                                            • 1 Cake
                                            • 1 box Betty Crocker Super Moist white or vanilla cake mix
                                            • 1 Ingredients listed on the box, usually eggs, oil, and water
                                            • 1 cup mini Oreos, crushed, mix into the batter
                                            • Tres leches soak:
                                            • 1 can sweetened condensed milk, 14 oz
                                            • 1 can evaporated milk, 12 oz
                                            • ½ cup heavy cream, or whole milk if you prefer lighter
                                            • Optional:
                                            • 1 tsp vanilla extract
                                            • Topping:
                                            • 1 tub Cool Whip, thawed, 8 oz
                                            • ½ cup crushed Oreos, mix into Cool Whip
                                            • 1 Mini Oreos + extra crushed Oreos for decoration

                                            Directions

                                            1. Preheat oven to 350°F (325°F for dark/nonstick pans).
                                            2. Prepare cake mix according to the box directions.
                                            3. Fold in 1 cup crushed mini Oreos.
                                            4. Pour batter into a greased 9×13 inch pan.
                                            5. Bake as directed on the box (usually ~30 minutes) or until a toothpick inserted in the center comes out clean.
                                            6. Let the cake cool completely.
                                            7. Prepare the Tres Leches Mixture. In a medium bowl, whisk together sweetened condensed milk, evaporated milk, heavy cream, and vanilla extract until fully combined.
                                            8. Once the cake is cooled, use a fork or skewer to poke holes all over the surface.
                                            9. Slowly pour the milk mixture evenly over the entire cake, letting it soak in.
                                            10. Cover and refrigerate for at least 4 hours, or overnight for the best texture.
                                            11. In a medium bowl, fold ½ cup crushed Oreos into the Cool Whip.
                                            12. Spread evenly over the chilled, soaked cake.
                                            13. Sprinkle with more crushed Oreos. Add mini Oreos on top for garnish. Keep refrigerated until ready to serve , it’s best served cold!

                                            Notes

                                            Callate mmg

                                            Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                            Dessert Cookies Mama Goose’s Recipes

                                            Peanut Butter and Jelly Cookie Recipe! (5 Ingredients)

                                            Serves: 24 cookies · Prep: 15 min · Cook: 8 min

                                            This easy Peanut Butter and Jelly Cookie Recipe serves up your favorite nostalgic flavors… in the form of a cookie! Plus, with just 5 ingredients, they’re SO easy to make!

                                            Ingredients

                                            • 1 cup Jif Creamy Peanut Butter OR this Homemade Peanut Butter Recipe
                                            • ⅓ cup Smucker's Raspberry Preserves
                                            • 1 cup Sugar + 1/4 cup Sugar
                                            • 1 Egg
                                            • 1 tsp Vanilla Extract

                                            Directions

                                            1. Mix Peanut Butter, 1 cup Sugar, Egg and Vanilla together in medium sized mixing bowl with large spoon. Stir well.
                                            2. Place dough in fridge, and refrigerate dough for 45 minutes – 1 hour.
                                            3. While waiting for dough to chill, line cookie sheets with Parchment Paper, and pour 1/4 cup sugar into small bowl and set aside.
                                            4. After dough has been chilled, roll into small tablespoon sized balls, then roll around in small sugar bowl.
                                            5. Place on parchment lined cookie sheets, approx. 2 inches apart. (Do not flatten dough balls.)
                                            6. Bake at 350 degrees for 8 – 10 minutes, or until done.
                                            7. Immediately after removing from oven, gently push down in the center of the cookie making an indent with your thumb.
                                            8. Allow to cool slightly, then transfer to cooling rack to finish cooling.
                                            9. Once cookies have cooled, microwave jam for 30 seconds in a microwave safe dish and stir until consistency softens up a bit.
                                            10. Carefully spoon a dollop of jam into the center of each cookie. Once jam sets it's time to ENJOY!

                                            Notes

                                            When storing cookies make sure to store in a single layer. For answers to Frequently Asked Questions or tips for common substitutions and variations, please refer to the detailed information above this recipe card.

                                            Recipe from: thefrugalgirls.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                            Cookies Mama Goose’s Recipes

                                            Peanut Butter Blossoms

                                            Serves: 80 servings · Prep: 30 min · Cook: 12 min

                                            Peanut Butter Blossoms – sometimes known as Hershey Kiss Cookies or Peanut Butter Kiss Cookies – are the perfect classic holiday cookie to make with kids or bring to a cookie exchange.

                                            Ingredients

                                            • 1 cup shortening
                                            • 1 cup peanut butter
                                            • 1 cup packed brown sugar
                                            • 1 cup granulated sugar plus 1/2 cup for rolling
                                            • 2 eggs
                                            • ¼ cup milk
                                            • 2 teaspoons pure vanilla extract
                                            • 3 ½ cups all-purpose flour
                                            • 2 teaspoons baking soda
                                            • 1 teaspoon fine sea salt
                                            • 18 ounces milk chocolate candy kisses, unwrapped

                                            Directions

                                            1. Preheat oven to 375 degrees F. Line two baking sheets with parchment paper.
                                            2. In a large bowl, cream together shortening, peanut butter, brown sugar, and 1 cup granulated sugar until smooth. Add in the eggs one at a time, beating until fully incorporated. Mix in milk and vanilla.
                                            3. In a large bowl, combine flour, baking soda, and salt. Gradually add to the peanut butter mixture until well blended.
                                            4. Shape tablespoonfuls of dough into balls, and roll in remaining granulated sugar. Place cookies 2 inches apart on the prepared baking sheets.
                                            5. Bake in preheated oven for 10 to 12 minutes. Remove from oven, and immediately press a chocolate kiss into each cookie. Allow to cool completely; the kiss will harden as it cools.

                                            Notes

                                            This makes a lot of cookies, but it’s perfect for cookie exchange prep or holiday parties. I have not attempted this recipe using butter instead of shortening. Store cookies in an airtight container at room temperature for up to 3 days.

                                            Nutrition (per serving)

                                            Serving Size: 1 · Calories: 83 · Fat: 4 · Saturated fat: 1 · Unsaturated fat: 3 · Carbs: 10 · Sugar: 5 · Protein: 1 · Sodium: 76 · Cholesterol: 6

                                            Recipe from: mybakingaddiction.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                            Peanut Noodles

                                            Serves: 4 servings · Prep: 5 min · Cook: 10 min

                                            These Peanut Noodles are covered in a creamy peanut sauce for a unique Asian inspired Thai dish that will have your family begging for more. Sometimes these peanut butter noodles are called beggar’s noodles because of its simple ingredients like spaghetti and peanut butter.

                                            Ingredients

                                            • 8 ounces spaghetti
                                            • 2 tablespoons toasted sesame oil
                                            • 1 bunch green onions, sliced, white parts only - reserve green parts for garnish
                                            • 1 teaspoon minced fresh ginger, see Note
                                            • ⅓ cup creamy peanut butter, use crunchy peanut butter if you want nuts in it
                                            • ¼ cup low-sodium soy sauce
                                            • ¼ cup hot water
                                            • 1 Tablespoon cider vinegar
                                            • 1 teaspoon sugar
                                            • ¼ teaspoon crushed red pepper flakes

                                            Directions

                                            1. Cook spaghetti in a large pot of salted water until al dente according to package directions. Drain.
                                            2. While noodles are cooking, prepare the creamy sauce. In a small skillet, heat sesame oil over low heat Add the onions (white parts only) and cook until tender. Add the ginger and cook for about 1 minute.
                                            3. Increase the heat to medium and stir in the stir in remaining sauce ingredients: peanut butter, soy sauce, water, vinegar, sugar, and pepper. Stir until combined. Remove from heat.
                                            4. Toss the hot noodles with sauce and garnish with sliced green onions and crunchy peanuts if desired. Store leftovers in an airtight container.

                                            Notes

                                            *Fresh ginger can be frozen until ready to use. I don't use a lot of ginger so this is a trick I use often.

                                            Cookies Mama Goose’s Recipes

                                            Pecan Shortbread Christmas Tree Cookies

                                            Serves: 32 servings · Prep: 30 min

                                            Butter, flour and sugar come together to create rich, delicate cookies.

                                            Ingredients

                                            • 1 cup butter, softened
                                            • ⅔ cup powdered sugar
                                            • ½ teaspoon vanilla
                                            • 5 drops green food color
                                            • 1 ¾ cups Gold Medal™ all-purpose flour
                                            • ½ cup coarsely chopped pecans
                                            • 32 pecan halves
                                            • ¾ cup white vanilla baking chips
                                            • 1 Granulated sugar, if desired
                                            • 32 yellow decorating stars, if desired

                                            Directions

                                            1. Heat oven to 325°F. Spray or lightly grease 2 large cookie sheets. In large bowl, beat butter and powdered sugar with electric mixer on medium speed until light and fluffy. Beat in vanilla and food color. On low speed, beat in flour just until mixed. Stir in chopped pecans.
                                            2. Divide dough into 4 equal parts; shape each into ball. Place 2 balls of dough on each cookie sheet, on opposite ends. With rolling pin or floured fingers, gently flatten and shape each ball into 6-inch circle. With large knife, divide and cut each round into 8 wedges, slightly separating each cut with knife. Poke tops of wedges with fork, and place 1 pecan half in middle of each outer edge to make tree trunk.
                                            3. Bake 15 to 18 minutes or until firm but not brown. While still warm, cut into wedges again. Cool completely on cookie sheets, about 30 minutes.
                                            4. Place cooled tree wedges on cooling racks or waxed paper. Place baking chips in small resealable freezer plastic bag. Microwave on High 40 to 60 seconds, turning bag over after 30 seconds. Squeeze bag gently until chips are melted and smooth. Cut small tip off one corner of bag, and drizzle side to side over wedges to make tree garland. Sprinkle with sugar Top each tree with star.

                                            Nutrition (per serving)

                                            Serving Size: 1 · Calories: 160 · Fat: 11 · Saturated fat: 6 · Trans fat: 0 · Carbs: 15 · Sugar: 9 · Fiber: 0 · Protein: 1 · Sodium: 65 · Cholesterol: 15

                                            Recipe from: bettycrocker.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                            Pasta Mama Goose’s Recipes

                                            Penne alla Carbonara

                                            Serves: 1 servings · Prep: 20 min · Cook: 40 min

                                            Bacon, eggs, and cream, tossed with pasta and finished with cracked black pepper. A Classic dish!

                                            Ingredients

                                            • ½ pound bacon, cut in to 1” pieces
                                            • ½ cup onion, finely chopped
                                            • ¼ cup roasted red pepper, diced
                                            • 4 garlic cloves, finely chopped
                                            • 3 large egg yolks
                                            • 1 cup freshly grated Parmigiano-Reggiano
                                            • 1 Freshly ground black pepper
                                            • 2 tablespoons finely chopped fresh parsley

                                            Directions

                                            1. Cooks note:
                                            2. Cook the pasta while you prepare the sauce. It is important that the pasta be hot when the raw eggs are added in order to finish the sauce.
                                            3. Preparation:
                                            4. Ladle about 1 cup of pasta water into a bowl and reserve.
                                            5. Separate the eggs. Place the yolks in a small bowl with the grated cheese and mix well. (Hold to the side to be added to the hot pasta).
                                            6. In a large sauté pan, over medium heat, cook the bacon until crispy. Remove the bacon and drain on paper towels. Pour off all of the oil except for about 3 tablespoons. Add the onion and red pepper. Sauté until onions become translucent. Add garlic and cook another 30 seconds. Add the crispy bacon, drained pasta (do not rinse), and fresh ground black pepper. Mix well, turning pasta to coat with oil. Cook for about 1 minute. Remove pan from heat.
                                            7. Off of the heat, add one half of the parsley and the egg / cheese mixture. Stir well to allow hot the hot pasta to cook the egg and create the sauce. Add reserved pasta water as needed to create a creamy consistency. Top with the remaining chopped parsley, grated cheese and ground black pepper.

                                            Notes

                                            Cook the pasta while you prepare the sauce. It is important that the pasta be hot when the raw eggs are added in order to finish the sauce.

                                            Recipe from: keyingredient.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                            Pesto Caprese Grilled Cheese

                                            Serves: 1 servings

                                            This is my all time favorite grilled cheese sandwich in the summer. With fresh garden basil pesto, delicious vine ripened tomatoes, and natural grated mozzarella cheese, you cannot go wrong. This is definitely not yo Mama's grilled cheese!

                                            Ingredients

                                            • 1 bread
                                            • 1 butter softened
                                            • 1 classic basil pesto
                                            • 1 grated mozzarella cheese
                                            • 1 sliced garden tomatoes

                                            Directions

                                            1. Completely butter one side of each slice of bread.
                                            2. Then spread pesto on the opposite side of each slice.
                                            3. Next heat pan or griddle over medium heat.
                                            4. Place one slice of bread on the griddle buttered side down.
                                            5. Next layer with fresh grated mozzarella, garden sliced tomatoes, and top with a little more cheese.
                                            6. Now add the remaining slice of bread, butter side up! Adjust heat as needed, and cook until golden brown, about 2-4 minutes.
                                            7. Then flip and cook until golden brown on the other side, about 2-4 minutes.
                                            8. Enjoy!

                                            Appetizers

                                            Pigs in a Blanket

                                            Serves: 28 mini crescent dogs · Cook: 15 min

                                            Pigs in a blanket are one of the easiest appetizers that are both kid-approved and adult-approved. Great for parties of all kinds or as a quick snack or weeknight dinner!

                                            Ingredients

                                            • 8 ounces refrigerated crescent rolls 1 can of 8 rolls
                                            • 14 ounces cocktail sausages
                                            • 1 Tablespoon butter melted
                                            • 1 teaspoon flaked sea salt optional

                                            Directions

                                            1. Preheat oven to 375°F and line a baking sheet with parchment paper.
                                            2. Remove crescent rolls from packaging and gently unroll, taking care to keep the perforated sheet intact.
                                            3. Press over the seams gently to form a single sheet of crescent roll dough.
                                            4. Use a pizza wheel to slice the crescent sheet into four sections and then slice each section into 7 triangles. (The triangles don’t have to be perfect or sized exactly the same – approximate triangles are just fine.)
                                            5. Place one cocktail sausage aligned with the wide end of each triangle. Roll up the sausage to form a small crescent roll surrounding the sausage.
                                            6. Place prepared “pigs in a blanket” on the prepared baking sheet.
                                            7. Brush the tops of each roll with melted butter and top with a sprinkle of flaked/coarse salt, if desired.
                                            8. Bake for 15 minutes or until the crescent rolls are puffed and golden brown.
                                            9. Serve hot with ketchup, mustard, and any of your favorite dipping sauces.

                                            Notes

                                            The salt is optional, but it really adds a nice salty contrast to the sweetness in the crescent roll dough. Brushing the pigs in a blanket with melted butter helps them to achieve a nice golden brown color and it also helps the salt stick to the top of the roll. Salted or unsalted butter will work find to brush over the crescent dough. You could also use an egg wash. One can of crescent dough is enough to cover about 1 full 14 oz. package of sausages. Double, triple, or quadruple the recipe as needed – these little snacks go fast! Leave a little room between sausages on the baking sheet as the dough does puff up around the sausage and you don’t want them bumping into one another. Some stores sell crescent roll SHEETS – if you can find one of these, you can eliminate the step of pressing the perforated seams together. Simply slice the sheet into 28 small triangles. You can even just cut small strips of the dough, though I like the more traditional crescent roll appearance.

                                            Nutrition (per serving)

                                            Serving Size: 1 · Calories: 75 · Fat: 6 · Saturated fat: 2 · Trans fat: 0.02 · Carbs: 3 · Sugar: 1 · Protein: 2 · Sodium: 212 · Cholesterol: 10

                                            Recipe from: amandascookin.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                            Dessert Pie

                                            Pineapple Cream Cheesecake

                                            Serves: 8 servings · Prep: 20 min · Cook: 70 min

                                            This Pineapple Cream Cheesecake is a tropical twist on the classic cheesecake, featuring a creamy, smooth filling with the sweetness of pineapple, crunchy pecans, and toasted coconut. Perfect for any occasion, this dessert will impress your guests with its delicious layers and tropical flair. Whether it’s for a holiday or a weeknight treat, this pineapple-infused cheesecake will become a favorite in your dessert repertoire.

                                            Ingredients

                                            • For the crust:
                                            • 1 ½ cups graham cracker crumbs
                                            • ¼ cup sugar
                                            • ½ cup melted butter
                                            • For the cheesecake filling:
                                            • 24 oz cream cheese, softened
                                            • 1 cup sugar
                                            • 1 teaspoon vanilla extract
                                            • 3 eggs
                                            • 1 cup crushed pineapple, drained
                                            • For the topping:
                                            • ½ cup sour cream
                                            • ½ cup heavy cream
                                            • ¼ cup toasted coconut flakes
                                            • ¼ cup chopped pecans
                                            • 1 Pineapple chunks for topping

                                            Directions

                                            1. Prepare the crust:
                                            2. Preheat your oven to 325°F (165°C).
                                            3. In a medium bowl, combine graham cracker crumbs, sugar, and melted butter. Mix until well combined.
                                            4. Press the mixture firmly into the bottom of a 9-inch springform pan to form the crust.
                                            5. Make the cheesecake filling:
                                            6. In a large bowl, beat the softened cream cheese until smooth.
                                            7. Add the sugar and vanilla extract, and continue beating until combined.
                                            8. Add the eggs one at a time, beating well after each addition.
                                            9. Gently fold in the crushed pineapple, making sure it’s evenly incorporated.
                                            10. Assemble and bake:
                                            11. Pour the cream cheese mixture over the prepared crust, spreading it evenly with a spatula.
                                            12. Bake in the preheated oven for 60-70 minutes, or until the center is set with a slight jiggle.
                                            13. Once baked, remove from the oven and allow the cheesecake to cool on a wire rack.
                                            14. Refrigerate for at least 4 hours, or overnight for best results.
                                            15. Prepare the topping:
                                            16. In a small bowl, mix the sour cream and heavy cream until smooth.
                                            17. Spread the cream mixture over the chilled cheesecake layer.
                                            18. Garnish and serve:
                                            19. Sprinkle the top with toasted coconut flakes and chopped pecans for added crunch.
                                            20. Add pineapple chunks for a tropical finishing touch.
                                            21. Slice and serve chilled.

                                            Notes

                                            Make sure to drain the pineapple well to prevent the cheesecake from becoming watery. This cheesecake is best when chilled for at least 4 hours or overnight. If you prefer a nut-free version, you can skip the pecans and toasted coconut, or substitute with another nut of your choice.

                                            Nutrition (per serving)

                                            Serving Size: 8 · Calories: 450

                                            Recipe from: recipessmile.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                            Dessert Mexican

                                            Postre de queso y frutas frescas extra delicioso 😋

                                            Serves: 1 servings

                                            Postre fácil con frutas 🍇

                                            Ingredients

                                            • 1 12-ounce can of evaporated milk
                                            • 1 8-ounce stick of cream cheese, chilled
                                            • 1 14-ounce can sweetened condensed milk, or to taste
                                            • 1 Vanilla to taste, approximately 1 teaspoon
                                            • 1 Yellow food coloring, optional, lemon yellow gel
                                            • 2 cans of peaches in syrup, 425 grams each, total 850 grams
                                            • 3 apples, 2 red, 1 yellow
                                            • 250 grams of fresh strawberries
                                            • 200 grams of green seedless grapes
                                            • 1 cup of raisins, to taste
                                            • 1 cup of chopped walnuts

                                            Directions

                                            1. In a blender, add the evaporated milk, the cold cream cheese stick, and the sweetened condensed milk. You can adjust the amount of condensed milk to your liking. Add about a teaspoon of vanilla.
                                            2. Optionally, add yellow food coloring (lemon yellow gel) for a more vibrant hue. Blend for approximately 2 minutes or until the cream cheese is well combined with the milk.
                                            3. Pour the mixture into a large container.
                                            4. One can of peaches in syrup will be added along with the syrup. The other can of peaches will be drained and added last.
                                            5. Wash and disinfect the apples, strawberries, and grapes. Cut large grapes in half. If the strawberries are large, cut them into quarters. Cut the apples into chunks; you can leave the skin on for a more attractive appearance.
                                            6. Immediately after cutting the fruit, add it to the milk and cream cheese smoothie to prevent it from browning. Mix well to coat the fruit.
                                            7. Add one cup of raisins and one cup of chopped walnuts. Mix well.
                                            8. Finally, add the can of peaches in syrup from which you drained all the syrup. Mix everything well.
                                            9. Refrigerate the dessert for a few hours before serving for a creamier consistency and better flavor.

                                            Recipe from: youtu.be, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                            Dessert

                                            Powdered Cranberries: The TikTok Viral Holiday Treat You Need to Try

                                            Serves: 1 servings

                                            Move over classic holiday snacks—TikTok Viral Candied Cranberries are the treat everyone is raving about this season! These candied cranberries strike the perfect balance between tart and sweet, thanks to a flavorful soak in Sprite and pineapple juice that softens their natural tartness. Coated in snowy powdered sugar, they’re a festive and irresistible addition to any holiday celebration.

                                            Ingredients

                                            • 12 oz bag of fresh cranberries
                                            • 1 small bottle or a can of Sprite, 8 oz
                                            • 1 small can of pineapple juice, 6 oz
                                            • 1 Approximately 2 cups of powdered sugar, adjust as needed for full coating

                                            Directions

                                            1. Place the cranberries in a large bowl and pour in the Sprite and pineapple juice. Cover the bowl, gently shake to mix, and refrigerate overnight to let the flavors infuse.
                                            2. Drain the cranberries, reserving the juice for a cocktail or mocktail. Transfer the cranberries to a large zip-top bag and add powdered sugar, shaking gently until the cranberries are fully coated. Add as much powdered sugar as needed to fully coat them.
                                            3. Preheat the oven to 200°F. Line a baking sheet with parchment paper and spread the coated cranberries in a single layer on the sheet.
                                            4. Place the baking sheet in the oven for 5–7 minutes to dry the cranberries. Allow them to cool completely, then store them in an airtight container in the fridge or a cool, dark pantry for up to one week.

                                            Notes

                                            Plan Ahead: Let the cranberries soak overnight for the best flavor. This step is key to creating that juicy, flavor-packed center. Work in Batches: When coating with powdered sugar, work in smaller batches to ensure every cranberry is evenly covered. Watch the Oven: Drying the cranberries in the oven is a quick step—keep an eye on them to avoid melting the sugar. Experiment with Juices: If you don’t have pineapple juice, try orange juice for a different citrus twist.

                                            Recipe from: sparklestosprinkles.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                            Baked Goods Dessert

                                            pumpkin cinnamon pull-apart bread

                                            Serves: 16 biscuit portions · Cook: 35 min

                                            pumpkin cinnamon pull-apart bread 🎃

                                            Ingredients

                                            • 1 Can of biscuits
                                            • 1 cup sugar
                                            • 1 tbsp cinnamon
                                            • 1 tsp pumpkin pie spice
                                            • ½ cup pumpkin puree
                                            • 1 stick of butter melted.

                                            Directions

                                            1. Open the biscuits, and split in half so there are now 16 biscuit portions.
                                            2. mix the sugar, cinnamon, & pumpkin spice in a shallow bowl, and melt the butter into another bowl.
                                            3. Add pumpkin puree to separate bowl and add in 1-2 tbsp of the cinnamon sugar mixture to combine.
                                            4. Dip each piece of biscuit in the butter, and then the cinnamon sugar until full coated.
                                            5. Stack the biscuits on top of each other with about 1/2-1tsp of pumpkin puree between each biscuit and place sideways into a loaf pan.
                                            6. Bake at 350 for 35-40 mins until baked through.

                                            Pumpkin Pie

                                            Serves: 8 servings · Prep: 20 min · Cook: 60 min

                                            Our best Pumpkin Pie recipe is the one to make, regardless of your baking skills. The easy, no-roll crust and spiced pumpkin filling are irresistible.

                                            Ingredients

                                            • 1 Pat-in-the-Pan Pastry
                                            • 1 ⅓ cups Gold Medal™ All Purpose Flour
                                            • ½ teaspoon salt
                                            • ⅓ cup vegetable oil
                                            • 2 tablespoons cold water
                                            • 1 Filling
                                            • 2 large eggs
                                            • ½ cup sugar
                                            • 1 teaspoon ground cinnamon
                                            • ½ teaspoon salt
                                            • ½ teaspoon ground ginger
                                            • ⅛ teaspoon ground cloves
                                            • 1 can pumpkin, 15 oz, not pumpkin pie mix
                                            • 1 can evaporated milk, 12 oz
                                            • 1 Sweetened Whipped Cream
                                            • ¾ cup whipping cream
                                            • 2 tablespoons sugar

                                            Directions

                                            1. Heat oven to 425°F.
                                            2. In medium bowl, mix flour, 1/2 teaspoon salt, and the oil with fork until all flour is moistened. Sprinkle with cold water, 1 tablespoon at a time, tossing with fork until all water is absorbed.
                                            3. Shape pastry into a ball. Press in bottom and up side of 9-inch glass pie plate.
                                            4. In large bowl, beat eggs slightly with wire whisk or electric mixer. Beat in 1/2 cup sugar, the cinnamon, 1/2 teaspoon salt, ginger, cloves, pumpkin, and milk.
                                            5. To prevent spilling, place pastry-lined pie plate on oven rack before adding filling. Carefully pour pumpkin filling into pie plate. Bake 15 minutes.
                                            6. Reduce oven temperature to 350°F. Bake about 45 minutes longer or until knife inserted in center comes out clean. Place pie on cooling rack. Cool completely, about 2 hours.
                                            7. In chilled medium bowl, beat whipping cream and 2 tablespoons sugar with electric mixer on high speed until stiff peaks form. Serve pie with whipped cream. Store in refrigerator.

                                            Notes

                                            For this Pumpkin Pie recipe, work quickly as you mix up and press the crust into the pie plate. Be sure your pie plate is a regular 9-inch pie plate versus a larger deep pie plate, as you want the crust to press up high enough on the sides to hold the filling. Read the canned pumpkin label carefully to ensure you’re not buying canned pumpkin pie mix, which already contains seasonings and sweeteners. Stir up your own Pumpkin Pie spice to keep in your pantry—it’s not only delicious in a pie but it’s wonderful sprinkled over root vegetables before roasting them or to season caramelized nuts during the holidays. Mix 3 tablespoons ground cinnamon, 2 teaspoons ground gin... Think ahead and freeze spoonfuls of whipped cream to use later. Just drop whipped cream by 8 spoonfuls onto waxed paper-lined cookie sheet. Freeze uncovered at least 2 hours. Then, place frozen mounds of whipped cream in a freezer container. Cover tightly and freeze no longer than 2 months. Create your own whipped cream like a barista: beat heavy cream with a splash of flavored coffee syrup instead of sugar. Caramel, hazelnut, or French vanilla are great flavor partners with Pumpkin Pie. With a few simple substitutions, you can create a lighter pumpkin pie filling that still has all the delicious flavor you’re craving. First, swap the 2 eggs for 3 egg whites or 1/2 cup of fat-free egg product. Next, opt for 1 can (12 oz) of fat-free evaporated milk inst...

                                            Pumpkin Soup

                                            Serves: 4 servings · Prep: 15 min · Cook: 40 min

                                            The perfect winter warmer

                                            Ingredients

                                            • 1.5 tbsp olive oil
                                            • 0.5 tsp ground cumin
                                            • 1 medium onion, diced
                                            • 4 tbsp light cream, to top
                                            • 0.5 tsp nutmeg
                                            • 1 vegtable stock cube
                                            • 1 medium potato, peeled and diced
                                            • 0.5 tsp ground coriander
                                            • 500 ml water
                                            • 1 pinch salt and pepper, to taste
                                            • 750 g pumpkin, peeled and diced
                                            • 1 clove garlic, crushed

                                            Directions

                                            1. Dice the onion, peel the potato and dice. If using a whole pumpkin use a potato peeler to remove the skin. Cut the pumpkin in half then using a metal spoon remove the pumpkin seeds. Cut the pumpkin into 2 inch pieces.
                                            2. In a large saucepan heat up the oil on medium-low heat, add the onion and cook until soft. Add the crushed garlic and spices, cook for a further 30 seconds ensuring to continue stirring the mix
                                            3. Add the chicken stock cube and water to the pan, along with the potato and pumpkin. Bring to the boil then simmer for 25 minutes (around 90ºC) or until the pumpkin and potatoes are cooked through.
                                            4. Once cooked through allow the mix to cool. Put it all into a blender along with the cream, blend until smooth.
                                            5. Reheat and serve

                                              Dessert Ninja Creami

                                              REAL FOOD LEMON FROZEN CUSTARD

                                              Serves: 2 servings

                                              REAL FOOD LEMON FROZEN CUSTARD and not a fake sweetener or pudding mix packet in sight! This is one of my favorite recipes in ICE CREAM ESOTERICA, our protein rich Ninja Creami recipe book - comment “book” if you’d like the 🔗 to this and 25 more recipes!

                                              Ingredients

                                              • 1 cup milk of choice
                                              • 1 cup non fat greek yogurt
                                              • 3 tablespoons sugar
                                              • 2 egg yolks
                                              • 1 Zest from 1.5 Meyer lemons
                                              • 1 Juice from 2 Meyer lemons
                                              • Pinch of salt
                                              • ⅛ th teaspoon xanthan gum, optional but recommended
                                              • 1 Scoop of whey protein

                                              Directions

                                              1. Add milk and sugar to a saucepan and bring to heat, stirring constantly
                                              2. Whisk egg yolks in a small bowl and add a few tablespoons of hot milk at a time, whisking constantly
                                              3. Slowly whisk the tempered yolks into the milk, whisking until thick
                                              4. Allow the custard to cool
                                              5. Add the cooled custard base and all other ingredients to a blender and blend well
                                              6. Pour the mixture into your creami container and freeze for 24 hours
                                              7. When ready to eat, run the edges of the frozen creami container under hot water for 30 seconds
                                              8. Spin on “lite ice cream” 2-3x
                                              9. Enjoy!

                                                Dessert Cookies

                                                Rice Krispie Chocolate Chip Cookies

                                                Serves: 1 servings · Prep: 15 min · Cook: 9 min

                                                Mix butter, sugars, eggs, vanilla; add dry ingredients. Fold in oats, Rice Krispies, chips; bake at 350°F for 9-10 mins.

                                                Ingredients

                                                • 1 cup butter, softened
                                                • 1 cup brown sugar
                                                • 1 cup granulated sugar
                                                • 2 teaspoons vanilla extract
                                                • 2 large eggs
                                                • 2 cups all-purpose flour
                                                • 1 teaspoon baking powder
                                                • 1 teaspoon baking soda
                                                • 2 cups rolled oats
                                                • 2 cups Rice Krispies
                                                • 2 cups chocolate chips, 1-2

                                                Directions

                                                1. Preheat oven to 350°F. Line baking sheet with parchment paper
                                                2. Mix flour, baking powder, and baking soda in a bowl
                                                3. Cream butter, brown sugar, and granulated sugar until fluffy
                                                4. Mix in eggs and vanilla extract
                                                5. Gradually add dry ingredients to wet ingredients
                                                6. Fold in oats, Rice Krispies, and chocolate chips
                                                7. Scoop dough onto baking sheet, spacing 2 inches apart
                                                8. Bake 9-10 minutes until edges are golden.
                                                9. Cool on sheet briefly, then transfer to wire rack
