                                                    Mexican

                                                    Taco Crunch Supreme Wrap Recipe 🌮

                                                    Serves: 1 servings

                                                    Taco Crunch Supreme Wrap Recipe 🌮

                                                    Ingredients

                                                    • For the taco meat:
                                                    • 2 lbs ground beef
                                                    • Salt, to taste
                                                    • 1 tbsp garlic powder
                                                    • 1 tbsp onion powder
                                                    • 1 tbsp smoked paprika
                                                    • 1 tbsp cumin powder
                                                    • 1 tsp black pepper powder
                                                    • 2 tbsp tomato paste
                                                    • 1 white onion, diced
                                                    • 1 tbsp freshly minced garlic
                                                    • 1 jalapeño, diced
                                                    • 2 tbsp oil for cooking
                                                    • ½ cup water
                                                    • For the assembly:
                                                    • 10 large tortillas
                                                    • 10 tostadas corn tortillas or 1 cup nacho chips
                                                    • 10 mini tortillas
                                                    • 1 cup lettuce, sliced
                                                    • 1 cup tomato, diced
                                                    • 1 cup red onion, diced
                                                    • ½ cup sour cream
                                                    • 1 cup guacamole, optional
                                                    • 1 cup salsa
                                                    • 1 cup cheddar cheese, shredded
                                                    • 1 cup Monterey Jack cheese, shredded
                                                    • 1 Oil for cooking

                                                    Directions

                                                    1. Heat oil in a large skillet over medium-high heat. Add diced onion, minced garlic cook until onion is translucent.
                                                    2. Add ground beef; cook, breaking it up with a spoon. Once browned, add garlic powder, onion powder, smoked paprika, cumin powder, black pepper powder jalapeños and tomato paste. Add water
                                                    3. Stir well, cook 8-10 minutes or until flavors combine and cooked through
                                                    4. Take a large tortilla.Spoon taco meat onto tortilla; add Tostada or nacho chips,Lettuce, Tomato,Red onion, Sour cream,Guacamole (if using) , Salsa, Cheddar cheese,Monterey Jack cheese
                                                    5. Place smaller flour tortilla on top of the filling.
                                                    6. Fold the edges of the large tortilla toward the center, creating pleats.
                                                    7. Invert the Crunchwrap so the pleats are on the bottom, securing the filling.
                                                    8. Heat oil in a skillet over medium heat.
                                                    9. Grease a non stick pan with oil, Cook the Crunchwrap seam-side down on medium low heat for 3 minutes or until golden.
                                                    10. Flip and cook the other side for an additional 3 minutes. Repeat with remaining Crunchwraps.

                                                    Notes

                                                    Fold gently to avoid tearing the tortilla. Press down lightly for even browning. Serve immediately and enjoy your crispy Crunchwrap!

                                                    Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                                    Pasta Mexican Mama Goose’s Recipes

                                                    Taco Spaghetti

                                                    Serves: 6 people · Prep: 15 min · Cook: 30 min

                                                    Taco Spaghetti Recipe – THE BEST! We ate this three days in a row! Ready in 30 minutes!! Taco meat, Velveeta, diced tomatoes with green chilies, spaghetti noodles, cream of chicken soup and cheddar cheese. CRAZY good! Kids and adults gobbled this up – even our picky eaters! This is our favorite Mexican casserole!

                                                    Ingredients

                                                    • 8 oz spaghetti
                                                    • 1 ¼ pounds lean ground beef or ground turkey
                                                    • 1 package taco seasoning, 1-oz
                                                    • ⅔ cup water
                                                    • 1 can Unsalted Cream of Chicken Soup, 10.5-oz
                                                    • 1 can Rotel diced tomatoes and green chilies undrained, 10-oz
                                                    • 1 package Velveeta cheese, cubed, 8-oz
                                                    • 1 ½ cups shredded cheddar cheese

                                                    Directions

                                                    1. Preheat oven to 350ºF. Lightly spray a 9×9-inch pan with cooking spray. Set aside.
                                                    2. Cook pasta according to package directions. Drain. Set aside.
                                                    3. In a large skillet cook ground beef over medium-high heat until no longer pink. Drain fat. Return meat to skillet. Add taco seasoning and water. Stir to combine. Cook for 5 minutes.
                                                    4. Stir in soup, Velveeta and Rotel tomatoes. Reduce heat to low, and cook until the cheese melts, stirring constantly.
                                                    5. Stir in cooked spaghetti and pour into prepared dish. Top with cheddar cheese.
                                                    6. Bake for 30 minutes or until heated through.

                                                    Notes

                                                    Is Taco Spaghetti spicy? If you are worried about the heat, make sure to buy the Mild can of Rotel diced tomatoes and green chilies. Substitutions/Additions onions bell peppers corn green chiles cilantro black beans red pepper flakes I use cheddar cheese in this recipe. You can substitute Mexican cheese blend. I suggest using Unsalted Cream of Chicken Soup in this dish to control the sodium. You can buy it by the case at Amazon: https://amzn.to/49n4uZE Need Gluten-free Cream of Chicken Soup? You can buy it here: https://amzn.to/3MtqQ1s Here is our recipe for Homemade Cream of Chicken Soup: https://www.plainchicken.com/homemade-cream-of-chicken-soup/ Here is our recipe for Homemade Taco Seasoning made with Chili powder, garlic powder, onion powder, red pepper flakes, oregano, paprika, cumin, salt, and black pepper: https://www.plainchicken.com/homemade-taco-seasoning/ Can Taco Spaghetti be made in advance? Yes. This spaghetti casserole can be made ahead of time and refrigerated overnight or even frozen for later. Store leftovers in an airtight container in the refrigerator, Reheat in the microwave.

                                                    Recipe from: plainchicken.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                                    Sandwich Middle Eastern

                                                    Tavuklu üçgen ( Chicken triangle ) wrap - Turkish

                                                    Serves: 12 adet

                                                    Ingredients

                                                    • 500 g chicken breast
                                                    • 1 medium onion
                                                    • 1 clove of garlic
                                                    • 1 green pepper
                                                    • 1 capia pepper
                                                    • 1 Half yellow pepper, optional
                                                    • 1 teaspoon red pepper powder, black pepper, salt
                                                    • ½ teaspoon curry
                                                    • 200 ml liquid cream
                                                    • 1 handful of mozzarella and kashar cheese
                                                    • 5 tablespoons of oil, 4-5
                                                    • 1 To wrap
                                                    • 8 large lavash, 6-8
                                                    • 1 Cheddar cheese
                                                    • 1 To paste
                                                    • 2 tablespoons of flour
                                                    • 5 tablespoons of water, 4-5

                                                    Directions

                                                    1. Chop the chicken into small pieces, heat a pan, add a little oil, and cook the chicken until its juices are absorbed.
                                                    2. Add the onion and peppers and fry.
                                                    3. Let's add the spices and mix.
                                                    4. Add the cream and once it thickens, add the mozzarella and cheddar cheese. Once the cheeses melt, turn off the heat.
                                                    5. Split the lavash in half and place cheddar cheese and 1 spoon of the chicken mixture in the middle. Mix the flour and water together to make it stick to the edges. Shape the lavash into a triangle, as shown in the video.
                                                    6. Let's put it on a tray and add cheddar cheese on it.
                                                    7. Let's bake it in the oven until it turns golden brown.
                                                    8. Let's serve it hot.

                                                    Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                                    Soup karissastevens

                                                    The Best Chicken and Dumplings

                                                    Serves: 6 servings · Prep: 20 min · Cook: 45 min

                                                    This recipe is a classic for a reason. It’s hearty, comforting, and relatively easy to make. The dumplings are light and fluffy, and the chicken and vegetables are cooked in a rich, flavorful broth. It’s the perfect meal for a cold winter night.

                                                    Ingredients

                                                    • For the chicken and vegetables:
                                                    • 2 tablespoons olive oil
                                                    • 1 ½ pounds boneless, skinless chicken thighs, cut into 1-inch pieces
                                                    • 1 large yellow onion, chopped
                                                    • 2 carrots, peeled and chopped
                                                    • 2 celery stalks, chopped
                                                    • 2 cloves garlic, minced
                                                    • 8 cups chicken broth
                                                    • 1 teaspoon dried thyme
                                                    • ½ teaspoon dried rosemary
                                                    • Salt and freshly ground black pepper to taste
                                                    • ½ cup frozen peas
                                                    • ¼ cup chopped fresh parsley, for garnish
                                                    • For the dumplings:
                                                    • 1 ½ cups all-purpose flour
                                                    • 2 teaspoons baking powder
                                                    • ½ teaspoon salt
                                                    • ¾ cup milk
                                                    • ¼ cup unsalted butter, melted

                                                    Directions

                                                    1. For the chicken and vegetables:
                                                    2. Heat the olive oil in a large Dutch oven or pot over medium-high heat. Add the chicken pieces and cook until browned on all sides, about 5-7 minutes. Remove the chicken from the pot and set aside.
                                                    3. Add the onion, carrots, and celery to the pot. Cook, stirring occasionally, until softened, about 8-10 minutes. Add the garlic and cook for another minute until fragrant.
                                                    4. Return the chicken to the pot. Pour in the chicken broth and add the thyme and rosemary. Bring the mixture to a boil, then reduce the heat to low, cover, and simmer for 15 minutes.
                                                    5. For the dumplings:
                                                    6. While the chicken is simmering, prepare the dumplings. In a medium bowl, whisk together the flour, baking powder, and salt.
                                                    7. In a separate small bowl, whisk together the milk and melted butter. Pour the wet ingredients into the dry ingredients and stir until just combined. Do not overmix.
                                                    8. Combine and finish:
                                                    9. Drop the dumpling dough by the tablespoonful directly into the simmering chicken mixture. Cover the pot and cook for 15-20 minutes, or until the dumplings are cooked through and fluffy. (To check for doneness, you can cut one open; it should be cooked all the way through with no raw dough in the center.)
                                                    10. Stir in the frozen peas and cook for another 2-3 minutes, or until heated through. Season with salt and freshly ground black pepper to taste.
                                                    11. Ladle the chicken and dumplings into bowls and garnish with fresh parsley before serving.

                                                    Recipe from: open.substack.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                                    Soup

                                                    The Best Chili

                                                    Serves: 4 servings · Prep: 20 min · Cook: 90 min

                                                    This is the best chili recipe I’ve ever made! Ground beef, beans, and the perfect mix of spices simmer together for a delicious thick and hearty chili.

                                                    Ingredients

                                                    • 1 tablespoon olive oil
                                                    • 1 medium yellow onion, chopped
                                                    • 1 pound lean ground beef
                                                    • 4 cloves garlic, minced
                                                    • 2 tablespoons chili powder*, 1-2, use 1 tablespoon for mild chili, 2 tablespoons for spicier chili
                                                    • 1 tablespoon ground cumin
                                                    • 1 tablespoon brown sugar
                                                    • 1 teaspoon dried oregano
                                                    • ½ teaspoon salt
                                                    • ¼ teaspoon black pepper
                                                    • 3 tablespoons tomato paste
                                                    • 1 ¾ cups low sodium beef broth, or one 14.5 ounce can
                                                    • 28 ounce can fire roasted crushed tomatoes, or fire roasted diced tomatoes
                                                    • 15 ounce can red kidney beans, rinsed and drained
                                                    • 15 ounce can pinto beans, or black beans, rinsed and drained
                                                    • For topping: shredded cheddar cheese, sour cream, avocado, chopped red or green onions, cilantro, etc., as desired

                                                    Directions

                                                    1. Heat the olive oil in a large Dutch oven or other heavy pot over medium-high heat. Add the onion and cook 2 minutes, until onion starts to soften.
                                                    2. Add the ground beef and cook, crumbling, until browned, about 5 minutes. Drain off excess grease. Add the garlic and cook, stirring, for 30 seconds.
                                                    3. Add the chili powder, cumin, brown sugar, oregano, salt, pepper and tomato paste. Stir to combine.
                                                    4. Add the broth and stir, scraping up any bits from the bottom of the pot.
                                                    5. Stir in the crushed or diced tomatoes and beans.
                                                    6. Bring to a boil, then reduce heat to low and cook at a low simmer for 1-2 hours, stirring occasionally. Cover the chili during the first part of the cook time and then uncover during the last 30 minutes as needed to thicken the chili.
                                                    7. Serve chili with toppings, as desired.

                                                    Notes

                                                    Chili powder: Some chili powders are more spicy than others. Adjust the amount accordingly. To make chili without beans: Omit the beans and use 2 pounds of ground beef. To freeze: Freeze chili in an airtight container for up to 3 months. Thaw overnight in the refrigerator before reheating. Reheat in a covered pot on the stove or in the microwave. I like to freeze chili in individual portion sizes to thaw and reheat for quick lunches or dinners. To store: Store leftover chili in an airtight container in the refrigerator for up to 3-4 days.

                                                    Nutrition (per serving)

                                                    Calories: 320 · Fat: 8 · Saturated fat: 2 · Unsaturated fat: 4 · Carbs: 38 · Sugar: 12 · Fiber: 11 · Protein: 28 · Sodium: 1007 · Cholesterol: 47

                                                    Recipe from: kristineskitchenblog.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                                    The Best Egg Salad

                                                    Serves: 8 servings · Prep: 10 min

                                                    Light and creamy, this is the very Best Egg Salad recipe ever. Eggs, mayonnaise, mustard, scallions and celery are blended together to create a flavorful egg salad that’s perfect for lunch, brunch or a light dinner.

                                                    Ingredients

                                                    • ½ cup mayonnaise
                                                    • 1 teaspoon ground mustard
                                                    • 3 scallions, chopped
                                                    • 1 celery stalk, finely chopped
                                                    • salt and fresh ground pepper, to taste

                                                    Directions

                                                    1. In a medium bowl, stir together the eggs, mayonnaise, ground mustard, scallions, celery, salt and pepper until thoroughly combined.
                                                    2. Serve immediately or refrigerate in an airtight container for up to 3 days.

                                                    Soup Mama Goose’s Recipes

                                                    The Best White Chicken Chili

                                                    Serves: 6 servings · Prep: 15 min · Cook: 35 min

                                                    Flavorful White Chicken Chili made with hearty beans, tender chicken, and a rich and creamy broth. It’s a lighter alternative to traditional beef chili and makes for a super satisfying dinner! If you’re lucky to have any left it will make great leftovers for lunch the next day too.

                                                    Ingredients

                                                    • 1 small yellow onion, diced
                                                    • 1 tbsp olive oil
                                                    • 2 cloves garlic, finely minced
                                                    • 2 cans low-sodium chicken broth, 14.5 oz
                                                    • 1 can diced green chilies, 7 oz
                                                    • 1 ½ tsp cumin
                                                    • ½ tsp paprika
                                                    • ½ tsp dried oregano
                                                    • ½ tsp ground coriander
                                                    • ¼ tsp cayenne pepper
                                                    • salt and freshly ground black pepper, to taste
                                                    • 1 pkg Neufchatel cheese, cut into small cubes, 8 oz, aka light cream cheese
                                                    • 1 ¼ cup frozen or fresh corn
                                                    • 2 cans cannellini beans, 15 oz
                                                    • 2 ½ cups shredded cooked rotisserie or left-over chicken*
                                                    • 1 Tbsp fresh lime juice
                                                    • 2 Tbsp chopped fresh cilantro, plus more for serving
                                                    • Tortilla chips or strips, monterrey jack cheese, sliced avocado for serving, optional

                                                    Directions

                                                    1. Heat olive oil in a large pot over medium-high heat. Add onion and saute 4 minutes minutes. Add garlic and saute 30 seconds longer.
                                                    2. Add chicken broth, green chilies, cumin, paprika, oregano, coriander, cayenne pepper and season with salt and pepper to taste. Bring mixture just to a boil then reduce heat to medium-low and simmer 15 minutes.
                                                    3. Drain and rinse beans in a fine mesh strainer or colander then measure out 1 cup. Set whole beans aside, transfer 1 cup beans to a food processor along with 1/4 cup broth from soup, puree until nearly smooth**.
                                                    4. Add Neufchatel cheese to soup along with corn, whole beans and pureed beans and stir well. Simmer 5 - 10 minutes longer.
                                                    5. Stir in chicken, fresh lime juice and cilantro. Serve with Monterrey Jack cheese, more cilantro, avocado slices and tortilla chips if desired.

                                                    Notes

                                                    *Recipe updated to use pre-cooked shredded chicken (previously used 1 lb raw chicken in this recipe and cooked at the beginning). I like the flavor of rotisserie chicken better plus this way you don't end with over-cooked chicken breasts. **If you don't own a food processor you can skip the pureeing step, soup just won't be quite as creamy.

                                                    Nutrition (per serving)

                                                    Calories: 383 · Fat: 14 · Saturated fat: 6 · Carbs: 35 · Sugar: 5 · Fiber: 8 · Protein: 33 · Sodium: 525 · Cholesterol: 77

                                                    Recipe from: cookingclassy.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                                    The Cheesesteak Sub Sandwich macro friendly high protein bowl💪

                                                    Serves: 6 bowls

                                                    The Cheesesteak Sub Sandwich macro friendly high protein bowl💪By @tastyshreds Hope you guys enjoy . 😉

                                                    Ingredients

                                                    • 4 medium russet potatoes
                                                    • 1 tablespoon olive oil
                                                    • Salt, to taste
                                                    • Pepper, to taste
                                                    • 1 teaspoon garlic powder
                                                    • 1 teaspoon paprika
                                                    • 32 oz sirloin steak, thinly sliced
                                                    • 1 white onion, thinly sliced
                                                    • 8 jalapeños, thinly sliced
                                                    • 120 g reduced-fat mozzarella, shredded
                                                    • 120 g reduced-fat cheddar, shredded
                                                    • Salt, to taste
                                                    • Pepper, to taste
                                                    • 1 tablespoon olive oil

                                                    Directions

                                                    1. Make chipotle sauce:
                                                    2. Blend 300g non-fat Greek yogurt, 50g chipotle in adobo, and 3 minced garlic cloves. Season with salt and pepper. Chill.
                                                    3. Prep potatoes:
                                                    4. Peel and cube 4 russet potatoes. Soak in cold water for 30 minutes for crispiness.
                                                    5. Season potatoes:
                                                    6. Drain, dry, and toss with 1 tbsp olive oil, salt, pepper, garlic powder, and paprika.
                                                    7. Air fry:
                                                    8. Cook seasoned potatoes at 400°F (200°C) for 15-20 minutes, shaking halfway, until crispy.
                                                    9. Sauté veggies:
                                                    10. Heat 1 tbsp olive oil in a skillet. Cook sliced onions and jalapeños until translucent.
                                                    11. Cook steak:
                                                    12. Add sliced sirloin to skillet with onions and jalapeños. Season with salt and pepper. Cook 5-7 minutes until done.
                                                    13. Shred cheese:
                                                    14. Grate 120g reduced-fat mozzarella and cheddar.
                                                    15. Assemble:
                                                    16. Layer air-fried potatoes, steak mixture, and cheese in containers. Serve with chipotle sauce.

                                                    Appetizers Baked Goods

                                                    Three Cheese Jalapeno Popovers

                                                    Serves: 12 large popovers · Prep: 15 min · Cook: 32 min

                                                    These Three-Cheese Jalapeño Popovers are everything you love about classic popovers. They are light, airy, irresistibly steamy, but with a bold, savory twist. A blend of cheddar, Monterey Jack, and Parmesan melts into the batter, creating pockets of creamy richness and golden, toasty edges. Fresh jalapeños add just the right amount of heat and brightness, balancing the buttery depth of the cheeses without overwhelming the delicate rise. They bake up beautifully tall with a soft, custardy center and a perfectly crisp shell. Serve them warm for brunch or with your chili. They are perfect holiday side that always please a crowd!

                                                    Ingredients

                                                    • 3 cups whole milk, 720 ml
                                                    • 8 large eggs
                                                    • 1 tsp salt, 6 g
                                                    • 1 tsp sugar, 4 g
                                                    • 3 cups all-purpose flour, 360 g, not packed, spooned & leveled
                                                    • 6 tbsp extra virgin olive oil, plus more for greasing the pan, 88 ml
                                                    • 1 ½ oz cheddar cheese, shredded, 43 g
                                                    • 1 ½ oz monterey jack cheese, shredded, 43 g
                                                    • 2 jalapenos, seeded and diced
                                                    • 1 Parmesan for topping
                                                    • 1 Butter & Honey for topping

                                                    Directions

                                                    1. For the popovers, In a large bowl, using a hand mixer or whisk, mix flour, milk, olive oil, salt, sugar and eggs. Add the jalapenos. Mix gently but well. A few lumps are okay! Let the batter rest for about 10 minutes. (Note: see FAQs for more details about making the batter beforehand.)
                                                    2. Preheat oven to 450 degrees and liberally grease a popover pan with olive oil. (Muffin pan also works) Note: When greasing the pan, I like to add about a tsp of olive oil to the bottom of each popover compartment. Then , I like to oil my fingers or a napkin and make sure the sides of each compartment are nice and greased up! Then place the popover pan onto a large baking sheet (to catch any drippings) and then into the oven to heat for about 3 minutes.
                                                    3. Remove pans and immediately divide/pour batter into the compartments of the pan (I fill my popover compartments about 3/4 of the way). Press some of the shredded cheese mix in each compartment filled with the popover batter. And sprinkle a little more of the shredded cheese on top, along with the parmesean.
                                                    4. Bake for about 25 minutes at 450 degrees. Then turn down the heat to 350 degrees and bake for an additional 5-10 minutes. (try not to open the oven or that will release all of the steam and the popovers could deflate!)
                                                    5. Remove popovers from the oven. Then, using a toothpick, poke the tops of each popover so they can breathe (this also keeps the popovers from deflating). Popovers should easily slip out of the pan. Once you have removed the popovers, add more olive oil to the pan and place it back in the oven for 3 minutes, then pour the second batch of popover batter and bake.
                                                    6. Once baked, serve immediately with butter and honey.

                                                    Notes

                                                    Storage: Store in fridge in an air tight container for up to 2 days. To reheat, place popovers onto a baking sheet and bake at 350 for about 10 minutes or until warmed through. Helpful tips: Use room-temperature ingredients– Eggs and milk should be at room temperature so the batter incorporates evenly and rises well. Cold ingredients can make popovers dense. Don’t overmix the batter– Mix just until all dry ingredients disappear. Overmixing develops gluten and can make the popovers tough instead of light and airy. Preheat popover pan – Don’t skip the step of placing your popover or muffin tin in the oven before pouring in the batter. Pouring batter into the hot pan with oil helps them spring up immediately.

                                                    Recipe from: eatatmaudes.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                                    Dessert

                                                    Vintage Lime Jello Salad

                                                    Serves: 12 servings · Cook: 10 min

                                                    You will love this refreshing Lime Jello Salad. With only 5 ingredients (and water) it is so easy to make to serve a crowd.

                                                    Ingredients

                                                    • 1 package lime Jello, 6 ounce, or two 3 ounce packages
                                                    • 2 cups boiling water
                                                    • 1 package cream cheese, room temperature, 8 ounce
                                                    • 2 cups 7 UP, chilled
                                                    • 1 can crushed pineapple, drained, 8 ounce
                                                    • 1 container Cool Whip, thawed, 12 ounce

                                                    Directions

                                                    1. Bring water to a boil and add Jello packet - while in pot. Mix well.
                                                    2. In a separate bowl, blend cream cheese.
                                                    3. Add dissolved Jello mixture to cream cheese in 3 batches, blending with a hand mixer between each addition. This is to help prevent lumps.
                                                    4. Add 7 UP and mix by hand.
                                                    5. Chill until mixture resembles egg whites or slime, about 30 - 60 minutes - this will vary depending on the temperature of the fridge, so check every 15 minutes or so.
                                                    6. When the mixture is thick but soupy, add cool whip and mix well by hand.
                                                    7. Add crushed pineapple and mix well.
                                                    8. Pour mixture into a 9x13 serving dish or Jello mold. Chill till fully set.

                                                    Nutrition (per serving)

                                                    Serving Size: 1 · Calories: 47 · Fat: 1 · Saturated fat: 1 · Trans fat: 0 · Unsaturated fat: 0 · Carbs: 9 · Sugar: 9 · Fiber: 0 · Protein: 0 · Sodium: 22 · Cholesterol: 2

                                                    Recipe from: simplesidedishes.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                                    Dessert

                                                    Viral Dubai Pistachio Chocolate Bar

                                                    Serves: 4 servings · Prep: 10 min · Cook: 15 min

                                                    The STUFFED Viral Dubai Pistachio Chocolate Bar - the crunchy pistachio kataifi dessert sensation that has had us in a chokehold this year. Here's the BEST step-by-step copycat recipe so you can make it for your friends at home!

                                                    Ingredients

                                                    • 3 tablespoon unsalted butter
                                                    • 8 oz kataifi
                                                    • 3 tbsp tahini
                                                    • ¼ teaspoon salt
                                                    • 16 oz milk chocolate bars roughly chopped
                                                    • 10 ½ oz pistachio cream
                                                    • any color candy melts for decoration, melted optional

                                                    Directions

                                                    1. Remove kataifi from freezer to defrost for 1-2 hours. This will make chopping it easier, or shred in a food processor. Melt butter in large pan on medium heat and add kataifi, toasting it for 8-10 mins or until golden brown. Transfer to large mixing bowl and allow to cool completely.
                                                    2. Add pistachio cream, tahini, and salt to cooled kataifi. Mix until well combined and set saide.
                                                    3. Optional:
                                                    4. melt any decorating candy melts in small bowls using the microwave. Drizzle desired decorations in chocolate mold, and chill in fridge for 10 mins.
                                                    5. Melt the milk chocolate in a large bowl also using the microwave, or alternatively over a double boiler. If you wish to temper your chocolate, reserve a small handful of chocolate and melt the rest until it reaches 113℉ (45℃). Remove bowl from the heat and stir in the reserved pieces until it melts in. Cool chocolate until it reaches 84-86℉ (28-30℉).
                                                    6. Pour milk chocolate into mold. Tilt mold sideways or upside-down to allow excess chocolate to drip off back into chocolate bowl. We're trying to layer a thin even coating. You can also use an offset spatula to scrape off all extra chocolate. We will be re-melting the chocolate bowl, so avoid wasting any! Once you finish the first layer, tap mold on the counter a few times to allow chocolate to settle and to get rid of any air bubbles. Transfer back to fridge for another 10 mins to set.
                                                    7. Spoon filling into chocolate mold and spread evenly. Be sure to leave room for the final chocolate layer - do not overfill. Chill mold in fridge for 10 more mins.
                                                    8. Re-melt (or temper) milk chocolate. Then, pour over filling and spread evenly to seal in the filling. Tap mold again gently a few times on counter to create a smooth finish. Chill mold in fridge for 1-2 hours minimum before enjoying. Carefully detach finished bars from mold. Keep refrigerated.

                                                    Recipe from: happytummy702.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                                    Asian Casserole

                                                    Viral Dumpling Lasagna

                                                    Serves: 1 servings · Cook: 22 min

                                                    Homemade dumplings meet homemade lasagna—easier, quicker, and better than both.

                                                    Ingredients

                                                    • 1 lb ground chicken or pork, 450 g
                                                    • ½ cup green onions, chopped
                                                    • 6 garlic cloves, minced
                                                    • 3 tbsp soy sauce
                                                    • 1 tbsp rice vinegar
                                                    • 1 tbsp toasted sesame oil
                                                    • 2 tbsp chili crunch
                                                    • 1 tbsp fresh ginger, grated
                                                    • 1 tbsp oyster sauce
                                                    • 24 wonton or dumpling wrappers, 18-24
                                                    • 2 tbsp chicken stock, or water
                                                    • For garnish:
                                                    • 1 Soy sauce
                                                    • 1 Chili crunch
                                                    • 1 Green onions, sliced
                                                    • 1 Sesame seeds

                                                    Directions

                                                    1. In a bowl, combine the ground chicken or pork with the green onions, garlic, soy sauce, rice vinegar, sesame oil, Chili Crunch, ginger, and oyster sauce. Mix with your hands until just combined.
                                                    2. Set up a steamer over a pot of gently boiling water. If you don’t have a steamer, simply place a heat-safe rack or trivet inside a large pot instead. Make sure your baking dish fits comfortably inside & the lid can fully close (I used an 8×8-inch dish). Keep the water level below the baking dish.
                                                    3. Briefly dip each wrapper in water for just 1–2 secs, then arrange a layer across the bottom of your dish. I used 6 wrappers per layer. Overlap them as needed to completely cover the bottom… the overlap actually helps everything hold together.
                                                    4. Spread a thin layer of the meat mixture over the wrappers. Repeat with wrappers and meat until everything is used. I made 4 layers, but work with whatever fits your dish. Finish with a final layer of wrappers, then pour the chicken stock evenly over top.
                                                    5. Carefully place the dish into your steamer, cover, & steam for 20–22 mins, keeping the water at a gentle boil. Remove once the meat is cooked through & the top is glossy.
                                                    6. Finish with a drizzle of soy sauce, plenty of chili crunch, sliced green onions, and sesame seeds. Slice it up like lasagna and enjoy this new family favorite!

                                                    Recipe from: instagram.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                                    Whipped Bone Marrow

                                                    Serves: 1 servings · Cook: 25 min

                                                    Starting solids with a nutrient-dense powerhouse: whipped bone marrow. 💪🏼 This was Emmett’s very first food—and it’s loaded with healthy fats, iron, and collagen to support brain development, gut health, and overall growth.

                                                    Ingredients

                                                    • 1 marrow bones

                                                    Directions

                                                    1. Spread marrow bones on a parchment-lined baking pan
                                                    2. Roast at 425°F for ~25–30 mins
                                                    3. Scoop out the marrow + pour in the dripped oils (tallow)
                                                    4. Cool in fridge for 10–15 mins
                                                    5. Whip with a mixer until soft peaks form

                                                    Notes

                                                    Storage: • Fridge: ~1 week • Freezer: up to 6 months To serve: • Thaw at room temp 10–15 mins • Spoon-feed directly or mix into food Pro tip: Use the leftover bones to make bone broth—just simmer in water until milky white.

                                                    Dips Middle Eastern

                                                    Yemeni lahsa - Turkish

                                                    Serves: 1 servings

                                                    Yemeni lahsa @ndcmuscat

                                                    Ingredients

                                                    • 1 large onion
                                                    • 2 large ripe tomatoes
                                                    • 5 spicy chilies, 4-5, optional
                                                    • 3 eggs
                                                    • 1 @ndcmuscat olive oil
                                                    • 1 liquid cheese
                                                    • 1 salt & pepper
                                                    • 1 tsp cumin powder
                                                    • 1 tsp coriander powder
                                                    • 1 tsp paprika
                                                    • 1 tbsp tomato puree

                                                    Directions

                                                    1. start by chopping the veggies. Saute the onion in a drizzle of olive oil.
                                                    2. once the onion starts to brown add the tomatoes. Season with salt. Pepper. Cumin. Coriander. Paprika. Tomato puree and any spices that you prefer.
                                                    3. keep cooking the tomatoes on medium low till dissolved.
                                                    4. switch off the heat then crack the eggs in the mixture. Mix it thoroughly and add a splash of water.
                                                    5. turn the heat back on low and spread the cheese on top.
                                                    6. cover and let it cook for 3-5 minutes.
                                                    7. garnish. Serve with bread and enjoy.

                                                    Notes

                                                    @k.8th.7 صحيح ✅

                                                    Baked Goods Carnivorous

                                                    Zero Carb Carnivore Bread

                                                    Serves: 1 servings · Cook: 50 min

                                                    This bread does not collapse after taking out of the oven and has a fantastic bread like texture thanks to the egg white powder and precise ratios. I’ve now tried crisping it up in the air fryer and have turned it into luxurious French toast for my husband. It will also be great for sandwiches.

                                                    Ingredients

                                                    • ½ cup butter, 120g - 1 stick
                                                    • 12 ounces full fat cream cheese, 340g - 1.5 blocks
                                                    • 1 both must be softened
                                                    • 4 large eggs at room temperature
                                                    • 1 cup egg white protein powder
                                                    • 2 tsp of gelatin
                                                    • Optional - Will always use:
                                                    • 2 teaspoons baking powder, 12g
                                                    • ½ teaspoon baking soda, 2g
                                                    • ½ teaspoon cream of tartar, 2g

                                                    Directions

                                                    1. Pre heat oven for 320
                                                    2. Add wet ingredients to bowl
                                                    3. Then add egg white power and then mix
                                                    4. Add the rest of the powders
                                                    5. Bake at 320°F for 45-50 minutes, let cool, slice, and enjoy!

                                                    Notes

                                                    Bread

                                                    Healthy Mama Goose’s Recipes

                                                    Zucchini fritters with chili lime mayo

                                                    Serves: 30 fritters

                                                    It's the time of year for zucchini everything! Our zucchini plants have had a particularly fruitful year and I've been looking for yummy ways to use it up. A friend suggested doing a zucchini fritter, which sounded like a brilliant idea. She has lots of brilliant ideas about food. So I set to work on coming up with my own recipe and they turned out amazing the first time. I made a chili lime mayo to serve them with, and MMM! Those flavours together are frittastic! This recipe makes 25-30 fritters so they could be served as a main course or as a party appetizer.

                                                    Ingredients

                                                    • 1 vegetable oil for frying
                                                    • 3 cups grated zucchini
                                                    • 1 cup diced onion
                                                    • 2 eggs
                                                    • 1 cup all-purpose flour
                                                    • 1 tsp baking powder
                                                    • ½ tsp chili powder
                                                    • ½ tsp salt
                                                    • ¼ tsp pepper
                                                    • 1 cup prepared mayo
                                                    • ½ a lime, juice
                                                    • ½ tsp chili powder

                                                    Directions

                                                    1. I started by pouring the vegetable oil in my skillet so that it was half an inch deep and turned the heat to medium. Meantime, I combined the zucchini, onion, and eggs in a large bowl.
                                                    2. I gave the mixture a good stir to combine. It got all kinda frothy. Awesome.
                                                    3. Next, I combined the flour, baking powder, 1/2 tsp of chili powder, salt, and pepper in a small mixing bowl and stirred to combine. To finish the batter, I added the dry ingredients to the zucchini mixture and folded it all together.
                                                    4. To test my oil, I dipped a spoon into my batter then dipped the spoon into the oil. Once the oil bubbled in contact with the batter, I knew it was hot enough. I dropped my batter into the oil by the heaping tablespoon, frying about six fritters at a time. They cooked for 2-3 minutes a side. Once the centres were firm, I removed the fritters from the oil and let them drain on a dinner plate lined with paper towel.
                                                    5. To make the dip, I simply combined the prepared mayo, lime juice, and chili powder in a small mixing bowl and stirred it all up. This is a seriously yummy dip! I love limes. And chili powder. And mayo. So this really couldn't go wrong.

                                                    Recipe from: familyfeedbag.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                                    Healthy Mama Goose’s Recipes

                                                    zucchini or summer squash fritters

                                                    Serves: 1 servings · Prep: 15 min · Cook: 15 min

                                                    These fritters will be amazing if they're made with shredded zucchini or summer squash. Easy to prepare, they're a tasty afternoon snack. The outer edges are crispy and the center stays soft. Onion adds a tiny hint of sweetness to the fritters. We placed ours in the oven to keep warm and they turned out perfect.

                                                    Ingredients

                                                    • 5 md zucchini or summer squash, 4-5
                                                    • 1 sm onion, grated
                                                    • 1 tsp coarse salt
                                                    • 1 lg egg, well beaten
                                                    • ½ c all-purpose flour
                                                    • ½ tsp black pepper, freshly grated
                                                    • 3 Tbsp oil of your choice for frying, 2-3

                                                    Directions

                                                    1. If planning to serve as soon as they’re done, preheat oven to 250 degrees. Have a baking sheet lined with paper towels ready.
                                                    2. Trim ends off zucchini and grate them either on the large holes of a box grater or, if you have one, using the shredding blade of a food processor.
                                                    3. In a large bowl, toss zucchini with 1 teaspoon coarse salt and set aside for 10 minutes.
                                                    4. Wring out the zucchini in one of the following ways: pressing it against the holes of a colander with a wooden spoon to extract the water, squeezing out small handfuls at a time, or wrapping it up in a clean dishtowel or piece of cheesecloth and wringing away.
                                                    5. Return deflated mass of zucchini shreds to bowl. Taste and if you think it could benefit from more salt add a little bit more. Stir in onion, egg, flour, and some freshly ground black pepper.
                                                    6. In a large heavy skillet, heat 2-3 tablespoons of oil over medium-high heat until shimmering. Drop small bunches of the zucchini mixture onto the skillet only a few at a time so they don’t become crowded and lightly nudge them flatter with the back of your spatula.
                                                    7. Cook the fritters over moderately high heat until the edges underneath are golden, about 3 to 4 minutes. Flip the fritters and fry them on the other side until browned underneath again, about 2 to 3 minutes more.
                                                    8. Drain briefly on paper towels then transfer to the baking sheet.
                                                    9. Then place into the warm oven until needed. Repeat the process, keeping the pan well-oiled, with remaining batter.

                                                    Notes

                                                    Cook’s Note: I like to make sure that the fritters have at least 10 minutes in the oven to finish setting and getting extra crisp. Do ahead: (This is the best part!) These fritters keep well, either chilled in the fridge for the better part of a week and or frozen in a well-sealed package for months. When you’re ready to use them, simply spread them out on a tray in a 325 degree oven until they’re hot and crisp again.

                                                    Recipe from: justapinch.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                                    Healthy Mama Goose’s Recipes Casserole

                                                    Zucchini Tomato Casserole with Crumb Topping

                                                    Serves: 1 servings · Prep: 15 min · Cook: 45 min

                                                    Zucchini Tomato Casserole with Bread Crumb Topping – exciting gourmet side dish recipe with layered zucchini, fresh tomatoes, shredded mozzarella and Parmesan with a great crunchy bread crumb topping.

                                                    Ingredients

                                                    • 1 ½ cups shredded mozzarella cheese
                                                    • ½ cup shredded Parmesan cheese
                                                    • 2 teaspoons garlic powder
                                                    • 1 teaspoon basil
                                                    • 1 teaspoon oregano
                                                    • salt and pepper
                                                    • 2 zucchini squash sliced thin
                                                    • 5 to matoes sliced, 4-5
                                                    • 2 tablespoons butter melted
                                                    • ¾ cup fine bread crumbs

                                                    Directions

                                                    1. Preheat oven to 375 and grease a 9×9 baking dish with nonstick cooking spray. Set aside.
                                                    2. In a small bowl, add cheese and spices. Mix well. Season with salt and pepper. Set aside.
                                                    3. Place zucchini in a single layer in the bottom of the prepared pan. Sprinkle with 1/4 of the cheese mixture. Top with tomatoes, in a single layer and 1/4 of the cheese mixture. Repeat layers.
                                                    4. Add melted butter and bread crumbs together and mix well. Sprinkle on top of baking dish.
                                                    5. Cover with foil and bake for 25 minutes.
                                                    6. Remove foil and bake for an additional 20 minutes or until vegetables are tender and top is crusty.
                                                    7. Let casserole stand 5 minutes before cutting into slices and serving.

                                                    Nutrition (per serving)

                                                    Serving Size: 1 · Calories: 177 · Fat: 9 · Saturated fat: 5 · Unsaturated fat: 3 · Carbs: 14 · Sugar: 4 · Fiber: 2 · Protein: 10 · Sodium: 371 · Cholesterol: 25

                                                    Recipe from: saltysidedish.com, saved in Lucy's cookbook.

                                                    Mexican Mama Goose’s Recipes Casserole

                                                    ♥ Tortilla Bake

                                                    Serves: 4 serves · Prep: 10 min · Cook: 15 min

                                                    This is a quick and easy meal to make and there are so many things you can add to it to make just the way you like it. I keep mine pretty simple and on the mild side, but you can give it as much kick as you want by adding spicy peppers and chilies and other seasonings.

                                                    Ingredients

                                                    • 1 lb ground beef
                                                    • 1 small onion
                                                    • 1 packet taco seasoning
                                                    • 4 medium corn or flour tortillas
                                                    • 2 cups Mexican Blend Cheese
                                                    • 1 enchilada sauce, mild or hot, 10 oz
                                                    • 4 oz sour cream for topping

                                                    Directions

                                                    1. Preheat oven to 350 degrees. Brown ground beef and onion over medium-high heat until beef is cooked through; drain.
                                                    2. Return the beef and onions to the skillet and add taco seasoning. Stir in the enchilada sauce. Leave on low heat to simmer for 10 minutes.
                                                    3. Spray a 9 x 9 baking dish with cooking spray. Lay the first tortilla in the dish and sprinkle meat mixture on top, taking it all the way to the edges. Then sprinkle a layer of cheese on top. Cover with another tortilla and repeat until you use all four tortillas. Sprinkle top layer with more cheese and bake at 350 degrees for 15 minutes until heated through. Serve with your choice of topping such as sour cream, salsa or guacamole.

                                                    Notes

                                                    Other optional ingredients you may want to add.... Green or red chilies Jalepeno peppers whole corn Refried beans salsa green or red peppers black olives guacamole Pepper Jack Cheese hot sauce

                                                    Recipe from: welcome-home-blog.net, saved in Lucy's cookbook.
